```py
        """How many times its size a call is drawn."""
        return self.transform().m11() * sh.frame.scale

    def _in_middle(self, sh):
        """The call is near the middle of the window and no open chart lies over it."""
        rect = sh.mapToScene(QtCore.QRectF(0, 0, sh.w, sh.h)).boundingRect()
        r = self.mapFromScene(rect).boundingRect()
        vp = self.viewport().rect()
        if not nest.middle((r.x(), r.y(), r.width(), r.height()), (vp.width(), vp.height())):
            return False
        mine = sh.frame.chain()                   # the charts it is inside lie around it, not over
        return not any(f.panel is not None and f.panel.isVisible() and f not in mine
                       and f.panel.sceneBoundingRect().intersects(rect) for f in self.frames)

    def _update_nest(self):
        """Open the calls zoomed into (making their charts first, one at a time), fade them in
        and out with the zoom, drop those zoomed out of."""
        if self.root is None or self.provider is None:
            return
        seen = self.mapToScene(self.viewport().rect()).boundingRect()
        if self.transform().m11() * min(f.scale for f in self.frames) < nest.CLOSE and len(self.frames) == 1:
            self._find_active()
            return                                # nothing open, nothing near opening
        changed = False
        for fr in list(self.frames):
            if fr not in self.frames or (fr.panel is not None and not fr.panel.isVisible()):
                continue
            for sh in fr.calls:
                r = self._reach(sh)
                if sh.panel is not None:
                    if r < nest.CLOSE:
                        self._close(sh)
                        changed = True
                        continue
                    op = nest.opening(r)
                    if abs(sh.panel.opacity() - op) > 1e-3:
                        sh.panel.setOpacity(op)
                    if sh.panel.isVisible() != (op > 0.01):
                        sh.panel.setVisible(op > 0.01)
                        changed = True
                elif r >= nest.PREFETCH and self._in_middle(sh):
                    entry = self.provider.cached(sh.ea)
                    if entry is not None:
                        self._open(sh, entry, r)
                        changed = True
                    elif sh.state is None:
                        sh.state = "queued"
                        sh.update()
                        self._queue.append(sh)
                        self._loader.start(0)
                elif sh.state == "queued" and r < nest.CLOSE:
                    sh.state = None
                    sh.update()
        self._limit(seen)
        if changed:
            self.content = self._extent()
            self._canvas()
        self._find_active()

    def _limit(self, seen):
        """At most MAX_OPEN charts open: those out of sight go first, then the least zoomed into."""
        open_ = [f.shape for f in self.frames if f.shape is not None and f.shape.panel is not None]
        if len(open_) <= nest.MAX_OPEN:
            return
        open_.sort(key=lambda sh: (sh.panel.sceneBoundingRect().intersects(seen), self._reach(sh)))
        for sh in open_[:len(open_) - nest.MAX_OPEN]:
            if sh.panel is not None:
                self._close(sh)

    def _load_next(self):
        """Make one queued chart (decompiling takes a moment); the next on the next turn."""
        while self._queue:
            sh = self._queue.pop(0)
            try:
                alive = sh.scene() is not None and sh.state == "queued"
            except RuntimeError:                  # dropped with the chart it was in
                alive = False
            if not alive:
                continue
            if self._reach(sh) < nest.PREFETCH:   # zoomed out again meanwhile
                sh.state = None
                sh.update()
                continue
            try:
                self.provider.load(sh.ea)
                sh.state = None
            except Exception:
                sh.state = "failed"
            sh.update()
            self._update_nest()
            break
        if self._queue:
            self._loader.start(0)

    def _open(self, sh, entry, r):
        """The called chart in a panel over its call, faded in as far as r says."""
        fr = sh.frame
        panel = _Panel(self, fr.holder)
        holder = _Holder(panel)
        child = _Frame(self, entry.chart, holder, depth=fr.depth + 1, eas=fr.eas + (sh.ea,),
                       entry=entry, shape=sh)
        x, y, w, h, s = nest.panel((sh.pos().x(), sh.pos().y(), sh.w, sh.h), child.size)
        panel.prepareGeometryChange()
        panel.setPos(x, y)
        panel.w, panel.h = w, h
        holder.setPos(nest.PAD, nest.PAD)
        holder.setScale(s)
        child.scale = fr.scale * s
        child.panel, panel.frame, sh.panel = panel, child, panel
        op = nest.opening(r)
        panel.setOpacity(op)
        panel.setVisible(op > 0.01)
        self.frames.append(child)
        self._light_inside(child)

    def _close(self, sh):
        """Drop a call's open chart and every chart opened inside it."""
        panel = sh.panel
        if panel is None:
            return
        gone = {f for f in self.frames if any(g.shape is sh for g in f.chain())}
        self.frames = [f for f in self.frames if f not in gone]
        for f in gone:
            for c in f.calls:
                c.panel = None
        sh.panel = None
        if self.active in gone:
            self.active = None
        self.scene().removeItem(panel)
        sh.update()

    def _light_inside(self, child):
        """What a called chart lights: the arguments the light outside is handed over in (the
        followed value, one computed from it, a pointer to its memory), else everything."""
        at = child.shape.frame.args.get(child.shape.line.id, ())
        if at:
            lit, chosen, around, args = self.provider.args(child.entry, at)
            child.set_var(lit, chosen, "", around, args)
        else:
            child.all_lit()

    def _find_active(self):
        """The chart being looked at: the deepest fully open one under the window's middle."""
        mid = self.mapToScene(self.viewport().rect().center())
        best = self.root
        for f in self.frames:
            if (f.panel is not None and f.panel.isVisible() and f.panel.opacity() > 0.99
                    and f.panel.sceneBoundingRect().contains(mid) and f.depth > best.depth):
                best = f
        best = None if best is self.root else best
        if best is not self.active:
            self.active = best
            self._legend()

    def scrollContentsBy(self, dx, dy):
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.scrollContentsBy(self, dx, dy)
        self._nest_later.start(40)

    # ---- light ----
    def show_way(self, box, frame=None):
        """The way to shape `box` in a chart (the main one by default); None: back."""
        frame = frame or self.root
        if frame is None:
            return
        msg = frame.show_way(box)
        if msg is not None:
            self.say(msg)
        self._legend()
        self.viewport().update()

    def focus(self, lit, chosen="", message="", around=None, args=None, animate=True):
        """Full colour for the lit lines (and the shapes and lines that join them), the name
        of the followed variable marked and the names around it (from / into), the rest
        faded; the open charts of calls follow the arguments the light is handed over in."""
        root = self.root
        self._peek_cache = {}
        root.set_var(lit, (chosen,) if chosen else (), message, around, args)
        for f in self.frames[1:]:
            self._light_inside(f)
        self.say(message)
        self._legend()
        self.target = None
        first = None
        for line in root.chart.lines:
            if line.id in lit and line.box in root.shapes:
                first = first or line
                if chosen and re.search(r"(?<![\w])%s(?![\w])" % re.escape(chosen), line.text):
                    self.target = line
                    break
        self.target = self.target or first
        self.home(animate)
        self.viewport().update()

    def line_point(self, line, frame=None):
        """The middle of a line's text, in the drawing."""
        it = (frame or self.root).shapes[line.box]
        rows = [r for r in it.rows if r[0] == line.id]
        _, text, x, y = rows[0]
        return it.mapToScene(QtCore.QPointF(x + self.fm.horizontalAdvance(text) / 2,
                                            y - self.fm.ascent() / 2))

    def home(self, animate=False):
        """The followed variable's first line in the middle of the window; with none, a new
        drawing shows its top and a moved one stays where it is."""
        if self.target is not None:
            p = self.line_point(self.target)
            top = self.content.top() + self.viewport().height() / self.transform().m11() / 2
            if not animate and p.y() < top:       # near the start: show the start too
                p.setY(top)
            self.center_on(p, animate)
        elif not animate:
            h = self.viewport().height() / self.transform().m11()
            self.centerOn(self.content.center().x(),
                          self.content.top() + min(h, self.content.height()) / 2)

    def center_on(self, dest, animate=True):
        if not animate or not self.isVisible():
            self.centerOn(dest)
            return
        start = self.mapToScene(self.viewport().rect().center())
        a = QtCore.QVariantAnimation(self)
        a.setStartValue(QtCore.QPointF(start))
        a.setEndValue(QtCore.QPointF(dest))
        a.setDuration(220)
        a.setEasingCurve(QtCore.QEasingCurve.OutCubic)
        a.valueChanged.connect(lambda v: self.centerOn(v))
        a.start()
        self._anim = a

    def resizeEvent(self, ev):
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.resizeEvent(self, ev)
        self._canvas()
        self._nest_later.start(40)

    def drawBackground(self, p, rect):
        """Faint dots that move with the drawing, so a drag over empty space can be seen."""
        p.fillRect(rect, self.th["bg"])
        z = self.transform().m11()
        step = float(GRID)
        while step * z < 16:
            step *= 2
        while step * z > 64:
            step /= 2
        x0, y0 = math.floor(rect.left() / step) * step, math.floor(rect.top() / step) * step
        nx, ny = int((rect.right() - x0) / step) + 1, int((rect.bottom() - y0) / step) + 1
        if nx * ny > 40000:
            return
        pen = QtGui.QPen(self.th["grid"], 2)
        pen.setCosmetic(True)
        pen.setCapStyle(QtCore.Qt.RoundCap)
        p.setPen(pen)
        p.drawPoints(QtGui.QPolygonF([QtCore.QPointF(x0 + i * step, y0 + j * step)
                                      for i in range(nx) for j in range(ny)]))

    # ---- input ----
    # ---- a preview of the chart a call opens ----
    def _peek_at(self, vpos):
        """The call under the mouse that would open, if it is not open yet."""
        frame, sh = self._under(vpos)
        if sh is None or sh.ea is None or sh.state == "failed":
            return None
        if sh.panel is not None and sh.panel.isVisible() and sh.panel.opacity() > 0.5:
            return None
        return sh

    def _hide_peek(self):
        self._peek_timer.stop()
        self._peek_on = None
        self.peek.hide()

    def _peek_image(self, sh):
        """The called chart drawn small, lit as it would be when opened. Made on a scene of its
        own, then dropped; kept per call until the light or the drawing changes."""
        key = (id(sh.frame), sh.box.id)
        if key in self._peek_cache:
            return self._peek_cache[key]
        try:
            entry = self.provider.load(sh.ea)
        except Exception:
            sh.state = "failed"
            sh.update()
            return None
        scene = QtWidgets.QGraphicsScene()
        holder = _Holder()
        scene.addItem(holder)
        fr = _Frame(self, entry.chart, holder, depth=nest.MAX_DEPTH, eas=sh.frame.eas + (sh.ea,),
                    entry=entry, shape=sh)
        self._light_inside(fr)
        W, H = fr.size
        k = min(460.0 / max(W, 1), 340.0 / max(H, 1), 0.7)
        dpr = self.devicePixelRatioF()
        pix = QtGui.QPixmap(int(W * k * dpr) + 1, int(H * k * dpr) + 1)
        pix.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr)
        pix.fill(self.th["fill"])
        p = QtGui.QPainter(pix)
        p.setRenderHints(QtGui.QPainter.Antialiasing | QtGui.QPainter.TextAntialiasing)
        scene.render(p, QtCore.QRectF(0, 0, W * k, H * k), QtCore.QRectF(0, 0, W, H))
        p.end()
        scene.clear()
        self._peek_cache[key] = (fr.name, pix)
        return self._peek_cache[key]

    def _show_peek(self):
        sh = self._peek_on
        if sh is None:
            return
        try:
            got = self._peek_image(sh)
        except RuntimeError:                      # the call was dropped meanwhile
            got = None
        if got is None or self._peek_on is not sh:
            return
        name, pix = got
        self.peek_title.setText("%s  ·  %s" % (name, T("zoom in to open", "滚轮放大展开")))
        self.peek_img.setPixmap(pix)
        self.peek.adjustSize()
        self._place_peek(self._peek_pos)
        self.peek.show()
        self.peek.raise_()

    def _place_peek(self, pos):
        """Beside the mouse, inside the window."""
        w, h = self.peek.width(), self.peek.height()
        x, y = pos.x() + 18, pos.y() + 20
        if x + w > self.width() - 6:
            x = pos.x() - 18 - w
        if y + h > self.height() - 6:
            y = max(6, pos.y() - 20 - h)
        self.peek.move(max(6, x), y)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, ev):
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.mouseMoveEvent(self, ev)
        if ev.buttons() or self.provider is None or self.root is None:
            self._hide_peek()
            return
        sh = self._peek_at(ev.pos())
        self._peek_pos = self.viewport().mapTo(self, ev.pos())
        if sh is not self._peek_on:
            self._hide_peek()
            if sh is not None:
                self._peek_on = sh
                self._peek_timer.start(350)
        elif sh is not None and self.peek.isVisible():
            self._place_peek(self._peek_pos)

    def leaveEvent(self, ev):
        self._hide_peek()
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.leaveEvent(self, ev)

    def wheelEvent(self, ev):
        """The wheel zooms about the mouse, by how far it turned (a fine-notched wheel or a
        touchpad zooms smoothly); Shift+wheel, or a sideways swipe, goes sideways."""
        self._hide_peek()
        d = ev.angleDelta()
        if ev.modifiers() & QtCore.Qt.ShiftModifier or (d.x() and not d.y()):
            bar = self.horizontalScrollBar()
            step = QtWidgets.QApplication.wheelScrollLines() * bar.singleStep()
            bar.setValue(bar.value() - int((d.y() or d.x()) / 120 * step))
        elif d.y():
            at = ev.position() if hasattr(ev, "position") else ev.pos()
            self._zoom(STEP ** (d.y() / 120), at)
        ev.accept()

    def _under(self, vpos):
        """(frame, shape or None) under a viewport position: the topmost chart there."""
        for it in self.items(vpos):
            if isinstance(it, _Shape):
                return it.frame, it
            if isinstance(it, _Panel) and it.frame is not None:
                return it.frame, None
        return self.root, None

    def _shape_at(self, vpos):
        return self._under(vpos)[1]

    def mousePressEvent(self, ev):
        self._hide_peek()
        self._press = ev.pos()
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.mousePressEvent(self, ev)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, ev):
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.mouseReleaseEvent(self, ev)
        if (ev.button() == QtCore.Qt.LeftButton and self._press is not None
                and (ev.pos() - self._press).manhattanLength() < 5):       # a click, not a drag
            self._click_at = ev.pos()
            self._click.start(QtWidgets.QApplication.doubleClickInterval())
        self._press = None

    def _clicked(self):
        if self.root is None or self._click_at is None:
            return
        frame, it = self._under(self._click_at)
        self.show_way(it.box.id if it is not None else None, frame)

    def hit(self, vpos):
        """(line, identifier or None) under a viewport position, or (None, None)."""
        frame, line, word = self._hit(vpos)
        return line, word

    def _hit(self, vpos):
        frame, it = self._under(vpos)
        if it is not None:
            lid, word = it.token_at(it.mapFromScene(self.mapToScene(vpos)))
            if lid is not None:
                return frame, frame.chart.lines[lid], word
        return frame, None, None

    def mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, ev):
        self._click.stop()
        frame, line, word = self._hit(ev.pos())
        if word and frame is not None and word in frame.chart.refs:
            kind, x = frame.chart.refs[word]
            if kind == "func":
                self.on_open("func", x)
            elif frame is self.root:
                self.on_open("follow", (kind, x))
            else:                                 # inside a called chart: follow it there
                lit, chosen, around, args, msg = self.provider.follow(frame.entry, (kind, x))
                frame.set_var(lit, chosen, msg, around, args)
                for f in self.frames:
                    if f is not frame and frame in f.chain():
                        self._light_inside(f)
                self.say(msg)
                self._legend()
            ev.accept()
            return
        it = self._shape_at(ev.pos())
        if it is not None:
            self.show_way(it.box.id, it.frame)
            ev.accept()
            return
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, ev)

    def keyPressEvent(self, ev):
        k, mods = ev.key(), ev.modifiers()
        ctrl, alt = bool(mods & QtCore.Qt.ControlModifier), bool(mods & QtCore.Qt.AltModifier)
        if k in (QtCore.Qt.Key_Plus, QtCore.Qt.Key_Equal, QtCore.Qt.Key_Minus) and not alt:
            self._zoom(STEP if k != QtCore.Qt.Key_Minus else 1 / STEP)
            return
        if k == QtCore.Qt.Key_0 and ctrl:
            self.resetTransform()
            self._canvas()
            self._update_nest()
            return
        if k == QtCore.Qt.Key_Escape:
            ways = [f for f in self.frames if f.goal is not None]
            if ways:
                for f in sorted(ways, key=lambda f: f is not self.active):
                    self.show_way(None, f)
                    break
                return
        vk = {QtCore.Qt.Key_Space: 0x20, QtCore.Qt.Key_Escape: 0x1B, QtCore.Qt.Key_Return: 0x0D,
              QtCore.Qt.Key_Enter: 0x0D, QtCore.Qt.Key_Left: 0x25, QtCore.Qt.Key_Right: 0x27}.get(k)
        if vk == 0x0D and not ctrl:
            line, _ = self.hit(self.mapFromGlobal(QtGui.QCursor.pos()))
            line = line or self.target
            if line is not None:
                self.on_open("jump", line)
            return
        if vk is not None and self.on_key(vk, ctrl, alt):
            ev.accept()
            return
        QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.keyPressEvent(self, ev)

    def event(self, ev):
        if ev.type() == QtCore.QEvent.ShortcutOverride:
            k, mods = ev.key(), ev.modifiers()
            if k in (QtCore.Qt.Key_Escape, QtCore.Qt.Key_Space, QtCore.Qt.Key_Return,
                     QtCore.Qt.Key_Enter) or (mods & QtCore.Qt.AltModifier and
                                              k in (QtCore.Qt.Key_Left, QtCore.Qt.Key_Right)):
                ev.accept()
                return True
        return QtWidgets.QGraphicsView.event(self, ev)


class GraphForm(ida_kernwin.PluginForm):
    """The dockable IDA window around a GraphView."""

    def __init__(self, on_key, on_open, on_close, provider=None):
        ida_kernwin.PluginForm.__init__(self)
        self._args = (on_key, on_open, provider)
        self._on_close = on_close
        self.view = None
        self.shown = None           # the chart on screen (identity), so a refocus is cheap

    def OnCreate(self, form):
        from .qt import qwidget
        w = qwidget(form)
        lay = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout()
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.view = GraphView(w, *self._args)
        lay.addWidget(self.view)
        w.setLayout(lay)
        w.setFocusProxy(self.view)          # keys go to the drawing, not to IDA's frame

    def OnClose(self, form):
        self._on_close(self)

    def Close(self, options=0):
        return ida_kernwin.PluginForm.Close(self, options)

    def show_graph(self, chart, lit, chosen="", message="", around=None, args=None, take_focus=True):
        fresh = self.shown is not chart
        if fresh:
            self.view.build(chart)
            self.shown = chart
        self.view.focus(lit, chosen, message, around, args, animate=not fresh)
        if fresh:
            QtCore.QTimer.singleShot(60, lambda: self._recenter())
        if take_focus:
            self.view.setFocus()

    def _recenter(self):
        try:
            self.view.home()
            self.view._update_nest()
        except Exception:
            pass

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/flowview.py`:

```py
"""Variable data flow windows: the chosen variable's lines of pseudocode and nothing else.

Ctrl-Alt-F (or right-click > Data flow of this variable) in a pseudocode view on a local
variable or a global opens a window with only the lines its value comes from (▲), the lines
that read or write it (●) and the lines its value goes to (▼), inside the if/loop headers
that enclose them. The lines are Hex-Rays' own, colours and notes included.
Space switches to a flowchart in the same place (flowgraph.py): the whole function the way
textbooks draw a program, the chosen variable's lines in full and its name marked, the rest
faded; Space switches back. Zooming into a call of the program's own function opens that
function's chart inside it (_Callees makes it then, not before).
In both: double-click a variable to follow it; Back / Forward (Esc, Ctrl-Enter, Alt-Left /
Alt-Right, the mouse side buttons) move between the variables followed; Enter jumps to the
line in the pseudocode. A variable double-clicked in the pseudocode itself is followed too
while a window is open. Nothing is written to the database."""
import collections
import time

import ida_hexrays as hr
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines
import ida_name

from ...analysis.dataflow import analyze, var_flow
from ...analysis.flowchart import around, build_chart, lit, merge_marks
from ...core.i18n import T
from .fntree import function_tree, var_info
from .live import _line_item_indices

ACTION = "ibr:varflow"
HOTKEY = "Ctrl-Alt-F"
_MARK = {"src": ("▲ ", ida_lines.SCOLOR_NUMBER), "self": ("● ", ida_lines.SCOLOR_KEYWORD),
         "dst": ("▼ ", ida_lines.SCOLOR_IMPNAME)}
_SITE_OF = {"stmt": "body", "return": "body", "cond": "head", "init": "head", "step": "head",
            "switch": "head"}


_CACHE = {}       # the last function's tree, analysis and flowchart, keyed by its printed text

def _items(t, out=None):
    out = set() if out is None else out
    stack = [t]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.index is not None:
            out.add(n.index)
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z, n.init, n.step))
        stack.extend(n.args)
    return out


class FlowText:
    """The window's content for one variable: [(colored line, ea or None)], title, names."""

    def __init__(self, cfunc, var=None, obj_ea=None):
        self.func_ea = cfunc.entry_ea
        sv = cfunc.get_pseudocode()           # numbers the items; the tree must be read after it
        self.src = [sv[i].line for i in range(sv.size())]
        key = (self.func_ea, hash(tuple(self.src)))
        if _CACHE.get("key") != key:          # following another variable of the same function
            body, names = function_tree(cfunc)
            _CACHE.clear()
            _CACHE.update(key=key, data=(body, names, analyze(body), var_info(cfunc)),
                          title=_title(cfunc))
        body, self.names, an, self.info = _CACHE["data"]
        self.body, self.an = body, an
        self.var, self.obj_ea = var, obj_ea
        self.name = self.names.get(var, "?") if var is not None else (
            ida_lines.tag_remove(ida_name.get_name(obj_ea) or "") or "%X" % obj_ea)
        flow = var_flow(body, var=var, obj_ea=obj_ea, an=an)
        self.flow = flow
        self.at = {}
        for n, line in enumerate(self.src):
            for idx in _line_item_indices(cfunc, line):
                self.at.setdefault(idx, []).append(n)
        # statements and headers to show, with their role
        self.role = {}
        self.shown = set()
        for s in flow.sites:
            if s.role == "join" or s.stmt is None:
                continue
            self.role[(id(s.stmt), _SITE_OF[s.role])] = (flow.role[s.id], s.may)
            for c in s.ctx:
                self.shown.add(id(c))
            if _SITE_OF[s.role] == "head":
                self.shown.add(id(s.stmt))
        self.lines, self._done = [], set()
        self._header()
        self._walk(body)

    # ---- the flowchart ----
    def chart_data(self):
        """(chart, lit line ids, the name to mark, message, {line id: {name: "from" | "into" |
        "both"}} for the names around it, {line id: argument positions} of the calls it is
        handed to).
        One chart per function, made once: following another variable only changes what is
        lit."""
        chart = _CACHE.get("chart")
        if chart is None:
            chart = _CACHE["chart"] = build_chart(self.body, self.an, self.info, _CACHE.get("title", ""))
            chart.ea = self.func_ea
        name = self.name
        if self.obj_ea is not None:               # as the chart writes it (_fread_chk, not __fread_chk)
            name = next((k for k, v in chart.refs.items() if v[0] != "var" and v[1] == self.obj_ea), name)
        flow = var_flow(self.body, var=self.var, obj_ea=self.obj_ea, an=self.an, may=True)
        on = lit(chart, self.an, flow, self.var)
        names, args = around(chart, self.an, flow, self.var) if on else ({}, {})
        msg = ""
        if not on:                                # a function, or nothing flows through it
            on = chart.mentions(name)
            msg = (T("%s carries no data here: only where it appears is marked." % name,
                     "%s 在这里不传递数据，只标出它出现的地方。" % name) if on else
                   T("%s is not used in this function." % name, "%s 在这个函数里没有用到。" % name))
        return chart, on, name, msg, names, args

    # ---- output ----
    def _add(self, text, ea=None):
        self.lines.append((text, ea))

    def _comment(self, text):
        self._add(ida_lines.COLSTR("// " + text, ida_lines.SCOLOR_AUTOCMT))

    def _header(self):
        f = self.flow
        count = {"src": 0, "self": 0, "dst": 0}
        for s in f.sites:
            if s.role != "join":
                count[f.role[s.id]] += 1
        fname = ida_lines.tag_remove(ida_name.get_name(self.func_ea) or "") or "%X" % self.func_ea
        self._comment(T("Data flow of %s in %s: ▲ from %d, ● itself %d, ▼ to %d."
                        " Everything else is hidden." % (self.name, fname, count["src"],
                                                        count["self"], count["dst"]),
                        "%s 在 %s 中的数据流：▲ 来源 %d 处，● 自身 %d 处，▼ 去向 %d 处。"
                        "其余代码已隐藏。" % (self.name, fname, count["src"], count["self"],
                                             count["dst"])))
        if f.may:
            self._comment(T("? = a call to an unknown function that may change the memory passed to it.",
                            "? = 调用了未知函数，可能改写传给它的内存。"))
        if f.cut:
            self._comment(T("Too long: only the lines closest to %s are shown." % self.name,
                            "太长：只显示离 %s 最近的部分。" % self.name))
        self._comment(T("Double-click a variable to follow it; Back / Forward (Esc, Ctrl-Enter, mouse"
                        " side buttons) step through them; Enter jumps to the line; Space: flowchart.",
                        "双击变量跟踪它；后退/前进（Esc、Ctrl+Enter、鼠标侧键）在跟踪过的变量间切换；"
                        "回车跳到原伪代码的这一行；空格切换流程图。"))
        self._add("")

    def _src_lines(self, nums, mark, ea):
        first = True
        for n in nums:
            if n in self._done:
                continue
            self._done.add(n)
            gutter = "    "                        # continuation lines and plain headers
            if first and mark is not None:
                (sym, col), may = _MARK[mark[0]], mark[1]
                may = ida_lines.COLSTR("? ", ida_lines.SCOLOR_AUTOCMT) if may else "  "
                gutter = may + ida_lines.COLSTR(sym, col)
            self._add(gutter + self.src[n], ea)
            first = False

    def _lines_of(self, *trees):
        out = set()
        for t in trees:
            for idx in _items(t):
                out.update(self.at.get(idx, ()))
        return sorted(out)

    def _indent(self, nums):
        if not nums:
            return ""
        raw = ida_lines.tag_remove(self.src[nums[0]])
        return raw[:len(raw) - len(raw.lstrip(" "))]

    def _synth(self, indent, text):
        self._add("    " + indent + ida_lines.COLSTR(text, ida_lines.SCOLOR_SYMBOL))

    def _walk(self, st):
        if st is None:
            return
        k = st.kind
        if k == "block":
            for c in st.body:
                self._walk(c)
            return
        if k in ("expr", "return"):
            mark = self.role.get((id(st), "body"))
            if mark:
                self._src_lines(self._lines_of(st.x) or self._lines_of(st), mark, st.ea)
            return
        if id(st) not in self.shown:
            return
        head = self.role.get((id(st), "head"))
        if k in ("if", "while", "switch"):
            nums = self._lines_of(st.x)
            ind = self._indent(nums)
            self._src_lines(nums, head, st.ea)
            if k == "switch":
                for case in st.body:
                    self._block(case, ind)
            else:
                self._block(st.body[0] if st.body else None, ind)
            if k == "if" and len(st.body) > 1 and self._has(st.body[1]):
                self._synth(ind, "else")
                self._block(st.body[1], ind)
        elif k == "for":
            nums = self._lines_of(st.init, st.x, st.step)
            ind = self._indent(nums)
            self._src_lines(nums, head, st.ea)
            self._block(st.body[0] if st.body else None, ind)
        elif k == "do":
            nums = self._lines_of(st.x)
            ind = self._indent(nums)
            body = st.body[0] if st.body else None
            if self._has(body):
                self._synth(ind, "do")
                self._block(body, ind)
            self._src_lines(nums, head, st.ea)

    def _has(self, st):
        """Something under st is shown."""
        stack = [st]
        while stack:
            n = stack.pop()
            if n is None:
                continue
            if id(n) in self.shown or (id(n), "body") in self.role:
                return True
            stack.extend(n.body)
        return False

    def _block(self, st, ind):
        """A shown statement's body, braced; nothing when none of it is shown, except
        a lone break / continue / goto, which says what the condition does."""
        if not self._has(st):
            jumps = _jumps(st)
            if jumps:
                self._src_lines(sorted(n for j in jumps for n in self.at.get(j.index, ())), None, None)
            return
        self._synth(ind, "{")
        self._walk(st)
        self._synth(ind, "}")


def _title(cfunc):
    """The start of the chart: the function and its arguments' names."""
    name = ida_lines.tag_remove(ida_name.get_name(cfunc.entry_ea) or "") or "%X" % cfunc.entry_ea
    try:
        args = [cfunc.lvars[i].name for i in cfunc.argidx]
    except Exception:
        args = []
    return "%s(%s)" % (name, ", ".join(args))


class _Callee:
    """A called function's chart and what lighting it needs."""

    def __init__(self, ea, chart, body, an, names, argidx):
        self.ea, self.chart, self.body, self.an = ea, chart, body, an
        self.names, self.argidx = names, argidx


class _Callees:
    """The charts of the functions the flowchart opens inside their calls: made when a call
    is zoomed into (the view asks for one at a time), kept for the last LIMIT functions and
    forgotten when the window shows another function."""
    LIMIT = 30

    def __init__(self):
        self.cache = collections.OrderedDict()

    def clear(self):
        self.cache.clear()

    def cached(self, ea):
        e = self.cache.get(ea)
        if e is not None:
            self.cache.move_to_end(ea)
        return e

    def load(self, ea):
        e = self.cached(ea)
        if e is not None:
            return e
        cf = hr.decompile(ea)
        if not cf:
            raise RuntimeError("cannot decompile %X" % ea)
        cf.get_pseudocode()                       # numbers the items; the tree is read after it
        body, names = function_tree(cf)
        an = analyze(body)
        chart = build_chart(body, an, var_info(cf), _title(cf))
        chart.ea = ea
        e = self.cache[ea] = _Callee(ea, chart, body, an, names, list(cf.argidx))
        while len(self.cache) > self.LIMIT:
            self.cache.popitem(last=False)
        return e

    def _one(self, e, var=None, obj_ea=None):
        flow = var_flow(e.body, var=var, obj_ea=obj_ea, an=e.an, may=True)
        on = lit(e.chart, e.an, flow, var)
        return (on,) + (around(e.chart, e.an, flow, var) if on else ({}, {}))

    def args(self, e, positions):
        """(lit line ids, names, {line id: {name: from / into / both}}, {line id: argument
        positions}) for the function's arguments at positions."""
        on, chosen, names, args = set(), [], {}, {}
        for i in positions:
            if i < len(e.argidx):
                n = e.argidx[i]
                a, m, g = self._one(e, var=n)
                on |= a
                names = merge_marks(names, m)
                for k, v in g.items():
                    args[k] = tuple(sorted(set(args.get(k, ())) | set(v)))
                chosen.append(e.names.get(n, "?"))
        return on, tuple(chosen), names, args

    def follow(self, e, ref):
        """A name double-clicked inside the chart: (lit, names, around, args, message)."""
        kind, x = ref
        if kind == "var":
            name = e.names.get(x, "?")
            on, names, args = self._one(e, var=x)
        else:
            name = next((k for k, v in e.chart.refs.items() if v == ref), "%X" % x)
            on, names, args = self._one(e, obj_ea=x)
        msg = ""
        if not on:
            on = e.chart.mentions(name)
            msg = (T("%s carries no data here: only where it appears is marked." % name,
                     "%s 在这里不传递数据，只标出它出现的地方。" % name) if on else
                   T("%s is not used in this function." % name, "%s 在这个函数里没有用到。" % name))
        return on, (name,), names, args, msg


_CALLEES = _Callees()


def _jumps(st):
    """The statements of st when all of them are break / continue / goto, else []."""
    if st is None:
        return []
    if st.kind in ("break", "continue", "goto"):
        return [st]
    if st.kind == "block" and st.body and all(c.kind in ("break", "continue", "goto") for c in st.body):
        return list(st.body)
    return []


class _Nav:
    """What both windows show, and the way back and forward through followed variables."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.cur = None         # FlowText on screen
        self.history = []       # (var, obj_ea) followed before it: Back goes there
        self.ahead = []         # and Forward comes back
        self.mode = "text"      # "text" or "graph"; Space switches
        self.last = 0.0         # one side-button click must not step twice
        self.last_toggle = 0.0


NAV = _Nav()
_TEXT = None                    # FlowViewer, when open
_GRAPH = None                   # flowgraph.GraphForm, when open
_CLOSING = [False]              # a window closed by us, not by the user


def _text_title():
    return T("Data flow", "数据流")


def _graph_title():
    return T("Flowchart", "流程图")


def _ours(w):
    """w is one of the data-flow windows."""
    if w is None:
        return False
    title = ida_kernwin.get_widget_title(w)
    return title in (_text_title(), _graph_title())


# ---- navigation, shared by both windows ----
def _refresh(var, obj_ea, activate=True):
    cf = hr.decompile(NAV.cur.func_ea)
    if cf:
        NAV.cur = FlowText(cf, var=var, obj_ea=obj_ea)
        _render(activate)


def follow(var=None, obj_ea=None, activate=True):
    """Show another variable of the same function; Back returns to this one."""
    if NAV.cur is None or (var == NAV.cur.var and obj_ea == NAV.cur.obj_ea):
        return
    NAV.history.append((NAV.cur.var, NAV.cur.obj_ea))
    NAV.ahead = []
    _refresh(var, obj_ea, activate)


def _sync(vu):
    """A local variable double-clicked in the pseudocode (IDA does nothing with that): an open
    data-flow window follows it and the pseudocode keeps the focus. Functions and globals,
    which IDA goes to on a double-click, are left alone."""
    if NAV.cur is None or (_TEXT is None and _GRAPH is None):
        return
    var, obj_ea = _target(vu)
    if var is None or obj_ea is not None:
        return
    if vu.cfunc.entry_ea != NAV.cur.func_ea:      # another function: a new start
        NAV.history, NAV.ahead = [], []
        NAV.cur = FlowText(vu.cfunc, var=var)
        _render(activate=False)
    else:
        follow(var=var, activate=False)


def _step(src, dst):
    now = time.time()
    if not src or NAV.cur is None or now - NAV.last < 0.15:
        return
    NAV.last = now
    dst.append((NAV.cur.var, NAV.cur.obj_ea))
    _refresh(*src.pop())


def back():
    """IDA's Back (Esc, Alt-Left, the mouse back button): the previous variable."""
    _step(NAV.history, NAV.ahead)


def forward():
    """IDA's Forward (Ctrl-Enter, Alt-Right, the mouse forward button)."""
    _step(NAV.ahead, NAV.history)


def toggle():
    """Space: text <-> graph, in the same place."""
    now = time.time()
    if now - NAV.last_toggle < 0.2:
        return
    NAV.last_toggle = now
    NAV.mode = "graph" if NAV.mode == "text" else "text"
    _render()


def jump(ea):
    """Show ea in the pseudocode (IDA's own history records it, so Esc there returns)."""
    if ea is not None:
        hr.open_pseudocode(ea, hr.OPF_REUSE)
        ida_kernwin.jumpto(ea)


def _keys(key, ctrl, alt):
    """The keys both windows share. key: a Windows virtual key code."""
    if key == 0x20 and not (ctrl or alt):
        toggle()
    elif key == 0x1B:
        back()
    elif key == 0x0D and ctrl:
        forward()
    elif key == 0x25 and alt:
        back()
    elif key == 0x27 and alt:
        forward()
    else:
        return False
    return True


# ---- the mouse side buttons ----
def _nav_filter():
    from .qt import QtCore
    E, Qt = QtCore.QEvent, QtCore.Qt

    class F(QtCore.QObject):
        def eventFilter(self, obj, ev):
            try:
                t = ev.type()
                if t in (E.MouseButtonPress, E.MouseButtonRelease, E.MouseButtonDblClick):
                    b = ev.button()
                    if b in (Qt.BackButton, Qt.ForwardButton):
                        if t == E.MouseButtonPress:
                            (back if b == Qt.BackButton else forward)()
                        return True
                elif t == E.KeyPress and ev.key() in (Qt.Key_Back, Qt.Key_Forward):
                    (back if ev.key() == Qt.Key_Back else forward)()      # WM_APPCOMMAND
                    return True
            except Exception:
                pass
            return False
    return F()


def _watch(view):
    """Side buttons over a window and every widget inside it."""
    try:
        from .qt import QtWidgets, qwidget
        w = qwidget(view.GetWidget())
        if w is None:
            return
        view._filter = getattr(view, "_filter", None) or _nav_filter()
        for x in [w] + w.findChildren(QtWidgets.QWidget):
            x.installEventFilter(view._filter)
    except Exception:
        pass


# ---- showing ----
def _render(activate=True):
    if NAV.cur is None:
        return
    if NAV.mode == "graph":
        _show(_graph_view, _TEXT, activate)
    else:
        _show(_text_view, _GRAPH, activate)


def _show(make, other, activate=True):
    """Fill the window of the current mode; it takes the other one's place."""
    global _TEXT, _GRAPH
    view, fresh = make(activate)
    w = view.GetWidget()
    if fresh:
        if other is not None and other.GetWidget():
            ida_kernwin.set_dock_pos(ida_kernwin.get_widget_title(w),
                                     ida_kernwin.get_widget_title(other.GetWidget()), ida_kernwin.DP_TAB)
        else:
            ida_kernwin.set_dock_pos(ida_kernwin.get_widget_title(w), "Pseudocode-A", ida_kernwin.DP_RIGHT)
        _watch(view)
    if other is not None:
        _CLOSING[0] = True
        try:
            other.Close()
        finally:
            _CLOSING[0] = False
        if other is _TEXT:
            _TEXT = None
        if other is _GRAPH:
            _GRAPH = None
    if activate or fresh:
        ida_kernwin.activate_widget(view.GetWidget(), True)
    if view is _TEXT:
        # the ordinary highlight, as if the name had been clicked: the next click replaces it
        ida_kernwin.set_highlight(view.GetWidget(), NAV.cur.name, ida_kernwin.HIF_IDENTIFIER)


def _text_view(activate=True):
    global _TEXT
    fresh = _TEXT is None or not _TEXT.GetWidget()
    if fresh:
        _TEXT = FlowViewer()
        _TEXT.Create(_text_title())
    _TEXT.ClearLines()
    for line, _ in NAV.cur.lines:
        _TEXT.AddLine(line)
    _TEXT.Refresh()
    if fresh or activate:                         # Show() also brings it to the front
        _TEXT.Show()
    return _TEXT, fresh


def _graph_view(activate=True):
    global _GRAPH
    from .flowgraph import GraphForm
    fresh = _GRAPH is None or not _GRAPH.GetWidget()
    if fresh:
        _GRAPH = GraphForm(_keys, _open, _graph_closed, _CALLEES)
        _GRAPH.Show(_graph_title())
    _GRAPH.show_graph(*NAV.cur.chart_data(), take_focus=activate or fresh)
    return _GRAPH, fresh


def _graph_closed(form):
    global _GRAPH
    if not _CLOSING[0] and form is _GRAPH:
        _GRAPH = None


def _open(what, value):
    """From the flowchart: ("follow", ("var", n) or ("obj", ea)) after a double-click on a
    name, ("func", ea) after one on a called function, ("jump", chart line) after Enter."""
    if NAV.cur is None:
        return
    if what == "follow":
        kind, x = value
        if kind == "var":
            follow(var=x)
        else:
            follow(obj_ea=x)
    elif what == "func":
        jump(value)
    else:
        jump(value.ea)


class FlowViewer(ida_kernwin.simplecustviewer_t):
    """The text window: the variable's lines of pseudocode inside their if/loop headers."""

    def _word(self):
        """The identifier under the cursor."""
        word = self.GetCurrentWord()
        if word:
            return word
        pos, line = self.GetPos(), self.GetCurrentLine(notags=1)
        if not pos or not line:
            return None
        x = pos[1]
        ok = lambda c: c.isalnum() or c == "_"
        if x >= len(line) or not ok(line[x]):
            return None
        a = x
        while a > 0 and ok(line[a - 1]):
            a -= 1
        b = x
        while b < len(line) and ok(line[b]):
            b += 1
        return line[a:b]

    def OnDblClick(self, shift):
        word = self._word()
        if word and NAV.cur is not None:
            for n, name in NAV.cur.names.items():
                if name == word:
                    follow(var=n)
                    return True
        return self._jump()

    def _jump(self):
        n = self.GetLineNo()
        if NAV.cur is not None and n is not None and 0 <= n < len(NAV.cur.lines):
            jump(NAV.cur.lines[n][1])
        return True

    def OnKeydown(self, vkey, shift):
        ctrl, alt = bool(shift & ida_kernwin.VES_CTRL), bool(shift & ida_kernwin.VES_ALT)
        if vkey == 0x0D and not ctrl:
            return self._jump()
        return _keys(vkey, ctrl, alt)

    def OnClose(self):
        global _TEXT
        if not _CLOSING[0]:
            _TEXT = None


def open_flow(cfunc, var=None, obj_ea=None):
    """A new start (from the pseudocode): forget the variables followed before."""
    NAV.history, NAV.ahead = [], []
    NAV.cur = FlowText(cfunc, var=var, obj_ea=obj_ea)
    _render()


def _target(vu):
    """(var number, global ea) under the cursor of a pseudocode view."""
    if not vu.get_current_item(hr.USE_KEYBOARD):
        return None, None
    it = vu.item
    if it.citype == hr.VDI_EXPR:
        e = it.e
        if e.op == hr.cot_var:
            return e.v.idx, None
        if e.op == hr.cot_obj:
            return None, e.obj_ea
    if it.citype == hr.VDI_LVAR:
        lv = it.get_lvar()
        for n, v in enumerate(vu.cfunc.lvars):
            if v.name == lv.name:
                return n, None
    return None, None


class _Handler(ida_kernwin.action_handler_t):
    def activate(self, ctx):
        vu = hr.get_widget_vdui(ctx.widget)
        if not vu:
            return 0
        var, obj_ea = _target(vu)
        if var is None and obj_ea is None:
            ida_kernwin.warning(T("Put the cursor on a variable first.", "请先把光标放在一个变量上。"))
            return 0
        open_flow(vu.cfunc, var=var, obj_ea=obj_ea)
        return 1

    def update(self, ctx):
        if ctx.widget_type == ida_kernwin.BWN_PSEUDOCODE:
            return ida_kernwin.AST_ENABLE_FOR_WIDGET
        return ida_kernwin.AST_DISABLE_FOR_WIDGET


class _UiHooks(ida_kernwin.UI_Hooks):
    def finish_populating_widget_popup(self, widget, popup, ctx=None):
        if ida_kernwin.get_widget_type(widget) == ida_kernwin.BWN_PSEUDOCODE:
            ida_kernwin.attach_action_to_popup(widget, popup, ACTION, None)

    def preprocess_action(self, name):
        # IDA's own graph/text switch (Space) and, where IDA routes them to our windows,
        # its Back / Forward act on the data-flow windows
        acts = {"ToggleRenderer": toggle, "Return": back, "UndoReturn": forward}
        if name in acts and _ours(ida_kernwin.get_current_widget()):
            acts[name]()
            return 1
        return 0


_POPUP = None
_HX = None


class _HxHooks(hr.Hexrays_Hooks):
    def double_click(self, vu, shift):
        try:
            _sync(vu)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return 0                                  # IDA does what it does on a double-click too


def _label():
    return T("Data flow of this variable", "这个变量的数据流")


def _tip():
    return T("Show only the lines this variable's value comes from and goes to",
             "只显示这个变量的值从哪来、到哪去的那些行")


def relabel():
    """After a language switch: the menu entry, and the open window (rebuilt, so its title
    changes too; Back / Forward keep their history)."""
    global _TEXT, _GRAPH
    ida_kernwin.update_action_label(ACTION, _label())
    ida_kernwin.update_action_tooltip(ACTION, _tip())
    if NAV.cur is None or (_TEXT is None and _GRAPH is None):
        return
    _CLOSING[0] = True
    try:
        for v in (_TEXT, _GRAPH):
            if v is not None:
                v.Close()
    finally:
        _CLOSING[0] = False
    _TEXT = _GRAPH = None
    _CACHE.pop("chart", None)                 # its words are in the old language
    _refresh(NAV.cur.var, NAV.cur.obj_ea)


def install():
    """Register the action (Ctrl-Alt-F), its pseudocode context-menu entry, and IDA's Back /
    Forward inside the data-flow windows."""
    global _POPUP
    ida_kernwin.unregister_action(ACTION)
    ok = ida_kernwin.register_action(ida_kernwin.action_desc_t(
        ACTION, _label(), _Handler(), HOTKEY, _tip(), -1))
    if _POPUP is None:
        _POPUP = _UiHooks()
        _POPUP.hook()
    global _HX
    if _HX is None:
        _HX = _HxHooks()
        _HX.hook()
    return ok


def uninstall():
    global _POPUP
    if _POPUP is not None:
        _POPUP.unhook()
        _POPUP = None
    global _HX
    if _HX is not None:
        _HX.unhook()
        _HX = None
    ida_kernwin.unregister_action(ACTION)

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/fntree.py`:

```py
"""Hex-Rays ctree -> ports.Tree, the neutral statement tree the analyses read.

Only the fields that belong to an item's operator are read: cexpr_t keeps its operands in a
union, so e.x on a number is not None but garbage."""
import re
import sys

import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_hexrays as hr
import ida_lines
import ida_nalt
import ida_name
import ida_segment
import ida_typeinf
import idaapi

from ... import ports

_BIN = {hr.cot_add: "+", hr.cot_sub: "-", hr.cot_mul: "*", hr.cot_sdiv: "/", hr.cot_udiv: "/",
        hr.cot_smod: "%", hr.cot_umod: "%", hr.cot_band: "&", hr.cot_bor: "|", hr.cot_xor: "^",
        hr.cot_shl: "<<", hr.cot_sshr: ">>", hr.cot_ushr: ">>", hr.cot_land: "&&", hr.cot_lor: "||",
        hr.cot_eq: "==", hr.cot_ne: "!=", hr.cot_sge: ">=", hr.cot_uge: ">=", hr.cot_sle: "<=",
        hr.cot_ule: "<=", hr.cot_sgt: ">", hr.cot_ugt: ">", hr.cot_slt: "<", hr.cot_ult: "<",
        hr.cot_fadd: "+", hr.cot_fsub: "-", hr.cot_fmul: "*", hr.cot_fdiv: "/"}
_UN = {hr.cot_neg: "-", hr.cot_lnot: "!", hr.cot_bnot: "~", hr.cot_fneg: "-"}
_ASG = {hr.cot_asg: "", hr.cot_asgbor: "|", hr.cot_asgxor: "^", hr.cot_asgband: "&",
        hr.cot_asgadd: "+", hr.cot_asgsub: "-", hr.cot_asgmul: "*", hr.cot_asgsshr: ">>",
        hr.cot_asgushr: ">>", hr.cot_asgshl: "<<", hr.cot_asgsdiv: "/", hr.cot_asgudiv: "/",
        hr.cot_asgsmod: "%", hr.cot_asgumod: "%"}
_X = (hr.cot_ptr, hr.cot_ref, hr.cot_cast, hr.cot_memref, hr.cot_memptr)


def _ea(item):
    return None if item.ea == idaapi.BADADDR else item.ea


def _printed(e):
    """Hex-Rays' own text of an expression, without colour tags."""
    try:
        return ida_lines.tag_remove(e.print1(None) or "")
    except Exception:
        return ""


def _member_is_array(e, name):
    """The structure member e reads is an array. Hex-Rays types `p->arr` as a pointer where
    the array decays, so the expression's own type does not say it."""
    try:
        t = e.x.type
        if e.op == hr.cot_memptr:
            t = t.get_pointed_object()
        udt = ida_typeinf.udt_type_data_t()
        if not t.get_udt_details(udt):
            return False
        return any(u.name == name and u.type.is_array() for u in udt)
    except Exception:
        return False


def _strlit(ea):
    """The text of a string literal at ea, or None."""
    try:
        if not ida_bytes.is_strlit(ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)):
            return None
        b = ida_bytes.get_strlit_contents(ea, -1, ida_nalt.get_str_type(ea))
        return b.decode("utf-8", "replace") if b is not None else None
    except Exception:
        return None


def _global_array(ea):
    """The global at ea is an array (a string literal included). Passed to a call it decays
    to a pointer, and only its declaration still says so."""
    try:
        tif = ida_typeinf.tinfo_t()
        if ida_nalt.get_tinfo(tif, ea):
            return tif.is_array()
        f = ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)
        if ida_bytes.is_strlit(f):
            return True
        if ida_bytes.is_data(f):
            return ida_bytes.get_item_size(ea) > max(1, ida_bytes.get_data_elsize(ea, f))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return False


def _library(ea, name):
    """A function the program did not write: an import, or library code FLIRT recognised.
    False for data and for the program's own functions."""
    f = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
    if f is None:
        seg = ida_segment.getseg(ea)
        return seg is not None and seg.type == ida_segment.SEG_XTRN
    return bool(f.flags & (ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB | ida_funcs.FUNC_THUNK)) or name.startswith("unknown_libname")


def _decl(tif, name):
    """`type name` the way a declaration reads (`char buf[32]`, `__m64 v29`)."""
    try:
        return tif._print(name, ida_typeinf.PRTYPE_1LINE) or name
    except Exception:
        return name


class _Conv:
    def __init__(self, cfunc):
        self.cf = cfunc

    def expr(self, e):
        if e is None or e.op == hr.cot_empty:
            return None
        op, t = e.op, e.type
        kw = {"ea": _ea(e), "index": e.index,
              "ptr": bool(t is not None and t.is_ptr()), "mem": bool(t is not None and t.is_array())}
        if op in _ASG:
            return ports.Tree("asg", op=_ASG[op], x=self.expr(e.x), y=self.expr(e.y), **kw)
        if op in (hr.cot_preinc, hr.cot_postinc, hr.cot_predec, hr.cot_postdec):
            kind = "inc" if op in (hr.cot_preinc, hr.cot_postinc) else "dec"
            pre = "pre" if op in (hr.cot_preinc, hr.cot_predec) else "post"
            return ports.Tree(kind, op=pre, x=self.expr(e.x), **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_var:
            lv = self.cf.lvars[e.v.idx]
            if lv.type().is_array():
                kw["mem"] = True                  # an array passed on decays to a pointer
            return ports.Tree("var", var=e.v.idx, name=lv.name, **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_obj:
            name = ida_name.get_name(e.obj_ea) or ""
            text = _printed(e)
            shown = text if re.fullmatch(r"[\w:~<>]+", text or "") else name   # demangled
            func = t is not None and t.is_func()
            own = ida_typeinf.tinfo_t()
            if not func and ida_nalt.get_tinfo(own, e.obj_ea):
                decl = _decl(own, shown)          # its declaration, not the decayed pointer
            else:
                decl = "" if func else _decl(t, shown)
            lit = _strlit(e.obj_ea)
            if not func and not kw["mem"] and _global_array(e.obj_ea):
                kw["mem"] = True
            return ports.Tree("obj", obj_ea=e.obj_ea, name=name, decl=decl, text=text,
                              string=lit if lit is not None else "", strlit=lit is not None,
                              lib=_library(e.obj_ea, name), **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_num:
            return ports.Tree("num", value=e.numval(), text=_printed(e), **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_fnum:
            return ports.Tree("fnum", **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_str:
            return ports.Tree("str", string=e.string, **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_helper:
            return ports.Tree("helper", name=e.helper or "", **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_call:
            return ports.Tree("call", x=self.expr(e.x), args=[self.expr(a) for a in e.a], **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_idx:
            return ports.Tree("idx", x=self.expr(e.x), y=self.expr(e.y), **kw)
        if op in (hr.cot_memref, hr.cot_memptr):
            m = re.search(r"(\w+)$", _printed(e))
            field = m.group(1) if m else ""
            if not kw["mem"] and _member_is_array(e, field):
                kw["mem"] = True
            return ports.Tree("mem", x=self.expr(e.x), op="->" if op == hr.cot_memptr else ".",
                              name=field, **kw)
        if op in _X:
            kind = {hr.cot_ptr: "ptr", hr.cot_ref: "ref", hr.cot_cast: "cast"}[op]
            return ports.Tree(kind, x=self.expr(e.x), **kw)
        if op in _BIN:
            return ports.Tree("bin", op=_BIN[op], x=self.expr(e.x), y=self.expr(e.y), **kw)
        if op in _UN:
            return ports.Tree("un", op=_UN[op], x=self.expr(e.x), **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_tern:
            return ports.Tree("tern", x=self.expr(e.x), y=self.expr(e.y), z=self.expr(e.z), **kw)
        if op == hr.cot_comma:
            return ports.Tree("comma", x=self.expr(e.x), y=self.expr(e.y), **kw)
        return ports.Tree("other", **kw)          # sizeof, type, statement-in-expression

    def stmt(self, i):
        if i is None:
            return None
        op = i.op
        kw = {"ea": _ea(i), "index": i.index, "label": i.label_num if i.label_num >= 0 else None}
        if op == hr.cit_block:
            return ports.Tree("block", body=[self.stmt(c) for c in i.cblock], **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_expr:
            return ports.Tree("expr", x=self.expr(i.cexpr), **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_if:
            body = [self.stmt(i.cif.ithen)] + ([self.stmt(i.cif.ielse)] if i.cif.ielse else [])
            return ports.Tree("if", x=self.expr(i.cif.expr), body=body, **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_for:
            f = i.cfor
            return ports.Tree("for", init=self.expr(f.init), x=self.expr(f.expr), step=self.expr(f.step),
                              body=[self.stmt(f.body)], **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_while:
            return ports.Tree("while", x=self.expr(i.cwhile.expr), body=[self.stmt(i.cwhile.body)], **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_do:
            return ports.Tree("do", x=self.expr(i.cdo.expr), body=[self.stmt(i.cdo.body)], **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_switch:
            cases = []
            for c in i.cswitch.cases:
                t = self.stmt(c)
                if t is not None:
                    t.values = [int(v) for v in c.values]
                    cases.append(t)
            return ports.Tree("switch", x=self.expr(i.cswitch.expr), body=cases, **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_return:
            return ports.Tree("return", x=self.expr(i.creturn.expr), **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_goto:
            return ports.Tree("goto", target=i.cgoto.label_num, **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_break:
            return ports.Tree("break", **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_continue:
            return ports.Tree("continue", **kw)
        if op == hr.cit_empty:
            return ports.Tree("block", body=[], **kw)
        return ports.Tree("other", **kw)          # asm, try, throw


def function_tree(cfunc):
    """(body as a Tree "block", {var number: name}) of a decompiled function."""
    old = sys.getrecursionlimit()
    sys.setrecursionlimit(max(old, 20000))
    try:
        body = _Conv(cfunc).stmt(cfunc.body)
    finally:
        sys.setrecursionlimit(old)
    return body, {n: v.name for n, v in enumerate(cfunc.lvars)}


def var_info(cfunc):
    """{var number: (declaration, is an argument)}."""
    return {n: (_decl(v.type(), v.name), bool(v.is_arg_var)) for n, v in enumerate(cfunc.lvars)}

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/live.py`:

```py
"""Live Hex-Rays rendering: notes appended to printed pseudocode, nothing stored."""
import re

import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines

from ...core.notes import collect_notes
from .context import PROG


_ADDR_TAG = ida_lines.COLOR_ON + chr(ida_lines.COLOR_ADDR)
_ADDR_RE = re.compile(re.escape(_ADDR_TAG) + ("([0-9A-Fa-f]{%d})" % ida_lines.COLOR_ADDR_SIZE))


def _line_item_indices(cfunc, line):
    out = []
    n = cfunc.treeitems.size()
    for m in _ADDR_RE.finditer(line):
        v = int(m.group(1), 16) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        if (v & 0xC0000000) == 0 and v < n:                 # ANCHOR_CITEM index
            out.append(v)
    return out


try:
    from .ctree import calls as _calls
except Exception:                     # no Hex-Rays: nothing calls _collect_notes then
    _calls = None


def _collect_notes(cfunc):
    """(by_ea, by_idx) notes of a decompiled function; see core.notes.collect_notes."""
    return collect_notes(PROG, _calls(cfunc))


def _render_into_sv(cfunc):
    """Append our notes to the pseudocode lines in-place. Returns count.
       Each call's note is placed once, on the line carrying that call's ctree
       item index (never on a parent brace that merely shares the address)."""
    sv = cfunc.get_pseudocode()          # populates item indices (ctree_item numbering)
    _, by_idx = _collect_notes(cfunc)
    if not by_idx:
        return 0
    tail = ida_lines.COLOR_ON + chr(ida_lines.COLOR_AUTOCMT)
    off = ida_lines.COLOR_OFF + chr(ida_lines.COLOR_AUTOCMT)
    placed = set()
    added = 0
    for i in range(sv.size()):
        line = sv[i].line
        hit = []
        for idx in _line_item_indices(cfunc, line):
            if idx in by_idx and idx not in placed:
                hit.append(by_idx[idx]); placed.add(idx)
        if hit:
            shown = ida_lines.tag_remove(line)
            hit = [h for h in dict.fromkeys(hit) if h not in shown]   # persisted already
        if hit:
            sv[i].line = line + "  " + tail + "// " + "   ".join(hit) + off
            added += 1
    return added


_RENDER_ON = [True]


try:
    import ida_hexrays as _hr_mod

    class _IBIHooks(_hr_mod.Hexrays_Hooks):
        def func_printed(self, cfunc):
            if _RENDER_ON[0]:
                try:
                    _render_into_sv(cfunc)
                except Exception:
                    pass
            return 0
    _HOOKS = _IBIHooks()
except Exception:
    _HOOKS = None


_hook_installed = [False]


def _refresh_pseudo_views():
    # every open Pseudocode-X, not just the current widget (that is our panel
    # when a button was clicked)
    try:
        import ida_hexrays as hr
        hr.clear_cached_cfuncs()
        for c in "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ":
            w = ida_kernwin.find_widget("Pseudocode-%s" % c)
            vu = hr.get_widget_vdui(w) if w else None
            if vu:
                vu.refresh_view(True)
    except Exception:
        pass


def set_live_render(on):
    _RENDER_ON[0] = on
    if _HOOKS is None:
        return False
    if on and not _hook_installed[0]:
        _hook_installed[0] = bool(_HOOKS.hook())      # retried next time if it failed
    _refresh_pseudo_views()
    return True

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/panel.py`:

```py
"""The dockable panel (Qt) and the plain form used when Qt is missing."""
import ida_funcs
import ida_kernwin
import idaapi

from ...core import i18n
from ...core.i18n import T
from ...core.report import _fmt
from .service import (_pse_err, annotate_all, annotate_math, annotate_pseudo, do_apply_sig, do_undo,
                      run_detection, set_live_render)
from .settings import save_lang

# the binding must match IDA's own Qt: Qt6/PySide6 from IDA 9.2, Qt5/PyQt5 before.
# A pip-installed PySide6 inside an IDA 9.1 would otherwise load Qt6 into a Qt5 process.
_HAS_QT = False
for _qt in (("PySide6", "PyQt5") if getattr(idaapi, "IDA_SDK_VERSION", 0) >= 920
            else ("PyQt5", "PySide6")):
    try:
        QtWidgets = __import__(_qt + ".QtWidgets", fromlist=["QtWidgets"]); _HAS_QT = True
        break
    except Exception:
        pass

_panel = None

if _HAS_QT:
    class IBIPanel(ida_kernwin.PluginForm):
        def OnCreate(self, form):
            conv = getattr(self, "FormToPyWidget", None) or getattr(self, "FormToPyQtWidget")
            self.parent = conv(form); self._r = None; self._build()

        def _build(self):
            L = QtWidgets.QVBoxLayout()
            top = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
            self.title = QtWidgets.QLabel("<b>IDA Better Reverse</b>")
            top.addWidget(self.title, 1)
            top.addWidget(QtWidgets.QLabel("Lang:"))
            self.lang = QtWidgets.QComboBox(); self.lang.addItems(["English", "中文"])
            self.lang.setCurrentIndex(1 if i18n.LANG == "zh" else 0)
            self.lang.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_lang)
            top.addWidget(self.lang)
            L.addLayout(top)
            self.result = QtWidgets.QTextEdit(); self.result.setReadOnly(True); self.result.setMinimumHeight(150)
            L.addWidget(self.result)
            self.cb_live = QtWidgets.QCheckBox(); self.cb_live.setChecked(True)
            self.cb_live.stateChanged.connect(self.on_live)
            self.cb_sig = QtWidgets.QCheckBox(); self.cb_sig.setChecked(True)
            self.cb_ann = QtWidgets.QCheckBox(); self.cb_ann.setChecked(True)
            self.cb_pse = QtWidgets.QCheckBox(); self.cb_pse.setChecked(False)
            self.cb_math = QtWidgets.QCheckBox(); self.cb_math.setChecked(False)
            for w in (self.cb_live, self.cb_sig, self.cb_ann, self.cb_pse, self.cb_math):
                L.addWidget(w)
            h = QtWidgets.QHBoxLayout()
            self.b0 = QtWidgets.QPushButton(); self.b1 = QtWidgets.QPushButton(); self.b2 = QtWidgets.QPushButton()
            self.b0.clicked.connect(self.on_detect); self.b1.clicked.connect(self.on_run); self.b2.clicked.connect(self.on_undo)
            h.addWidget(self.b0); h.addWidget(self.b1); h.addWidget(self.b2); L.addLayout(h)
            self.loglabel = QtWidgets.QLabel()
            L.addWidget(self.loglabel)
            self.log = QtWidgets.QTextEdit(); self.log.setReadOnly(True); L.addWidget(self.log)
            self.parent.setLayout(L)
            self._retext()
            set_live_render(True)     # live annotations on by default

        def _retext(self):
            # short labels (they truncate in a docked panel); details live in tooltips
            for cb, en, zh, ten, tzh in (
                    (self.cb_live, "Live annotations", "实时注释",
                     "Annotate the decompiler as you browse. Writes nothing to the database.",
                     "浏览时实时给反编译加注释，不写数据库。"),
                    (self.cb_sig, "Apply FLIRT signature", "套用 FLIRT 签名",
                     "Name library functions with the signature matching the detected build.",
                     "用与识别结果匹配的签名给库函数命名。"),
                    (self.cb_ann, "Describe libc functions", "libc 函数说明",
                     "Add a one-line description to recognised libc functions (keeps your comments).",
                     "给识别出的 libc 函数加一行用途说明（保留你的注释）。"),
                    (self.cb_pse, "Persist comments (this function)", "注释落盘（当前函数）",
                     "Write the current function's annotations into the database as comments.",
                     "把当前函数的注释作为普通注释写进数据库。"),
                    (self.cb_math, "Math / SIMD in disassembly", "反汇编算术 / SIMD",
                     "Annotate arithmetic and vector instructions of the current function.",
                     "给当前函数的算术和向量指令加注释。")):
                cb.setText(T(en, zh)); cb.setToolTip(T(ten, tzh))
            self.b0.setText(T("Detect", "检测"))
            self.b1.setText(T("Run", "执行"))
            self.b2.setText(T("Undo", "撤回"))
            self.loglabel.setText(T("log:", "日志："))

        def on_lang(self, idx):
            i18n.set_lang("zh" if idx == 1 else "en")
            save_lang(); self._retext()
            try:                                        # data-flow menu entry and window
                from . import flowview
                flowview.relabel()
            except Exception:
                pass
            if self._r:
                self.result.setPlainText(_fmt(self._r))
            set_live_render(self.cb_live.isChecked())    # re-render in new language

        def on_live(self, _state):
            set_live_render(self.cb_live.isChecked())

        def on_detect(self):
            r, err = run_detection(); self._r = r
            self.result.setPlainText(err if err else _fmt(r))

        def on_run(self):
            out = []
            if self.cb_sig.isChecked():
                if not self._r:
                    self.on_detect()
                out.append("[sig] " + (do_apply_sig(self._r) if self._r
                           else T("detect first.", "先检测。")))
            if self.cb_ann.isChecked():
                out.append("[libc] " + T("+%d function comments.", "+%d 处函数注释。") % annotate_all())
            if self.cb_pse.isChecked():
                ea = ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea()
                nm = ida_funcs.get_func_name(ea) if ida_funcs.get_func(ea) else "?"
                n = annotate_pseudo(ea)
                out.append("[pseudo] " + self._pseudo_msg(nm, n))
            if self.cb_math.isChecked():
                n = annotate_math()
                out.append("[math] " + (T("cursor not in a function.", "光标不在函数里。") if n == -1
                                        else T("+%d insn comments.", "+%d 处指令注释。") % n))
            if not out:
                out.append(T("(nothing selected)", "（什么都没勾）"))
            set_live_render(self.cb_live.isChecked())     # resumes the notes Undo paused
            self.log.append("\n".join(out))
            if self._r:
                self.result.setPlainText(_fmt(self._r))

        def _pseudo_msg(self, nm, n):
            if n == -1:
                return T("cursor not inside a function.", "光标不在函数里。")
            if n == -2:
                return T("Hex-Rays decompiler unavailable.", "没有 Hex-Rays 反编译器。")
            if n == -3:
                return T("error: ", "出错：") + _pse_err[0]
            if n == 0:
                return "%s: " % nm + T("nothing to annotate.", "没有可注释的调用。")
            return "%s: " % nm + T("+%d comment(s).", "+%d 处注释。") % n

        def on_undo(self):
            # do_undo pauses the live notes; the checkbox keeps the preference and
            # the next Run brings them back
            self.log.append("[undo] " + do_undo())

        def OnClose(self, form):
            global _panel; _panel = None

def open_panel():
    global _panel
    if not _HAS_QT:
        r, err = run_detection()
        if err:
            ida_kernwin.warning(err); return
        set_live_render(True)
        f = ida_kernwin.Form(r"""IDA Better Reverse
%s
<##Apply matching FLIRT signature:{rSig}>
<##Annotate libc functions (keep yours):{rAnn}>
<##Persist decompiler comments (current func):{rPse}>
<##Annotate math/SIMD in disassembly:{rMath}>
<##Undo last run:{rUndo}>{cG}>
""" % _fmt(r).replace("%", "%%"),
            {"cG": ida_kernwin.Form.ChkGroupControl(("rSig", "rAnn", "rPse", "rMath", "rUndo"))})
        f.Compile(); f.rSig.checked = True; f.rAnn.checked = True
        f.rPse.checked = False; f.rMath.checked = False; f.rUndo.checked = False
        if f.Execute() == 1:
            msg = []
            if f.rUndo.checked:
                msg.append("[undo] " + do_undo())
            else:
                if f.rSig.checked:
                    msg.append("[sig] " + do_apply_sig(r))
                if f.rAnn.checked:
                    msg.append("[libc] +%d." % annotate_all())
                if f.rPse.checked:
                    n = annotate_pseudo()
                    msg.append("[pseudo] " + ("not in a function." if n == -1 else
                                              ("no decompiler." if n == -2 else
                                               ("error: " + _pse_err[0] if n == -3 else "+%d." % n))))
                if f.rMath.checked:
                    msg.append("[math] +%d." % annotate_math())
            print("\n".join(msg)); ida_kernwin.info("\n".join(msg) or "nothing to do")
        f.Free(); return
    _panel = IBIPanel()
    try:
        _panel.Show("IDA Better Reverse", options=ida_kernwin.PluginForm.WOPN_PERSIST)
    except Exception:
        _panel.Show("IDA Better Reverse")
```
