`src/ibr/adapters/ida/persist.py`:

```py
"""Persist pseudocode notes as Hex-Rays user comments (reversible)."""
import ida_funcs
import ida_kernwin
import ida_lines

from ...core.comments import _JOIN, _strip_managed
from .context import JOURNAL
from .live import _RENDER_ON, _collect_notes


def _shown(cfunc, text):
    sv = cfunc.get_pseudocode()
    return sum(ida_lines.tag_remove(sv[i].line).count(text) for i in range(sv.size()))


def _hx_put(cfunc, ea, text, func_ea):
    """Persist one note as a user comment at the first item position of `ea` where
    Hex-Rays actually prints it. A position that would leave it orphaned gets back
    exactly what it held; other orphan comments (possibly the user's) are left alone."""
    import ida_hexrays as hr
    for itp in (hr.ITP_SEMI, hr.ITP_BLOCK1, hr.ITP_BLOCK2, hr.ITP_CASE, hr.ITP_COLON,
                hr.ITP_CURLY1, hr.ITP_CURLY2, hr.ITP_BRACE1, hr.ITP_BRACE2, hr.ITP_ELSE):
        tl = hr.treeloc_t(); tl.ea = ea; tl.itp = itp
        try:
            prev = cfunc.get_user_cmt(tl, hr.RETRIEVE_ALWAYS) or ""
        except Exception:
            prev = ""
        body = _strip_managed(prev)
        newtext = text if not body else (text + " " + _JOIN + body)
        if newtext == prev:
            return True                   # already persisted here
        before = _shown(cfunc, newtext)
        cfunc.set_user_cmt(tl, newtext)
        cfunc.refresh_func_ctext()
        if _shown(cfunc, newtext) > before:
            cfunc.save_user_cmts()
            JOURNAL.record("pcmts", (func_ea, ea, itp), prev, newtext)
            return True
        cfunc.set_user_cmt(tl, prev)      # not printed at this position: put it back
        cfunc.refresh_func_ctext()
    return False


def annotate_pseudo(func_ea=None):
    """Persist decompiler notes as user comments in the CURRENT function.
       Returns count; -1 not in a function; -2 no decompiler; -3 error."""
    try:
        import ida_hexrays as hr
    except Exception:
        return -2
    try:
        if not hr.init_hexrays_plugin():
            return -2
        if func_ea is None:
            func_ea = ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea()
        f = ida_funcs.get_func(func_ea)
        if not f:
            return -1
        cfunc = hr.decompile(f.start_ea)
        if not cfunc:
            return -2
        notes, _ = _collect_notes(cfunc)
        if not notes:
            return 0
        was_live = _RENDER_ON[0]
        _RENDER_ON[0] = False     # _hx_put counts printed copies; live copies would blur that
        try:
            cfunc.refresh_func_ctext()
            added = sum(1 for ea, txt in notes.items() if _hx_put(cfunc, ea, txt, f.start_ea))
        finally:
            _RENDER_ON[0] = was_live
        cfunc.save_user_cmts(); cfunc.refresh_func_ctext()
        return added
    except Exception as ex:
        _pse_err[0] = "%s" % ex
        return -3
_pse_err = [""]

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/plugin.py`:

```py
"""IDA plugin entry (Ctrl-Alt-G)."""
import idaapi

from ...core.i18n import T
from . import live
from .panel import open_panel
from .settings import load_lang

load_lang()


class IBIPlugin(idaapi.plugin_t):
    flags = idaapi.PLUGIN_KEEP
    comment = "Detect runtime/compiler, apply FLIRT, annotate libc + math (undoable)"
    help = "Ctrl-Alt-G"
    wanted_name = "IDA Better Reverse"
    wanted_hotkey = "Ctrl-Alt-G"

    def init(self):
        print("[IDA Better Reverse] " + T("loaded. Press Ctrl-Alt-G.",
                                          "已加载。按 Ctrl-Alt-G 打开面板。"))
        try:                                      # needs Hex-Rays
            from . import flowview
            flowview.install()
        except Exception:
            pass
        return idaapi.PLUGIN_KEEP

    def term(self):
        try:
            if live._HOOKS is not None and live._hook_installed[0]:
                live._HOOKS.unhook()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            from . import flowview
            flowview.uninstall()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def run(self, arg):
        open_panel()


def PLUGIN_ENTRY():
    return IBIPlugin()

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/program.py`:

```py
"""The ports implemented on top of idaapi."""
import os

import ida_auto
import ida_bytes
import ida_funcs
import ida_name
import idaapi
import idautils
import idc

from ... import ports


def cur_arch():
    try:
        import ida_ida
        if not ida_ida.inf_is_64bit():
            pn = ida_ida.inf_get_procname()
            return "i386" if pn == "metapc" else "arm" if pn.lower().startswith("arm") else pn
        pn = ida_ida.inf_get_procname()
        if pn == "metapc":
            return "x86_64"
        if pn.lower().startswith("arm") or pn.upper() == "AARCH64":
            return "aarch64"
        return pn
    except Exception:
        return None


def is_pe():
    try:
        import ida_ida
        return ida_ida.inf_get_filetype() == ida_ida.f_PE
    except Exception:
        return False


def _exec_segments():
    import ida_segment
    seg = ida_segment.get_first_seg()
    while seg is not None:
        yield seg
        seg = ida_segment.get_next_seg(seg.start_ea)


class IdaProgram(ports.Program):
    def arch(self):
        return cur_arch()

    def is_pe(self):
        return is_pe()

    # ---- bytes ----
    def input_bytes(self):
        path = idaapi.get_input_file_path()
        if path and os.path.exists(path):
            try:
                return open(path, "rb").read()
            except Exception:
                return None
        return None

    def header_bytes(self):
        return ida_bytes.get_bytes(idaapi.get_imagebase(), 0x1000) or b""

    def image_bytes(self):
        """Concatenate the executable segments (for fingerprinting and packer heuristics)."""
        import ida_segment
        chunks = []
        for seg in _exec_segments():
            if seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_EXEC:
                b = ida_bytes.get_bytes(seg.start_ea, min(seg.size(), 64 << 20))
                if b:
                    chunks.append(b)
        return b"".join(chunks)

    def has_code(self):
        import ida_segment
        for seg in _exec_segments():
            if seg.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_EXEC and seg.size() > 0:
                return True
        return any(True for _ in idautils.Functions())

    def function_heads(self, n):
        for ea in idautils.Functions():
            yield ida_bytes.get_bytes(ea, n)

    # ---- functions ----
    def functions(self):
        return list(idautils.Functions())

    def func_start(self, ea):
        f = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        return f.start_ea if f else None

    def func_name(self, ea):
        return ida_funcs.get_func_name(ea)

    def set_func_name(self, ea, name):
        if not name:
            # empty name = let IDA regenerate sub_XXXX; SN_FORCE makes this fail
            return ida_name.set_name(ea, "", ida_name.SN_NOWARN)
        return ida_name.set_name(ea, name, ida_name.SN_NOWARN | ida_name.SN_NOCHECK | ida_name.SN_FORCE)

    def is_lib(self, ea):
        f = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        return bool(f is not None and f.flags & ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB)

    def clear_lib(self, ea):
        f = ida_funcs.get_func(ea)
        if f is not None:
            f.flags &= ~ida_funcs.FUNC_LIB
            ida_funcs.update_func(f)

    # ---- comments ----
    def func_cmt(self, ea):
        return idc.get_func_cmt(ea, 1)

    def set_func_cmt(self, ea, text):
        idc.set_func_cmt(ea, text, 1)

    def insn_cmt(self, ea):
        return idc.get_cmt(ea, 0)

    def set_insn_cmt(self, ea, text):
        idc.set_cmt(ea, text, 0)

    # ---- types ----
    def type_snapshot(self):
        """{ea: (tinfo copy, user-specified?)} for every function that has a stored type."""
        import ida_nalt, ida_typeinf
        snap = {}
        for ea in idautils.Functions():
            t = ida_typeinf.tinfo_t()
            if ida_nalt.get_tinfo(t, ea):
                snap[ea] = (t, bool(ida_nalt.is_userti(ea)))
        return snap

    def type_str(self, ea):
        import ida_nalt, ida_typeinf
        t = ida_typeinf.tinfo_t()
        return str(t) if ida_nalt.get_tinfo(t, ea) else None

    def has_user_type(self, ea):
        import ida_nalt
        return bool(ida_nalt.is_userti(ea))

    def apply_type(self, ea, tif):
        import ida_typeinf
        return bool(ida_typeinf.apply_tinfo(ea, tif, ida_typeinf.TINFO_DEFINITE))

    def restore_type(self, ea, saved):
        import ida_nalt, ida_typeinf
        ida_nalt.del_tinfo(ea)
        if saved is not None:
            ida_typeinf.apply_tinfo(ea, saved[0], ida_typeinf.TINFO_DEFINITE if saved[1]
                                    else ida_typeinf.TINFO_GUESSED)

    # ---- data ----
    def name_at(self, ea):
        return idc.get_name(ea)

    def cstr_bytes(self, ea):
        f = ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)
        if ida_bytes.is_code(f) or ida_funcs.get_func(ea):
            return None
        if ida_bytes.is_data(f) and not ida_bytes.is_byte(f) and not ida_bytes.is_strlit(f):
            return None                       # a pointer/int variable (e.g. stdout), never text
        b = idc.get_strlit_contents(ea, -1, -1)
        if not b:
            b = (ida_bytes.get_bytes(ea, 256) or b"").split(b"\x00", 1)[0]
        return b

    def is_writable(self, ea):
        try:
            import ida_segment
            s = ida_segment.getseg(ea)
            if s is None:
                return False
            if s.perm:
                return bool(s.perm & ida_segment.SEGPERM_WRITE)
            return (ida_segment.get_segm_name(s) or "").lstrip(".") in ("bss", "data")
        except Exception:
            return False

    def is_byte_buffer(self, ea):
        f = ida_bytes.get_flags(ea)
        return not ida_bytes.is_data(f) or ida_bytes.is_byte(f)

    # ---- instructions ----
    def func_items(self, ea):
        return idautils.FuncItems(ea)

    def insn(self, ea):
        return (idc.print_insn_mnem(ea), idc.print_operand(ea, 0), idc.print_operand(ea, 1),
                idc.print_operand(ea, 2))

    def wait(self):
        ida_auto.auto_wait()


# FLIRT signatures are keyed by directory, not by the .sig processor byte:
# x86 (i386 + x86_64) live in sig/pc, ARM/AArch64 in sig/arm.
_SIG_SUBDIR = {"x86_64": "pc", "i386": "pc", "aarch64": "arm", "arm": "arm"}
_TIL = {"x86_64": "gnulnx_x64", "i386": "gnulnx_x86", "aarch64": "gnulnx_arm64", "arm": "gnulnx_arm"}


class IdaSignatures(ports.Signatures):
    def sig_dirs(self, arch):
        sub = _SIG_SUBDIR.get(arch, "pc")
        out = []
        for base in (getattr(idaapi, "get_user_idadir", lambda: None)(), idaapi.idadir("")):
            if not base:
                continue
            d = os.path.join(base, "sig", sub)
            if os.path.isdir(d) and d not in out:
                out.append(d)
        return out

    def sig_files(self, arch):
        out = []
        for d in self.sig_dirs(arch):
            try:
                out.extend(os.listdir(d))
            except Exception:
                pass
        return out

    def apply(self, signame):
        idaapi.plan_to_apply_idasgn(signame)
        ida_auto.auto_wait()

    def ensure_til(self, arch):
        """Load the gnulnx_* type library so named libc funcs get real prototypes."""
        try:
            import ida_typeinf
            til = ida_typeinf.get_idati()
            names = [til.base(i).name for i in range(til.nbases)]
            want = _TIL.get(arch, "gnuunx")
            if not any(want in n for n in names):
                ida_typeinf.add_til(want, ida_typeinf.ADDTIL_DEFAULT)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def prototype(self, sym):
        try:
            import ida_typeinf
        except Exception:
            return None
        til = ida_typeinf.get_idati()
        # prototypes live in the til's *symbol* table, C names with a leading '_'
        for key in ("_" + sym, sym):
            r = ida_typeinf.get_named_type(til, key, ida_typeinf.NTF_SYMM)
            if r:
                t = ida_typeinf.tinfo_t()
                if t.deserialize(til, r[1], r[2]) and t.is_func():
                    return t
        return None


class IdaDecompiler(ports.Decompiler):
    """Hex-Rays user comments; a location is (ea, item position)."""

    def _cfunc(self, func_ea):
        import ida_hexrays as hr
        return hr.decompile(func_ea)

    def pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, locs):
        import ida_hexrays as hr
        cf = self._cfunc(func_ea)
        if not cf:
            return None
        out = {}
        for ea, itp in locs:
            tl = hr.treeloc_t(); tl.ea = ea; tl.itp = itp
            out[(ea, itp)] = cf.get_user_cmt(tl, hr.RETRIEVE_ALWAYS) or ""
        return out

    def set_pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, texts):
        import ida_hexrays as hr
        cf = self._cfunc(func_ea)
        if not cf:
            return
        for (ea, itp), text in texts.items():
            tl = hr.treeloc_t(); tl.ea = ea; tl.itp = itp
            cf.set_user_cmt(tl, text)
        cf.save_user_cmts(); cf.refresh_func_ctext()

```

`src/ibr/adapters/ida/qt.py`:

```py
"""The Qt binding that matches IDA's own Qt (PySide6 from IDA 9.2, PyQt5 before), and the
QWidget behind an IDA widget. A pip-installed PySide6 inside an IDA 9.1 would otherwise
load Qt6 into a Qt5 process."""
import ctypes

import idaapi

QtCore = QtGui = QtWidgets = None
BINDING = None
for _name in (("PySide6", "PyQt5") if getattr(idaapi, "IDA_SDK_VERSION", 0) >= 920
              else ("PyQt5", "PySide6")):
    try:
        QtCore = __import__(_name + ".QtCore", fromlist=["QtCore"])
        QtGui = __import__(_name + ".QtGui", fromlist=["QtGui"])
        QtWidgets = __import__(_name + ".QtWidgets", fromlist=["QtWidgets"])
        BINDING = _name
        break
    except Exception:
        pass


def _ptr(tw):
    if type(tw).__name__ == "SwigPyObject":
        return int(tw)
    get = ctypes.pythonapi.PyCapsule_GetPointer
    get.restype, get.argtypes = ctypes.c_void_p, [ctypes.py_object, ctypes.c_char_p]
    return get(tw, b"$valid$")


def qwidget(tw):
    """The QWidget behind an IDA TWidget, or None."""
    if tw is None or BINDING is None:
        return None
    p = _ptr(tw)
    if BINDING == "PySide6":
        import shiboken6
        return shiboken6.wrapInstance(p, QtWidgets.QWidget)
    from PyQt5 import sip
    return sip.wrapinstance(p, QtWidgets.QWidget)


def dark_theme():
    """IDA is using a dark colour theme (judged by the window background)."""
    if QtWidgets is None:
        return False
    try:
        return QtWidgets.QApplication.palette().color(QtGui.QPalette.Window).lightness() < 128
    except Exception:
        return False

```

"""The plugin's operations for the panel, scripts and idalib, bound to this IDA session.

These names are also exported at the top level of ida_better_reverse.py."""
import ida_kernwin

from ...app import annotate as _annotate
from ...app import detection as _detection
from ...app import signatures as _signatures
from ...app import undo as _undo
from ...core.asm_math import insn_math
from ...core.i18n import T, set_lang
from ...core.names import _is_dummy_name
from .context import DEC, JOURNAL, PROG, SIGS
from .live import _RENDER_ON, _collect_notes, _refresh_pseudo_views, _render_into_sv, set_live_render
from .persist import _pse_err, annotate_pseudo
from .program import cur_arch as _cur_arch

__all__ = ["run_detection", "do_apply_sig", "annotate_all", "annotate_math", "annotate_pseudo",
           "do_undo", "set_live_render", "set_lang", "_collect_notes", "_render_into_sv",
           "_insn_math", "_is_dummy_name", "_cur_arch", "_pse_err"]


def run_detection():
    return _detection.run_detection(PROG)


def do_apply_sig(r):
    return _signatures.apply_signatures(PROG, SIGS, JOURNAL, r)

    return _annotate.annotate_all(PROG, JOURNAL)

    if func_ea is None:
        func_ea = ida_kernwin.get_screen_ea()
    return _annotate.annotate_math(PROG, JOURNAL, func_ea)


def _insn_math(ea, arch=None):
    return insn_math(*PROG.insn(ea), arch if arch is not None else (_cur_arch() or ""))


def do_undo():
    msg = _undo.undo(PROG, DEC, JOURNAL)
    # the live notes are plugin output too: without this, notes that do not depend
    # on names (SIMD, syscalls, canary reads) would stay on screen after Undo
    was_live = _RENDER_ON[0]
    _RENDER_ON[0] = False
    _refresh_pseudo_views()
    if was_live:
        msg += T(" Live annotations paused until the next Run.", "实时注释已暂停,下次\"执行\"时恢复。")
    return msg

"""The panel language, saved in the IDA user directory."""
import json
import os

from ...core import i18n

try:
    import ida_diskio
    _CFG = os.path.join(ida_diskio.get_user_idadir(), "ida_better_reverse.cfg")
except Exception:
    _CFG = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".ida_better_reverse.cfg")


def load_lang():
    i18n.set_lang("en")
    # pre-rename installs saved their setting as ida_better_information.cfg
    for cfg in (_CFG, _CFG.replace("better_reverse", "better_information")):
        try:
            i18n.set_lang(json.load(open(cfg)).get("lang", "en"))
            return
        except Exception:
            continue


def save_lang():
    try:
        json.dump({"lang": i18n.LANG}, open(_CFG, "w"))
    except Exception:
        pass

"""Challenge-solving analyses, each pure (written against ibr.ports, testable without IDA).

  dataflow.py    data flow of one variable inside one function: the lines its value comes
                 from and goes to (prototype, opened with Ctrl-Alt-F from the pseudocode)
  flowchart.py   the function as a program flowchart, the way textbooks draw one, with the
                 variable's lines lit (Space in the data-flow window; laid out by
                 core/chartlayout.py)
  expr.py        expressions as C and as math for the chart: casts and printf's guessed
                 arguments left out, MMX/SSE intrinsics folded (_m_pxor is ^, a shift pair <<<)

Planned:

  algo.py    algorithm recognition (TEA family, RC4, base64 alphabets, xor loops) with

  vm.py      custom VM helper: dispatcher loop, bytecode dump, per-opcode handler summary,
             disassembler skeleton
"""
