`src/ibr/analysis/dataflow.py`:

```py
"""Data flow of one variable inside one decompiled function: where its value comes from and
where it goes, everything else left out.

Input is the function's statement tree (ports.Tree). The analysis
  1. turns the tree into a control-flow graph of sites: an expression statement, a
     condition, a loop's init or step, a returned value;
  2. computes reaching definitions, so a read is linked only to the writes that can reach
     it (a variable reused for something else later stays apart);
  3. follows pointers to the memory they point at (a call's result such as malloc, a stack
     array, a global, what an argument points to), so a write through one pointer and a read
     through a copy of it meet on the same object;
  4. links every read to the writes that reach it, and slices from the chosen variable:
     backwards from its writes (where the value comes from), forwards from its writes and
     reads (where it goes).

Memory is tracked per object, not per byte: a store to buf[1] and a load of buf[0] are
linked. A call to an unknown function may read and write whatever its pointer arguments
point to; such sites are marked `may`. Nothing here imports IDA.
"""
import collections
import heapq

from ..core.names import canon

# library calls that write through pointer arguments: argument positions (the _chk variants
# keep the destination where the plain function has it)
WRITES = {
    "read": {1}, "pread": {1}, "recv": {1}, "recvfrom": {1}, "readlink": {1},
    "fread": {0}, "fgets": {0}, "gets": {0}, "getline": {0}, "getdelim": {0}, "getrandom": {0},
    "memcpy": {0}, "memmove": {0}, "mempcpy": {0}, "memset": {0}, "bzero": {0},
    "explicit_bzero": {0}, "qmemcpy": {0},
    "strcpy": {0}, "strncpy": {0}, "stpcpy": {0}, "stpncpy": {0}, "strcat": {0},
    "strncat": {0}, "strlcpy": {0},
    "sprintf": {0}, "snprintf": {0}, "vsprintf": {0}, "vsnprintf": {0},
    "qsort": {0}, "pipe": {0}, "time": {0}, "gettimeofday": {0}, "clock_gettime": {1},
}
# scanf family: everything after the format is written
SCANF = {"scanf": 1, "vscanf": 1, "fscanf": 2, "sscanf": 2, "vfscanf": 2, "vsscanf": 2}
# of those, the ones that read what they write over (append, sort, reuse a line buffer)
_READ_TOO = {"strcat", "strncat", "qsort", "getline", "getdelim"}
# library calls that only read what their pointer arguments point to
READS = set("""printf fprintf dprintf vprintf vfprintf puts fputs putchar putc fputc write send
sendto strlen strnlen strcmp strncmp memcmp strcasecmp strncasecmp strchr strrchr strstr memchr
strspn strcspn strpbrk atoi atol atoll strtol strtoul strtoll strtoull strtod strtof fopen fdopen
open openat creat fclose close free perror system exit abort fwrite fflush fseek ftell rewind
setvbuf setbuf signal sigaction alarm sleep usleep srand rand random ptrace getpid getppid
malloc calloc realloc strdup strndup mmap munmap mprotect stack_chk_fail assert_fail
syslog err errx warn warnx unlink remove access stat fstat lstat chdir puts_unlocked""".split())


class Site:
    """A place in the function that reads or writes values."""
    __slots__ = ("id", "expr", "stmt", "role", "ctx", "defs", "wdefs", "uses", "vrhs",
                 "mr", "mw", "odef", "ouse", "calls", "born", "mayw", "may")

    def __init__(self, sid, expr, stmt, role, ctx):
        self.id, self.expr, self.stmt, self.role, self.ctx = sid, expr, stmt, role, ctx
        self.defs = set()       # variables written as a whole (kill earlier writes)
        self.wdefs = set()      # variables written in part (a field, an element)
        self.uses = set()       # variables read
        self.vrhs = {}          # variable -> what was assigned (for pointer tracking)
        self.mr, self.mw = [], []   # addresses read / written through
        self.odef, self.ouse = set(), set()   # memory objects written / read
        self.calls = []
        self.born = set()       # objects created here whose content does not come from the reads
        self.mayw = set()       # objects an unknown call here may write
        self.may = False        # an unknown call may change memory here

    def gen(self):
        return self.defs | self.wdefs | self.odef

    def reads(self):
        return self.uses | self.ouse


class Flow:
    """The result: the sites to show, in program order, and how they depend on each other.

    sites    kept Site objects
    edges    (writer site id, reader site id) pairs among the kept sites
    role     site id -> "self" (writes or reads the variable), "dst" (where its value goes)
             or "src" (where it comes from); a site that is both, through a loop, gets the
             nearer of the two (a tie is "dst")
    may      some kept site calls an unknown function that may change memory
    cut      the slice was longer than the limit and was cut
    keys     what was followed: ("v", n) a variable, ("stk", n) a variable in memory,
             ("g", ea) a global, other tuples a memory object a pointer points at
    """

    def __init__(self, sites, edges, role, may, cut, keys=frozenset()):
        self.sites, self.edges, self.role, self.may, self.cut = sites, edges, role, may, cut
        self.keys = frozenset(keys)


def _lvalue_kinds():
    return ("var", "obj", "ptr", "idx", "mem")


class _Graph:
    def __init__(self, body):
        self.sites, self.succ = [], []
        self.labels, self.gotos, self.loops = {}, [], []
        self.memvars = set()
        self.ptrvars = set()        # variables that hold addresses: pointer typed, cast to a
                                    # pointer, or handed to a library call as its destination
        self._find_memvars(body)
        entry = self._node(None, None, "join", ())
        self._walk(body, [entry], ())
        for preds, tgt in self.gotos:
            if tgt in self.labels:
                self._link(preds, self.labels[tgt])
        self.pred = [set() for _ in self.sites]
        for a, bs in enumerate(self.succ):
            for b in bs:
                self.pred[b].add(a)
        self.live = _closure([entry], self.succ)    # code after a goto/return/break is dead

    # ---- tree -> sites and control flow ----
    def _find_memvars(self, t):
        """Arrays and variables whose address is taken live in memory, not in a register."""
        stack = [t]
        while stack:
            n = stack.pop()
            if n is None:
                continue
            if n.kind == "var" and n.mem:
                self.memvars.add(n.var)
            if n.kind == "var" and n.ptr:
                self.ptrvars.add(n.var)
            if n.kind == "ref" and n.x is not None and n.x.kind == "var":
                self.memvars.add(n.x.var)
            if n.kind == "cast" and n.ptr and n.x is not None and n.x.kind == "var":
                self.ptrvars.add(n.x.var)
            if n.kind == "call":
                for i in _writes(n):
                    a = _strip(n.args[i]) if i < len(n.args) else None
                    if a is not None and a.kind == "var":
                        self.ptrvars.add(a.var)
            stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z, n.init, n.step))
            stack.extend(n.args)
            stack.extend(n.body)

    def _node(self, expr, stmt, role, ctx):
        s = Site(len(self.sites), expr, stmt, role, ctx)
        self.sites.append(s)
        self.succ.append(set())
        if expr is not None:
            self._scan(s, expr)
        return s.id

    def _link(self, preds, n):
        for p in preds:
            self.succ[p].add(n)

    def _walk(self, st, preds, ctx):
        if st is None:
            return preds
        if st.label is not None:
            j = self._node(None, st, "join", ctx)
            self._link(preds, j)
            self.labels[st.label] = j
            preds = [j]
        k, inner = st.kind, ctx + (st,)
        if k == "block":
            for c in st.body:
                preds = self._walk(c, preds, ctx)
            return preds
        if k == "expr":
            n = self._node(st.x, st, "stmt", ctx)
            self._link(preds, n)
            return [n]
        if k == "return":
            n = self._node(st.x, st, "return", ctx)
            self._link(preds, n)
            return []
        if k == "if":
            c = self._node(st.x, st, "cond", ctx)
            self._link(preds, c)
            out = self._walk(st.body[0] if st.body else None, [c], inner)
            return out + (self._walk(st.body[1], [c], inner) if len(st.body) > 1 else [c])
        if k == "while":
            h = self._node(st.x, st, "cond", ctx)
            self._link(preds, h)
            brk = []
            self.loops.append((brk, h))
            out = self._walk(st.body[0] if st.body else None, [h], inner)
            self.loops.pop()
            self._link(out, h)
            return [h] + brk
        if k == "do":
            top = self._node(None, st, "join", ctx)
            cont = self._node(None, st, "join", inner)
            self._link(preds, top)
            brk = []
            self.loops.append((brk, cont))
            out = self._walk(st.body[0] if st.body else None, [top], inner)
            self.loops.pop()
            self._link(out, cont)
            c = self._node(st.x, st, "cond", ctx)
            self._link([cont], c)
            self._link([c], top)
            return [c] + brk
        if k == "for":
            if st.init is not None:
                i = self._node(st.init, st, "init", ctx)
                self._link(preds, i)
                preds = [i]
            h = self._node(st.x, st, "cond", ctx)
            self._link(preds, h)
            cont = self._node(None, st, "join", inner)
            brk = []
            self.loops.append((brk, cont))
            out = self._walk(st.body[0] if st.body else None, [h], inner)
            self.loops.pop()
            self._link(out, cont)
            if st.step is not None:
                s = self._node(st.step, st, "step", inner)
                self._link([cont], s)
                self._link([s], h)
            else:
                self._link([cont], h)
            return [h] + brk
        if k == "switch":
            sw = self._node(st.x, st, "switch", ctx)
            self._link(preds, sw)
            brk, prev = [], []
            self.loops.append((brk, None))
            for case in st.body:
                prev = self._walk(case, [sw] + prev, inner)
            self.loops.pop()
            return prev + brk + [sw]
        if k == "break":
            if self.loops:
                self.loops[-1][0].extend(preds)
            return []
        if k == "continue":
            for _, tgt in reversed(self.loops):
                if tgt is not None:
                    self._link(preds, tgt)
                    break
            return []
        if k == "goto":
            self.gotos.append((preds, st.target))
            return []
        n = self._node(None, st, "join", ctx)
        self._link(preds, n)
        return [n]

    # ---- what one site reads and writes ----
    def _root(self, e):
        """Where an lvalue lives: ("var", n), ("obj", object key) or ("via", address expr)."""
        k = e.kind
        if k == "cast":
            return self._root(e.x)
        if k == "var":
            return ("obj", ("stk", e.var)) if e.var in self.memvars else ("var", e.var)
        if k == "obj":
            return ("obj", ("g", e.obj_ea))
        if k == "ptr":
            return ("via", e.x)
        if k == "mem":
            return ("via", e.x) if e.op == "->" else self._root(e.x)
        if k == "idx":
            return ("via", e.x) if e.x.ptr or not e.x.mem else self._root(e.x)
        return ("via", e)

    def _path(self, s, e):
        """Values read to compute an lvalue's address: indexes, pointers loaded on the way."""
        k = e.kind
        if k == "cast":
            self._path(s, e.x)
        elif k == "ptr":
            self._addr(s, e.x)
        elif k == "mem":
            (self._addr if e.op == "->" else self._path)(s, e.x)
        elif k == "idx":
            (self._addr if (e.x.ptr or not e.x.mem) else self._path)(s, e.x)
            self._rd(s, e.y)

    def _rd_lv(self, s, e):
        """Read the value stored in an lvalue."""
        self._path(s, e)
        r = self._root(e)
        if r[0] == "var":
            s.uses.add(("v", r[1]))
        elif r[0] == "obj":
            s.ouse.add(r[1])
        else:
            s.mr.append(r[1])

    def _wr(self, s, e, rhs):
        k = e.kind
        if k == "cast":
            return self._wr(s, e.x, rhs)
        self._path(s, e)
        r = self._root(e)
        if r[0] == "var":
            if k == "var":
                s.defs.add(("v", r[1]))
                s.vrhs[r[1]] = rhs
            else:
                s.wdefs.add(("v", r[1]))
        elif r[0] == "obj":
            s.odef.add(r[1])
        else:
            s.mw.append(r[1])

    def _addr(self, s, a):
        """An address is computed (nothing is loaded from the final location)."""
        if a is None:
            return
        k = a.kind
        if k == "var":
            if a.var not in self.memvars and not a.ptr and not a.mem:
                s.uses.add(("v", a.var))          # an index or an offset
        elif k in ("cast",):
            self._addr(s, a.x)
        elif k == "bin":
            self._addr(s, a.x)
            self._addr(s, a.y)
        elif k == "ref":
            self._path(s, a.x)
        elif k in ("idx", "mem", "ptr") and a.mem:
            self._path(s, a)                      # an array inside memory: its address
        elif k in ("idx", "mem", "ptr"):
            self._rd_lv(s, a)                     # a pointer loaded from memory
        elif k != "obj":
            self._rd(s, a)

    def _rd(self, s, e):
        """Read the value of an expression."""
        if e is None:
            return
        k = e.kind
        if k in _lvalue_kinds():
            if e.mem:
                self._addr(s, e)                  # an array stands for its address
            else:
                self._rd_lv(s, e)
        elif k == "asg":
            self._rd(s, e.y)
            if e.op:
                self._rd_lv(s, e.x)
            self._wr(s, e.x, e.y if not e.op else ("op", e))
        elif k in ("inc", "dec"):
            self._rd_lv(s, e.x)
            self._wr(s, e.x, ("op", e))
        elif k == "ref":
            self._path(s, e.x)
        elif k == "call":
            for a in e.args:
                if self._is_addr(a):
                    self._addr(s, a)
                else:
                    self._rd(s, a)
            s.calls.append(e)
            s.odef.add(("ret", e.index))          # what a returned pointer points at
            s.born.add(("ret", e.index))
        elif k in ("num", "str", "helper", "fnum"):
            pass
        else:
            for c in (e.x, e.y, e.z):
                self._rd(s, c)
            for c in e.args:
                self._rd(s, c)

    def _scan(self, s, e):
        self._rd(s, e)

    def _is_addr(self, a):
        if a.ptr or a.mem or a.kind in ("ref", "str"):
            return True
        b = _strip(a)
        return b.kind == "var" and b.var in self.ptrvars


def _strip(a):
    while a is not None and a.kind == "cast":
        a = a.x
    return a


def _call_name(e):
    return canon(e.x.name) if e.x is not None and e.x.name else ""


def _writes(e):
    """Argument positions a known call writes through."""
    name = _call_name(e)
    if name in WRITES:
        return WRITES[name]
    if name in SCANF:
        return set(range(SCANF[name], len(e.args)))
    if e.x is not None and e.x.kind == "helper":
        low = name.lower()
        return {0} if ("store" in low or "stos" in low) else set()
    return set()


class _Analysis:
    def __init__(self, body):
        self.g = _Graph(body)
        self.sites = self.g.sites
        self._reaching(var_only=True)
        self._pts_memo = {}
        for s in self.sites:
            self._resolve(s)
        self._reaching(var_only=False)

    def _reaching(self, var_only):
        """Reaching definitions as bit sets: one bit per (site, key) write. Afterwards
        reach(site, key) lists the writer sites whose value can arrive at the site."""
        sites, pred, succ, live = self.sites, self.g.pred, self.g.succ, self.g.live
        bits = {}                       # key -> [(bit, writer site)]
        nbit = 0
        for s in sites:
            for k in (s.defs | s.wdefs if var_only else s.gen()):
                bits.setdefault(k, []).append((nbit, s.id))
                nbit += 1
        mask = {k: sum(1 << b for b, _ in v) for k, v in bits.items()}
        gen, kill = [0] * len(sites), [0] * len(sites)
        for k, v in bits.items():
            for b, sid in v:
                gen[sid] |= 1 << b
                if k in sites[sid].defs:
                    kill[sid] |= mask[k]
        IN, OUT = [0] * len(sites), [0] * len(sites)
        work = collections.deque(sorted(live))
        inq = set(work)
        while work:
            n = work.popleft()
            inq.discard(n)
            x = 0
            for p in pred[n]:
                x |= OUT[p]                 # dead predecessors stay empty
            IN[n] = x
            out = gen[n] | (x & ~kill[n])
            if out != OUT[n]:
                OUT[n] = out
                for m in succ[n]:
                    if m not in inq and m in live:
                        work.append(m)
                        inq.add(m)
        self._in, self._bits = IN, bits

    def reach(self, sid, key):
        """Writer sites of key whose value can arrive at site sid."""
        x = self._in[sid]
        return [w for b, w in self._bits.get(key, ()) if x >> b & 1]

    # ---- pointers ----
    def _pts_var(self, n, s):
        out = set()
        for d in self.reach(s.id, ("v", n)):
            if ("v", n) in self.sites[d].defs:
                out |= self._pts_def(d, n)
        return out or {("arg", n)}

    def _pts_def(self, d, n):
        key = (d, n)
        if key in self._pts_memo:
            return self._pts_memo[key]
        self._pts_memo[key] = set()               # a cycle (p = p + 1 in a loop) adds nothing
        rhs, s = self.sites[d].vrhs.get(n), self.sites[d]
        if isinstance(rhs, tuple):                # p += k, ++p: same objects as before
            r = self._pts_var(n, s)
        elif rhs is None:
            r = {("unk",)}
        else:
            r = self._pts(rhs, s)
        self._pts_memo[key] = r
        return r

    def _pts(self, a, s):
        """Memory objects that address expression a points into, at site s."""
        k = a.kind
        if k == "var":
            if a.var in self.g.memvars:
                return {("stk", a.var)} if a.mem else {("via", ("stk", a.var))}
            return self._pts_var(a.var, s)
        if k == "obj":
            return {("g", a.obj_ea)} if (a.mem or not a.ptr) else {("via", ("g", a.obj_ea))}
        if k == "cast":
            return self._pts(a.x, s)
        if k == "ref":
            return self._objs_of(a.x, s)
        if k == "bin":
            ops = [c for c in (a.x, a.y) if c is not None and c.kind not in ("num", "fnum")]
            out = set()
            for c in [c for c in ops if c.ptr or c.mem or c.kind == "ref"] or ops:
                out |= self._pts(c, s)
            return out or {("unk",)}
        if k in ("idx", "mem", "ptr"):
            if a.mem:                             # address of an array inside memory
                return self._objs_of(a, s)
            return {("via", o) for o in self._objs_of(a, s)}   # a pointer loaded from memory
        if k == "call":
            return {("ret", a.index)}
        if k == "str":
            return {("str", a.index)}
        if k == "num":
            return {("abs", a.value)}
        if k == "asg":
            return self._pts(a.y, s)
        if k == "tern":
            return self._pts(a.y, s) | self._pts(a.z, s)
        if k == "comma":
            return self._pts(a.y, s)
        return {("unk",)}

    def _objs_of(self, lv, s):
        """Objects an lvalue lives in."""
        r = self.g._root(lv)
        if r[0] == "var":
            return {("reg", r[1])}
        if r[0] == "obj":
            return {r[1]}
        return self._pts(r[1], s)

    def _resolve(self, s):
        for a in s.mr:
            s.ouse |= self._pts(a, s)
        for a in s.mw:
            s.odef |= self._pts(a, s)
        for e in s.calls:
            name = _call_name(e)
            known = (name in WRITES or name in SCANF or name in READS
                     or (e.x is not None and e.x.kind == "helper"))
            writes = _writes(e) if known else set(range(len(e.args)))
            if name in ("strdup", "strndup", "realloc"):
                s.born.discard(("ret", e.index))  # the new block is filled from the argument
            for i, a in enumerate(e.args):
                if not (self.g._is_addr(a) or (known and i in writes)):
                    continue
                objs = self._pts(a, s)
                if not (known and i in writes and name not in _READ_TOO):
                    s.ouse |= objs                # fread's buffer is only written
                if i in writes:
                    s.odef |= objs
                    if not known:
                        s.mayw |= objs
                        s.may = True


def analyze(body):
    """The per-function part of the work, reusable for every variable of the function."""
    return _Analysis(body)


def var_flow(body, var=None, obj_ea=None, limit=200, an=None, may=False):
    """Flow of local variable number `var` (or of the global at obj_ea) in a function body.
    `an` is analyze(body), when the caller keeps it. may: also follow where an unknown call
    may have written the value (encrypt(buf, out): on to whatever reads out)."""
    an = an or _Analysis(body)
    sites = an.sites
    pointer = var is not None and var in an.g.ptrvars and var not in an.g.memvars
    if var is not None:
        keys = {("stk", var) if var in an.g.memvars else ("v", var)}
        if pointer:                         # follow what the pointer points at
            for s in sites:
                if ("v", var) in s.defs:
                    keys |= an._pts_def(s.id, var)
                elif _mentions(s, var):
                    keys |= an._pts_var(var, s)
            keys = {k for k in keys if k[0] not in ("unk", "abs")}
    else:
        keys = {("g", obj_ea)}
    addr = {("v", n) for n in an.g.ptrvars}    # addresses are followed through points-to
    writers = [s.id for s in sites if keys & s.gen()]
    readers = [s.id for s in sites if keys & s.reads()
               or (pointer and s.id not in writers and _mentions(s, var))]
    # reader -> {writer: keys whose values it reads from that writer}, and the reverse
    deps = [dict() for _ in sites]
    users = [dict() for _ in sites]
    for s in sites:
        for k in s.reads() - addr:
            for d in an.reach(s.id, k):
                if d != s.id:
                    deps[s.id].setdefault(d, set()).add(k)
                    users[d].setdefault(s.id, set()).add(k)
    back = _back(writers, deps, sites, keys)
    fwd = _forward(writers + readers, users, sites, keys, set(readers), may)
    kept = set(back) | set(fwd)
    cut = False
    if len(kept) > limit:
        near = _nearest(set(writers + readers), deps, users, limit)
        kept, cut = near, True
    role = {}
    for i in kept:
        if i in writers or i in readers:
            role[i] = "self"
        elif i in fwd and (i not in back or fwd[i] <= back[i]):
            role[i] = "dst"
        else:
            role[i] = "src"
    edges = sorted((d, r) for r in kept for d in deps[r] if d in kept and d != r)
    out = [sites[i] for i in sorted(kept)]
    return Flow(out, edges, role, any(s.may for s in out), cut, keys)


def _back(seeds, deps, sites, target):
    """Where the values come from. A site reached only for an object it creates (malloc,
    fopen), or only for memory an unknown call may write, is shown but not followed
    further: its other inputs do not make up the content."""
    def opaque(d, keys):
        return keys <= sites[d].born | sites[d].mayw
    dist = {n: 0 for n in seeds}
    heap = [(0, n) for n in seeds if not opaque(n, target & sites[n].gen())]
    done = set()
    while heap:
        c, n = heapq.heappop(heap)
        if n in done:
            continue
        done.add(n)
        for d, keys in deps[n].items():
            if c + _cost(keys) < dist.get(d, 1e9):
                dist[d] = c + _cost(keys)
                if not opaque(d, keys):
                    heapq.heappush(heap, (dist[d], d))
    return dist


# memory of the function's own (a global, a stack buffer, a block it allocated): what an
# unknown call may write there is followed with may=True; memory reached through an argument
# (argv's strings) is not
_OWN = ("g", "stk", "ret")


def _forward(seeds, users, sites, target=None, readers=None, may=False):
    """Where the values go. A write that an unknown call only may do is not followed (with
    may: it is, into the function's own memory). From a
    site that writes the variable without reading it, only the variable is followed: what
    else it makes (fread's count) does not come from the variable."""
    dist = {n: 0 for n in seeds}
    heap = [(0, n) for n in seeds]
    only = {n for n in seeds if target is not None and readers is not None and n not in readers}
    while heap:
        c, n = heapq.heappop(heap)
        if c > dist[n]:
            continue
        for r, keys in users[n].items():
            if n in only and c == 0 and not keys & target:
                continue
            if keys <= sites[n].mayw and not (may and any(k[0] in _OWN for k in keys)):
                continue
            if c + _cost(keys) < dist.get(r, 1e9):
                dist[r] = c + _cost(keys)
                heapq.heappush(heap, (dist[r], r))
    return dist


def _cost(keys):
    """A step through a variable is exact; one through memory is per object, so it counts
    for more when a site is reached both ways."""
    return 1 if any(k[0] == "v" for k in keys) else 1.5


def _mentions(s, var):
    """The site's expression names the variable (a pointer used as an address counts)."""
    stack = [s.expr]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.kind == "var" and n.var == var:
            return True
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z, n.init, n.step))
        stack.extend(n.args)
    return False


def _closure(seeds, nxt):
    seen, stack = set(seeds), list(seeds)
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        for m in nxt[n]:
            if m not in seen:
                seen.add(m)
                stack.append(m)
    return seen


def _nearest(seeds, deps, users, limit):
    """The `limit` sites closest to the seeds, either direction."""
    seen, frontier = set(seeds), list(seeds)
    while frontier and len(seen) < limit:
        nxt = []
        for n in frontier:
            for m in list(deps[n]) + list(users[n]):
                if m not in seen and len(seen) < limit:
                    seen.add(m)
                    nxt.append(m)
        frontier = nxt
    return seen


def flow_graph(flow):
    """What to draw for a Flow: (site ids, edges). Only edges that go forward in program
    order are kept (a loop feeding its own start again would bend back across the picture),
    and an edge is dropped when a longer path already connects the same two sites, so the
    drawing settles into clear layers: sources above, the variable, destinations below."""
    ids = [s.id for s in flow.sites if s.role != "join"]
    keep = set(ids)
    succ = {n: set() for n in ids}
    for a, b in flow.edges:
        if a in keep and b in keep and a < b:
            succ[a].add(b)
    edges = []
    for a in ids:
        for b in sorted(succ[a]):
            stack = [c for c in succ[a] if c != b and c < b]
            seen = set(stack)
            longer = False
            while stack:
                c = stack.pop()
                if c == b:
                    longer = True
                    break
                for d in succ[c]:
                    if d not in seen and d <= b:
                        seen.add(d)
                        stack.append(d)
            if not longer:
                edges.append((a, b))
    return ids, edges

```

`src/ibr/analysis/expr.py`:

```py
"""Expressions of the statement tree as C text and as math, for the flowchart.

c_text writes an expression the way Hex-Rays does (printf-family calls with only the
arguments their format reads). Terms.norm turns one into a math term: MMX/SSE intrinsic
chains folded the way the notes fold them (_m_pxor is ^, a shift pair is a rotation), with
the lane width where there is no carry between lanes. _wrap breaks a long line the way
Hex-Rays does. Nothing here imports IDA."""
import re

from ..core import simd
from ..core.i18n import T
from ..core.names import resolve_variant
from ..core.notes import _CONV, _FMT_FUNCS
from .dataflow import READS, SCANF, WRITES, _call_name, _strip, _writes

# calls a value reaching them says nothing about
_QUIET = {"free", "fclose", "close", "munmap", "fflush", "fseek", "rewind", "setvbuf", "setbuf",
          "ftell", "stack_chk_fail", "emms"}
# library calls whose result is computed from their arguments (others return a count, a
# handle, a status: nothing to follow)
_PURE = set("""strlen strnlen strcmp strncmp memcmp strcasecmp strncasecmp bcmp atoi atol atoll
strtol strtoul strtoll strtoull strtod strtof atof toupper tolower abs labs llabs htonl htons
ntohl ntohs strchr strrchr strstr memchr strspn strcspn""".split())
# where data leaves the program: the argument positions that carry it
_OUT = {"puts": (0,), "fputs": (0,), "putchar": (0,), "putc": (0,), "fputc": (0,), "write": (1,),
        "send": (1,), "sendto": (1,), "fwrite": (0,), "puts_unlocked": (0,)}
_BITWISE = {"^", "&", "|", "~"}
_PREC = {"||": 4, "&&": 5, "|": 6, "^": 7, "&": 8, "==": 9, "!=": 9, "<": 10, "<=": 10, ">": 10,
         ">=": 10, "<<": 11, ">>": 11, "<<<": 11, ">>>": 11, "+": 12, "-": 12, "*": 13, "/": 13,
         "%": 13}
WIDTH = 60                      # a line is wrapped beyond this many characters


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- C text of a tree
def _num_text(t):
    s = t.text or (str(t.value) if 0 <= t.value < 10 else "0x%X" % t.value)
    m = re.fullmatch(r"(-?(?:0x[0-9A-Fa-f]+|\d+))(?:u?i64|ui64|[uU]?[lL]{0,2}|[lL]{1,2}[uU]?)", s)
    return m.group(1) if m else s


def _num_value(text):
    try:
        return int(text, 0)
    except ValueError:
        return None


def _cstr(s, limit=40):
    out = []
    for ch in s or "":
        o = ord(ch)
        out.append({"\n": "\\n", "\t": "\\t", "\r": "\\r", '"': '\\"', "\\": "\\\\"}.get(ch)
                   or (ch if 32 <= o < 127 or o > 160 else "\\x%02X" % (o & 0xFF)))
    t = "".join(out)
    return '"%s"' % (t if len(t) <= limit else t[:limit - 1] + "…")


def _c(e):
    """(C text, precedence) of an expression, casts left out."""
    e = _strip(e)
    if e is None:
        return "", 16
    k = e.kind
    if k == "var":
        return e.name or "v%d" % e.var, 16
    if k == "obj":
        if e.strlit:
            return _cstr(e.string), 16
        return e.text or e.name or "%X" % (e.obj_ea or 0), 16
    if k == "helper":
        return e.name, 16
    if k == "num":
        t = _num_text(e)
        return t, 14 if t.startswith("-") else 16
    if k == "fnum":
        return e.text or "float", 16
    if k == "str":
        return _cstr(e.string), 16
    if k == "ref":
        return "&" + _p(e.x, 14), 14
    if k == "ptr":
        return "*" + _p(e.x, 14), 14
    if k == "idx":
        return _p(e.x, 15) + "[" + _c(e.y)[0] + "]", 15
    if k == "mem":
        return _p(e.x, 15) + e.op + (e.name or "?"), 15
    if k == "call":
        args = list(e.args)
        raw = e.x.name if e.x is not None else ""
        fa = _format_args(raw, args) if raw else None
        if fa is not None:                       # printf: the format and what it prints
            base, keep = resolve_variant(raw)
            fixed = keep if keep is not None else list(range(_FMT_FUNCS[base] + 1))
            args = [args[i] for i in [i for i in fixed if i < len(args)] + sorted(fa)]
        return _c(e.x)[0] + "(" + ", ".join(_c(a)[0] for a in args) + ")", 15
    if k == "asg":
        return _p(e.x, 3) + " " + e.op + "= " + _p(e.y, 2), 2
    if k in ("inc", "dec"):
        o = "++" if k == "inc" else "--"
        return (o + _p(e.x, 14), 14) if e.op == "pre" else (_p(e.x, 15) + o, 15)
    if k == "bin":
        q = _PREC.get(e.op, 12)
        return _pb(e.x, q, e.op) + " " + e.op + " " + _pb(e.y, q + 1, e.op), q
    if k == "un":
        return e.op + _p(e.x, 14), 14
    if k == "tern":
        return _p(e.x, 4) + " ? " + _c(e.y)[0] + " : " + _p(e.z, 3), 3
    if k == "comma":
        return _c(e.x)[0] + ", " + _c(e.y)[0], 1
    return "…", 16


def _p(e, prec):
    t, q = _c(e)
    return t if q >= prec else "(" + t + ")"


_CLEAR = {"<<", ">>", "&", "|", "^"}


def _pb(e, prec, op):
    """An operand of a binary operator: a shift or a bitwise operation mixed with another
    operator is bracketed even where C would not need it, as Hex-Rays does: (n - 1) >> 3."""
    t, q = _c(e)
    c = _strip(e)
    if q < prec or (c is not None and c.kind == "bin" and c.op != op and q < 14
                    and (op in _CLEAR or c.op in _CLEAR)):
        return "(" + t + ")"
    return t


def c_text(e):
    """An expression as C, without casts."""
    return _c(e)[0]


def _strconst(a):
    """The text of a string literal an address points into, or None."""
    a = _strip(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if a.kind == "str":
        return _cstr(a.string)
    if a.kind == "obj" and a.strlit:
        return _cstr(a.string)
    if a.kind == "ref" and a.x is not None:
        b = _strip(a.x)
        if b.kind == "obj" and b.strlit:
            return _cstr(b.string)
        if b.kind == "idx" and _strip(b.x) is not None and _strip(b.x).kind == "obj" and \
                _strip(b.x).strlit and _strip(b.y) is not None and _strip(b.y).kind == "num":
            return _cstr(_strip(b.x).string[_strip(b.y).value:])
    if a.kind == "bin" and a.op == "+":
        b, n = _strip(a.x), _strip(a.y)
        if b is not None and n is not None and n.kind == "num" and b.kind in ("obj", "str") and \
                (b.kind == "str" or b.strlit):
            return _cstr(b.string[n.value:])
    return None


def _format_args(raw, args):
    """The argument positions a printf-family call reads through its format: Hex-Rays' extra
    guessed variadic arguments are not data."""
    base, keep = resolve_variant(raw)
    fi = _FMT_FUNCS.get(base)
    if fi is None or base.endswith("scanf"):
        return None
    pos = keep[fi] if keep is not None and fi < len(keep) else fi
    if pos >= len(args):
        return set()
    f = _strip(args[pos])
    if f is None or not (f.kind == "str" or (f.kind == "obj" and f.strlit)):
        return set(range(pos + 1, len(args)))
    out, k = set(), 0
    for m in _CONV.finditer(f.string):
        p, wid, prec, conv = m.groups()
        if conv in ("%", "m"):
            continue
        for star in (wid, prec):
            if star and star.startswith("*"):
                k += 1
        i = int(p) - 1 if p else k
        out.add(pos + 1 + i)
        if not p:
            k += 1
    return {i for i in out if i < len(args)}


def _data_args(raw, name, e):
    """Which arguments of a call carry data into it (None: all of them)."""
    if name in _PURE:
        return None
    fa = _format_args(raw, e.args)
    if fa is not None:
        return fa
    if name in _OUT:
        return set(_OUT[name])
    if name in WRITES or name in SCANF or name in READS or name.startswith("unknown_libname"):
        return set()                              # sizes, handles, flags
    return None                                   # a function of the program: everything


def _show(lv):
    """An lvalue the way the notes write it: `v28->m64_u64` is `*v28`."""
    return simd._vec_operand(c_text(lv))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- math terms
class _K:
    """A constant, or anything else that is only its text."""
    def __init__(self, text, value=None):
        self.text, self.value = text, value


class _S:
    """A value read: the variables or memory (keys) it comes from."""
    def __init__(self, keys, text, tree):
        self.keys, self.text, self.tree = keys, text, tree


class _Op:
    """kind "bin" x sym y, "un" sym x, "rot" x sym n, "fn" name(args), "tern" a ? b : c.
    lane: (lane bits, register bits) of a vector operation."""
    def __init__(self, kind, sym, args, lane=None):
        self.kind, self.sym, self.args, self.lane = kind, sym, args, lane


def _mtext(m, prec=0):
    if isinstance(m, (_K, _S)):
        t = m.text
        q = 0 if simd._top_ops(t) else 14 if t.startswith(("-", "*", "&", "~", "!")) else 16
        return t if q >= prec else "(" + t + ")"
    if m.kind == "bin" or m.kind == "rot":
        q = _PREC.get(m.sym, 12)
        t = "%s %s %s" % (_mtext(m.args[0], q), m.sym, _mtext(m.args[1], q + 1))
    elif m.kind == "un":
        q, t = 14, m.sym + _mtext(m.args[0], 14)
    elif m.kind == "tern":
        q = 3
        t = "%s ? %s : %s" % (_mtext(m.args[0], 4), _mtext(m.args[1]), _mtext(m.args[2], 3))
    else:
        q, t = 16, "%s(%s)" % (m.sym, ", ".join(_mtext(a) for a in m.args))
    return t if q >= prec else "(" + t + ")"


def _lane_tag(m):
    """` (per byte)` for an arithmetic vector operation on lanes narrower than the register:
    there is no carry between them. Bitwise operations need no tag."""
    if not m.lane or m.sym in _BITWISE:
        return ""
    bits, reg = m.lane
    if not bits or bits >= reg:
        return ""
    return T(" (per byte)", " (每字节)") if bits == 8 else T(" (per %d-bit)" % bits, " (每%d位)" % bits)


def _rot(sym, a, b, lane):
    """(x << n) | (x >> w-n) on w-bit values is a rotation; also with ^ or +."""
    if sym not in ("|", "^", "+"):
        return None
    for l, r in ((a, b), (b, a)):
        if not (isinstance(l, _Op) and isinstance(r, _Op) and l.kind == r.kind == "bin"
                and l.sym == "<<" and r.sym == ">>"):
            continue
        n, k = l.args[1], r.args[1]
        if not (isinstance(n, _K) and isinstance(k, _K) and n.value and k.value):
            continue
        if _mtext(l.args[0]) != _mtext(r.args[0]):
            continue
        w = n.value + k.value
        ln = l.lane or r.lane
        if (ln and ln[0] == w) or (not ln and w in (8, 16, 32, 64)):
            return _Op("rot", "<<<", [l.args[0], n], ln)
    return None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- an expression as a term
class Terms:
    """An expression of the statement tree as a math term: what it reads, the operations,
    intrinsic chains folded. `an` is the data-flow analysis of the function."""

    def __init__(self, an):
        self.an, self.g = an, an.g

    # ---- what an expression reads ----
    def _objs(self, addr, s):
        return {o for o in self.an._pts(addr, s) if o[0] not in ("unk", "abs")}

    def _read_keys(self, e, s):
        if e.mem:
            return set()                          # an array or a structure stands for its address
        if e.kind == "var":
            if e.var in self.g.memvars:
                return {("stk", e.var)}
            return set() if (e.var in self.g.ptrvars or e.ptr) else {("v", e.var)}
        if e.kind == "obj":
            return set() if e.ptr else {("g", e.obj_ea)}
        if e.ptr:
            return set()                          # a pointer loaded from memory: an address
        r = self.g._root(e)
        if r[0] == "var":
            return {("v", r[1])}
        if r[0] == "obj":
            return {r[1]}
        return self._objs(r[1], s)

    def norm(self, e, s):
        e = _strip(e)
        if e is None:
            return _K("?")
        k = e.kind
        if k == "num":
            t = _num_text(e)
            return _K(t, _num_value(t))
        if k in ("fnum", "str", "ref", "helper") or (k == "obj" and e.strlit):
            return _K(c_text(e))
        if k in ("var", "obj", "ptr", "idx", "mem"):
            keys = self._read_keys(e, s)
            return _S(keys, _show(e), e) if keys else _K(_show(e))
        if k in ("inc", "dec"):
            return self.norm(e.x, s)
        if k == "asg":
            if e.op:
                return _Op("bin", e.op, [self.norm(e.x, s), self.norm(e.y, s)])
            return self.norm(e.y, s)
        if k == "comma":
            return self.norm(e.y, s)
        if k == "call":
            return self._call(e, s)
        if k == "bin":
            a, b = self.norm(e.x, s), self.norm(e.y, s)
            return _rot(e.op, a, b, None) or _Op("bin", e.op, [a, b])
        if k == "un":
            return _Op("un", e.op, [self.norm(e.x, s)])
        if k == "tern":
            return _Op("tern", "?:", [self.norm(c, s) for c in (e.x, e.y, e.z)])
        return _K("…")

    def _arg(self, a, s, written=False):
        """A call argument: what an address points at is what the call reads."""
        if written:
            return _K(c_text(a))
        lit = _strconst(a)
        if lit is not None:
            return _K(lit)
        if self.g._is_addr(a):
            b = _strip(a)
            if b.kind == "ref":
                return self.norm(b.x, s)
            objs = self._objs(a, s)
            return _S(objs, c_text(a), a) if objs else _K(c_text(a))
        return self.norm(a, s)

    def _call(self, e, s):
        raw = e.x.name if e.x is not None else ""
        if e.x is not None and e.x.kind == "helper":
            r = self._intrinsic(raw, e, s)
            if r is not None:
                return r
        ws = _writes(e)
        data = _data_args(raw, _call_name(e), e)
        return _Op("fn", c_text(e.x) or raw, [self._arg(a, s, i in ws or (data is not None and i not in data))
                                             for i, a in enumerate(e.args)])

    def _intrinsic(self, raw, e, s):
        h = raw.lstrip("_")
        if simd._V_ZERO.match(h):
            return _K("0", 0)
        if e.args and simd._V_SCALAR.match(h):
            return self.norm(e.args[0], s)
        if len(e.args) == 1 and simd._V_LOAD.match(h):
            a = _strip(e.args[0])
            lit = _strconst(a)
            if lit is not None:
                return _K(lit)
            if a.kind == "ref":
                return self.norm(a.x, s)
            objs = self._objs(a, s)
            t = "*" + _p(a, 14)
            return _S(objs, t, a) if objs else _K(t)
        m = re.fullmatch(r"__RO([LR])(\d)__", raw)
        if m and len(e.args) == 2:
            w = int(m.group(2)) * 8
            return _Op("rot", "<<<" if m.group(1) == "L" else ">>>",
                       [self.norm(e.args[0], s), self.norm(e.args[1], s)], (w, w))
        vo = simd._vec_op(h)
        if not vo or len(e.args) < 2:
            return None
        op, lane = vo
        bits = simd._LANE_UNIT.get(lane, (0,))[0]
        ln = (bits, simd._reg_bits(h)) if bits else None
        a, b = self.norm(e.args[0], s), self.norm(e.args[1], s)
        if op == "andnot":
            return _Op("bin", "&", [_Op("un", "~", [a]), b], ln)
        sym = simd._INFIX.get(op)
        if not sym or (op in ("sll", "slli", "srl", "srli") and lane in ("128b", "256b", "512b")):
            return None                            # byte shifts, min/max, shuffles: as calls
        if op == "xor" and _mtext(a) == _mtext(b):
            return _K("0", 0)
        return _rot(sym, a, b, ln) or _Op("bin", sym, [a, b], ln)

    # ---- which nodes a read comes from ----


    # ---- one statement ----


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- long lines
def _split_top(t, seps):
    """Positions in t (outside strings and brackets) where one of seps starts."""
    out = []
    for i, c, st in simd._scan(t):
        if not st:
            for sep in seps:
                if t.startswith(sep, i):
                    out.append((i, sep))
    return out


def _wrap(head, inner, tail):
    """`head inner tail` on one line, or broken the way Hex-Rays breaks a long condition
    (before && / ||) or a long call (after a comma)."""
    one = head + inner + tail
    if len(one) <= WIDTH:
        return [one]
    cuts = [i for i, _ in _split_top(inner, (" && ", " || "))]
    lines = []
    if cuts:
        start = 0
        for i in cuts:
            lines.append(inner[start:i])
            start = i + 1
        lines.append(inner[start:])
        out = [head + lines[0]] + ["  " + l for l in lines[1:]]
        out[-1] += tail
        return out
    parts, start = [], 0
    for i, sep in _split_top(inner, (", ",)):
        parts.append(inner[start:i + 1])
        start = i + 2
    parts.append(inner[start:])
    out, cur = [], head
    for p in parts:
        if len(cur) + len(p) + 1 > WIDTH and cur.strip():
            out.append(cur.rstrip())
            cur = "    "
        cur += p + " "
    out.append(cur.rstrip() + tail)
    return out

```

`src/ibr/analysis/flowchart.py`:

```py
"""What the flowchart window draws: one function as a program flowchart, the way textbooks
draw one (core/chartlayout.py places it).

Every statement in program order. Arithmetic is written as math where the notes fold it
(_m_pxor is ^, a shift pair is a rotation <<<, `(每字节)` on a per-byte add); casts and
printf's guessed extra arguments are left out. Shapes: a condition is a diamond, input and
output a parallelogram, a call of the program's own function a box with double sides, a
return a rounded end, other statements boxes (consecutive ones share a box). Statements that
say nothing about the data (fseek, fclose, free, _m_empty) are not drawn.

A line that calls one of the program's own functions remembers it (Line.call: its address and
which variable each argument is), so the window can open that function's chart inside it.

lit() picks the lines that belong to one variable: where its value comes from and goes to
(dataflow.var_flow), what makes a pointer point where it does (v28 = v19, v19 = v18,
v18 = malloc(...)), the conditions around them, and what such a condition prints or returns.
Nothing here imports IDA."""
import re

from ..core import chartlayout as cl
from ..core import simd
from ..core.i18n import T
from . import expr as ex
from .dataflow import _OWN, READS, SCANF, WRITES, _call_name, _strip

# statements that say nothing about the data
_NOISE = ex._QUIET | {"fflush"}
# where data comes in and goes out
_INPUTS = {"read", "pread", "recv", "recvfrom", "fread", "fgets", "gets", "getline", "getdelim",
           "getchar", "fgetc", "getc", "getch", "getche", "getrandom"} | set(SCANF)
_OUTPUTS = {"printf", "vprintf", "fprintf", "vfprintf", "dprintf", "puts", "fputs", "putchar",
            "putc", "fputc", "write", "send", "sendto", "fwrite", "perror", "syslog"}
_IO = re.compile(r"operator<<|operator>>|getline|istream::read|ostream::write|\bReadFile|"
                 r"\bWriteFile|\bReadConsole|\bWriteConsole|\bMessageBox|\bgets_s\b|\bscanf_s\b")


class Line:
    """One statement (or a condition, a loop's init or step) as written in the chart.
    text may hold several lines (a long call broken after a comma). site: its site in the
    analysis; ctx: the condition lines it is inside; outs: for an if, the output and return
    lines its branches start with; for a loop, those right after it (where a check loop that
    did not fail comes out: puts("Yes")). call: (address, (variable number or None for each
    argument)) of the first function of the program's own it calls, or None."""
    __slots__ = ("id", "text", "site", "ea", "box", "ctx", "outs", "loop", "call")

    def __init__(self, lid, text, site, ea, ctx, call=None):
        self.id, self.text, self.site, self.ea, self.ctx = lid, text, site, ea, ctx
        self.box, self.outs, self.loop, self.call = None, (), False, call


class Box:
    """kind: start, end, process, call, io, decision, switch, return, connector."""
    __slots__ = ("id", "kind", "lines")

    def __init__(self, bid, kind, lines):
        self.id, self.kind, self.lines = bid, kind, lines

    def callee(self):
        """The line whose call opens a function's chart here: the only one of the box's lines
        that calls a function of the program's own, or None (none, or several)."""
        calls = [line for line in self.lines if line.call is not None]
        return calls[0] if len({line.call[0] for line in calls}) == 1 else None


class Chart:
    """root: the structure for chartlayout; refs: {identifier: ("var", n), ("obj", ea) or
    ("func", ea)}; jumps: (goto's connector, its label's connector) pairs; ea: the function's
    address, when the caller sets it."""

    def __init__(self, root, boxes, lines, end, refs, jumps=()):
        self.root, self.boxes, self.lines, self.end, self.refs = root, boxes, lines, end, refs
        self.jumps = list(jumps)
        self.ea = None

    def layout(self, sizes):
        return cl.layout(self.root, sizes, end=self.end)

    def mentions(self, word):
        """The lines that name `word`, and the conditions around them."""
        rx = re.compile(r"(?<![\w])%s(?![\w])" % re.escape(word))
        out = set()
        for line in self.lines:
            if line.site is not None and rx.search(line.text):
                out.add(line.id)
                out.update(line.ctx)
        return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- text
def _folds(e):
    """e holds an intrinsic the notes fold into math."""
    stack = [e]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.kind == "call" and n.x is not None and n.x.kind == "helper":
            raw = n.x.name or ""
            h = raw.lstrip("_")
            if (simd._vec_op(h) or simd._V_LOAD.match(h) or simd._V_ZERO.match(h)
                    or simd._V_SCALAR.match(h) or re.fullmatch(r"__RO[LR]\d__", raw)):
                return True
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z))
        stack.extend(n.args)
    return False


def _tags(m):
    """The lanes of a vector expression, once at its end: ` (每字节)`."""
    tags, stack = [], [m]
    while stack:
        x = stack.pop()
        if isinstance(x, ex._Op):
            t = ex._lane_tag(x)
            if t and (x.sym, t) not in tags:
                tags.append((x.sym, t))
            stack.extend(x.args)
    if not tags:
        return ""
    if len(tags) == 1:
        return tags[0][1]
    return " (%s)" % ", ".join("%s %s" % (sym, t.strip(" ()")) for sym, t in reversed(tags))


def _call_split(t):
    """(head up to the first call's "(", its arguments, ")") when t ends with that call."""
    depth, q, start, i = 0, None, None, 0
    while i < len(t):
        c = t[i]
        if q:
            if c == "\\":
                i += 1
            elif c == q:
                q = None
        elif c in "\"'":
            q = c
        elif c == "(":
            if depth == 0 and start is None and i and (t[i - 1].isalnum() or t[i - 1] == "_"):
                start = i
            depth += 1
        elif c == ")":
            depth -= 1
            if depth == 0 and start is not None:
                return (t[:start + 1], t[start + 1:i], t[i:]) if i == len(t) - 1 else None
        i += 1
    return None


def _wrapped(t):
    """A long line broken the way Hex-Rays breaks it: a call after a comma, a condition
    before && / ||."""
    if len(t) <= ex.WIDTH:
        return t
    parts = _call_split(t)
    if parts is not None and not ex._split_top(t, (" && ", " || ")):
        return "\n".join(ex._wrap(*parts))
    return "\n".join(ex._wrap("", t, ""))


def _flat(st):
    """The statements of a block, nested blocks opened (a labelled one stays a statement)."""
    if st is None:
        return []
    if st.kind != "block":
        return [st]
    out = []
    for c in st.body:
        if c is not None and c.kind == "block" and c.label is None:
            out += _flat(c)
        elif c is not None:
            out.append(c)
    return out


def _prints(st):
    """A call with a string literal among its arguments that writes no buffer: what an
    unnamed printf looks like (sprintf and strcpy are not one)."""
    e = _strip(st.x) if st.kind == "expr" else None
    return (e is not None and e.kind == "call" and _call_name(e) not in WRITES
            and _call_name(e) not in SCANF and any(ex._strconst(a) is not None for a in e.args))


def _own_call(e):
    """(address, argument variables) of the first call of a function of the program's own in
    e, outermost first: f(g(x)) is f. An argument is a variable number when it is one, or its
    address (&buf), else None."""
    stack = [e]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop(0)
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.kind == "call" and n.x is not None and n.x.kind == "obj" and not n.x.lib \
                and n.x.obj_ea is not None:
            args = []
            for a in n.args:
                a = _strip(a)
                if a is not None and a.kind == "ref":
                    a = _strip(a.x)
                args.append(a.var if a is not None and a.kind == "var" else None)
            return n.x.obj_ea, tuple(args)
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z))
        stack.extend(n.args)
    return None


def _vars(e, out):
    stack = [e]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.kind == "var":
            out.add(n.var)
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z))
        stack.extend(n.args)
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- building
class _Builder:
    def __init__(self, an, info):
        self.an = an
        self.site = {}
        for s in an.sites:
            if s.stmt is not None and s.role != "join":
                self.site.setdefault((id(s.stmt), s.role), s)
        self.vb = ex.Terms(an)
        self.boxes, self.lines = [], []
        self.ctx = []                 # condition lines we are inside
        self.of = {}                  # id(statement) -> its line
        self.labels, self.gotos = {}, []   # label -> its connector; (goto's connector, label)

    def _line(self, text, site, ea, stmt=None, expr=None):
        line = Line(len(self.lines), text, site.id if site is not None else None, ea, tuple(self.ctx),
                    _own_call(expr) if expr is not None else None)
        self.lines.append(line)
        if stmt is not None:
            self.of[id(stmt)] = line
        return line

    def _box(self, kind, lines):
        b = Box(len(self.boxes), kind, list(lines))
        for line in lines:
            line.box = b.id
        self.boxes.append(b)
        return b.id

    # ---- text ----
    def _math(self, e, site):
        if site is not None and _folds(e):
            try:
                m = self.vb.norm(e, site)
                return ex._mtext(m) + _tags(m)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return ex.c_text(e)

    def _text(self, e, site):
        e = _strip(e)
        if e is None:
            return "…"
        if e.kind == "asg":
            rhs = self._math(e.y, site)
            return "%s %s= %s" % (ex._show(e.x), e.op, rhs) if e.op else "%s = %s" % (ex._show(e.x), rhs)
        return self._math(e, site)

    def _class(self, st):
        """noise, call (a function of the program), io, or process."""
        e = _strip(st.x)
        call = None
        if e is not None and e.kind == "call":
            call = e
        elif e is not None and e.kind == "asg" and _strip(e.y) is not None and _strip(e.y).kind == "call":
            call = _strip(e.y)
        if call is None or call.x is None:
            return "process"
        if call.x.kind == "helper":
            return "noise" if re.search(r"m_empty|emms", call.x.name or "") else "process"
        name = _call_name(call)
        if call is e and name in _NOISE:
            return "noise"
        shown = ex.c_text(call.x)
        if name in _INPUTS or name in _OUTPUTS or _IO.search(shown):
            return "io"
        if call.x.kind == "obj" and not call.x.lib:
            return "call"
        return "process"

    # ---- statements ----
    def seq(self, st):
        items, group, gkind = [], [], None

        def flush():
            nonlocal group, gkind
            if group:
                items.append(cl.Leaf(self._box(gkind, group)))
            group, gkind = [], None

        for c in _flat(st):
            if c.label is not None:
                flush()
                box = self._box("connector", [self._line(str(c.label), None, c.ea)])
                self.labels[c.label] = box
                items.append(cl.Leaf(box))
            k = c.kind
            if k == "expr":
                kind = self._class(c)
                if kind == "noise":
                    continue
                site = self.site.get((id(c), "stmt"))
                line = self._line(_wrapped(self._text(c.x, site)), site, c.ea, c, c.x)
                if kind == "call":
                    flush()
                    items.append(cl.Leaf(self._box("call", [line])))
                    continue
                if kind != gkind:
                    flush()
                    gkind = kind
                group.append(line)
                continue
            flush()
            if k == "block":
                items.append(self.seq(c))
            elif k == "return":
                site = self.site.get((id(c), "return"))
                text = "return" + (" " + self._text(c.x, site) if c.x is not None else "")
                items.append(cl.Stop(self._box("return", [self._line(_wrapped(text), site, c.ea, c, c.x)])))
            elif k == "if":
                items.append(self._if(c))
            elif k in ("while", "for"):
                items.append(self._while(c))
            elif k == "do":
                items.append(self._do(c))
            elif k == "switch":
                items.append(self._switch(c))
            elif k == "break":
                items.append(cl.Break())
            elif k == "continue":
                items.append(cl.Continue())
            elif k == "goto":
                box = self._box("connector", [self._line(str(c.target), None, c.ea, c)])
                self.gotos.append((box, c.target))
                items.append(cl.Stop(box))
            else:
                items.append(cl.Leaf(self._box("process", [self._line("…", None, c.ea, c)])))
        flush()
        self._after_loops(_flat(st))
        return cl.Seq(items)

    def _out_line(self, c):
        ln = self.of.get(id(c))
        if ln is not None and (c.kind == "return" or self.boxes[ln.box].kind == "io" or _prints(c)):
            return ln
        return None

    def _after_loops(self, flat):
        for i, c in enumerate(flat):
            if c.kind in ("while", "for", "do") and id(c) in self.of:
                after = []
                for d in flat[i + 1:]:
                    ln = self._out_line(d)
                    if ln is None:
                        if d.kind == "expr" and self._class(d) == "noise":
                            continue
                        break
                    after.append(ln.id)
                self.of[id(c)].outs = tuple(after)

    def _cond(self, st, e, kind="decision"):
        site = self.site.get((id(st), "switch" if kind == "switch" else "cond"))
        text = self._text(e, site) if e is not None else "1"
        line = self._line(_wrapped(text), site, st.ea, expr=e)
        return line, self._box(kind, [line])

    def _inside(self, line, st):
        self.ctx.append(line.id)
        try:
            return self.seq(st)
        finally:
            self.ctx.pop()

    def _outs(self, line, branches):
        out = []
        for b in branches:
            for c in _flat(b):
                ln = self._out_line(c)
                if ln is not None:
                    out.append(ln.id)
        line.outs = tuple(out)

    def _if(self, c):
        line, box = self._cond(c, c.x)
        yes = self._inside(line, c.body[0]) if c.body else None
        no = self._inside(line, c.body[1]) if len(c.body) > 1 else None
        self._outs(line, c.body)
        return cl.If(box, yes, no)

    def _while(self, c):
        init = None
        if c.kind == "for" and c.init is not None:
            site = self.site.get((id(c), "init"))
            init = self._box("process", [self._line(_wrapped(self._text(c.init, site)), site, c.ea,
                                                    expr=c.init)])
        line, box = self._cond(c, c.x)
        self.of[id(c)] = line
        line.loop = True
        body = self._inside(line, c.body[0] if c.body else None)
        step = None
        if c.kind == "for" and c.step is not None:
            site = self.site.get((id(c), "step"))
            self.ctx.append(line.id)
            step = self._box("process", [self._line(_wrapped(self._text(c.step, site)), site, c.ea,
                                                    expr=c.step)])
            self.ctx.pop()
        x = _strip(c.x)
        forever = x is None or (x.kind == "num" and x.value != 0)
        loop = cl.While(box, body, step, forever)
        return cl.Seq([cl.Leaf(init), loop]) if init is not None else loop

    def _do(self, c):
        # the condition's line is made first so the body's lines know they are inside it
        site = self.site.get((id(c), "cond"))
        line = self._line(_wrapped(self._text(c.x, site)), site, c.ea, expr=c.x)
        self.of[id(c)] = line
        line.loop = True
        body = self._inside(line, c.body[0] if c.body else None)
        return cl.DoWhile(body, self._box("decision", [line]))

    def _switch(self, c):
        line, box = self._cond(c, c.x, "switch")
        cases, default = [], False
        for case in c.body:
            vals = list(getattr(case, "values", None) or [])
            if not vals:
                default = True
                lab = "default"
            else:
                shown = ", ".join(_case_value(v) for v in vals[:4]) + (", …" if len(vals) > 4 else "")
                lab = "case " + shown
            cases.append((lab, self._inside(line, case)))
        return cl.Switch(box, cases, default)


def _case_value(v):
    return str(v) if -10 < v < 10 else "0x%X" % (v & 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF)


def _refs(body):
    """{identifier: ("var", n), ("obj", ea) or ("func", ea)} of what a double-click opens: a
    variable or a global is followed, a called function is gone to."""
    out, stack = {}, [body]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        if n is None:
            continue
        if n.kind == "call" and n.x is not None and n.x.kind == "obj" and n.x.obj_ea is not None:
            out.setdefault(ex.c_text(n.x), ("func", n.x.obj_ea))
            stack.extend(n.args)
            continue
        if n.kind == "var" and n.name:
            out.setdefault(n.name, ("var", n.var))
        elif n.kind == "obj" and n.decl and not n.strlit and n.obj_ea is not None:
            out.setdefault(ex.c_text(n), ("obj", n.obj_ea))
        stack.extend((n.x, n.y, n.z, n.init, n.step))
        stack.extend(n.args)
        stack.extend(n.body)
    return out


def build_chart(body, an, info=None, title=""):
    """The chart of a function body; an = dataflow.analyze(body); title: the start shape's
    text (the function and its arguments)."""
    b = _Builder(an, info or {})
    start = b._box("start", [b._line(title or T("Start", "开始"), None, None)])
    root = b.seq(body)
    root.items.insert(0, cl.Leaf(start))
    end = b._box("end", [b._line(T("End", "结束"), None, None)])
    jumps = [(box, b.labels[t]) for box, t in b.gotos if t in b.labels]
    return Chart(root, b.boxes, b.lines, end, _refs(body), jumps)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- one variable
def _provenance(an, var, copied=None):
    """Sites that make pointer `var` point where it does; `copied` (a set) gets the pointers
    it is copied from (v28 = v19, v19 = v18)."""
    out, todo, seen = set(), [var], set()
    while todo:
        v = todo.pop()
        if v in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(v)
        for s in an.sites:
            if ("v", v) in s.defs and s.id in an.g.live:
                out.add(s.id)
                rhs = s.vrhs.get(v)
                if rhs is not None and not isinstance(rhs, tuple):
                    todo += [u for u in _vars(rhs, set()) if u in an.g.ptrvars and u not in seen]
    if copied is not None:
        copied.update(seen - {var})
    return out


def _fed(an, sites, keys):
    """Where an unknown call that may write the variable gets its other inputs from
    (sub_140011019(buf, Str): the getline that filled Str). One step only."""
    out = set()
    for sid in sites:
        s = an.sites[sid]
        if not s.mayw & keys:
            continue
        for k in s.reads() - keys:
            if k[0] == "v" and k[1] in an.g.ptrvars:
                continue                          # an address, not what is there
            out.update(w for w in an.reach(sid, k) if w != sid)
    return out


def lit(chart, an, flow, var=None):
    """The line ids that belong to the followed variable (flow = var_flow for it, with
    may=True: past calls that may write it)."""
    sites = {s.id for s in flow.sites if s.role != "join"}
    if var is not None and var in an.g.ptrvars:
        sites |= _provenance(an, var)
    sites |= _fed(an, sites, flow.keys)
    direct = {line.id for line in chart.lines if line.site is not None and line.site in sites}
    out = set(direct)
    for i in direct:
        line = chart.lines[i]
        out.update(line.ctx)
        out.update(line.outs)
        if line.outs:                             # a check: where its loop comes out too
            for c in line.ctx:
                if chart.lines[c].loop:
                    out.update(chart.lines[c].outs)
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the names around it
def _known(s):
    """Every call at the site is one whose writes are known (fread, memcpy, an intrinsic)."""
    return all(_call_name(c) in WRITES or _call_name(c) in SCANF or _call_name(c) in READS
               or (c.x is not None and c.x.kind == "helper") for c in s.calls)


def _writes_of(an, s):
    """(what site s writes as data, the part a known call fills). A variable assigned counts,
    a pointer does not (its value is an address); memory stored to counts, a new block
    (malloc) does not, nor what an unknown call only may write outside the function's own
    memory."""
    data = {k for k in s.defs | s.wdefs if k[1] not in an.g.ptrvars}
    filled = set()
    if s.calls and _known(s):
        for c in s.calls:
            for a in c.args:
                if an.g._is_addr(a):
                    filled |= an._pts(a, s)
    for k in s.odef - s.born:
        if k in s.mayw and k[0] not in _OWN:
            continue
        data.add(k)
    return data, filled & data


def marks(chart, an, flow, var=None):
    """around(...)'s names only."""
    return around(chart, an, flow, var)[0]


def merge_marks(a, b):
    """The names of two around(...) results on one chart (two arguments followed at once)."""
    out = {lid: dict(m) for lid, m in a.items()}
    for lid, m in b.items():
        mine = out.setdefault(lid, {})
        for n, k in m.items():
            mine[n] = k if mine.get(n) in (None, k) else "both"
    return out


def _drawn_order(chart):
    """{line id: its place from the top as drawn}: a do loop's condition after its body, a
    for loop's step after its body."""
    order = {}

    def box(b):
        if b is not None:
            for line in chart.boxes[b].lines:
                order.setdefault(line.id, len(order))

    def walk(n):
        if isinstance(n, cl.Seq):
            for x in n.items:
                walk(x)
        elif isinstance(n, (cl.Leaf, cl.Stop)):
            box(n.box)
        elif isinstance(n, cl.If):
            box(n.box)
            walk(n.yes)
            walk(n.no)
        elif isinstance(n, cl.While):
            box(n.box)
            walk(n.body)
            box(n.step)
        elif isinstance(n, cl.DoWhile):
            walk(n.body)
            box(n.box)
        elif isinstance(n, cl.Switch):
            box(n.box)
            for _, body in n.cases:
                walk(body)
    walk(chart.root)
    return order


def _from_entry(an, key):
    """The sites the value key has on entry reaches (until something assigns it)."""
    seen, stack = {0}, [0]
    while stack:
        n = stack.pop()
        for m in an.g.succ[n]:
            if m not in seen:
                seen.add(m)
                if key not in an.sites[m].defs:
                    stack.append(m)
    return seen


def around(chart, an, flow, var=None):
    """({line id: {name: "from" | "into" | "both"}}, {line id: argument positions}).

    The names, line by line: on the lines the followed value is computed from, above where it
    is assigned, the variables and globals that make it (▲); on the lines below, those computed
    from it (▼). Each is found step by step along the definitions that reach each use, a step
    from a use going up to what it reads (from) or down to what reads it (into): what a loop
    carries round to its next pass is not marked, and a name is marked on the lines where it
    takes part in the value, not wherever it appears. A line that is both (between two
    assignments of the variable: made from the first, making the second) marks its names
    "both". A pointer stands for the memory it points at: the pointer an encryption loop
    writes through is "into" there when what it writes comes from the value. Data only: the
    count handed to fread does not make fread's buffer, and a pointer copy computes nothing
    (the pointers a followed pointer is copied from are "from" where it is copied).

    The arguments: for each line that calls a function of the program's own, the arguments
    the value is handed over in: the variable itself, a value computed from it, or a pointer
    to its memory or to memory computed from it (the length passed next to a buffer is not)."""
    keys = set(flow.keys)
    kept = {s.id: s for s in flow.sites if s.role != "join"}
    ptr = an.g.ptrvars
    w = {i: _writes_of(an, s) for i, s in kept.items()}
    readers = {}
    for i, s in kept.items():
        for k in s.reads():
            readers.setdefault(k, []).append(i)
    on_lines = {}
    for line in chart.lines:
        if line.site is not None:
            on_lines.setdefault(line.site, []).append(line.id)
    drawn = _drawn_order(chart)
    pos = {i: min(drawn[lid] for lid in v) for i, v in on_lines.items() if all(lid in drawn for lid in v)}

    def above(a, b):
        """Site a is drawn above site b (or one of them is not drawn: no say)."""
        return a not in pos or b not in pos or pos[a] < pos[b]

    # forward, downwards: what each site reads that carries the value, and what it makes
    fresh = {}
    carry = {}
    for i, s in kept.items():
        c = set()
        for k in s.reads() & keys:
            if k not in fresh:
                fresh[k] = _from_entry(an, k)
            if i in fresh[k] or any(above(d, i) for d in an.reach(i, k)):
                c.add(k)                          # not only what the loop brings round
        carry[i] = c
    into, made = {}, {}
    frontier, level = {i for i, c in carry.items() if c}, 0
    while frontier and level < 64:
        level += 1
        nxt = set()
        for i in sorted(frontier):
            data, filled = w[i]
            if all(k[0] == "v" for k in carry[i]):   # a count or a flag: not what a known call fills
                data = data - filled
            made[i] = data - keys
            for k in data - keys:
                into.setdefault(k, level)
                for r in readers.get(k, ()):
                    if r != i and k not in carry[r] and above(i, r) and i in an.reach(r, k):
                        carry[r].add(k)
                        nxt.add(r)
        frontier = nxt

    # backward, upwards: what each site is asked to make, and what it makes it from
    want, src = {i: w[i][0] & keys for i in kept}, {}
    frontier, level = {i for i, m in want.items() if m}, 0
    while frontier and level < 64:
        level += 1
        nxt = set()
        for i in sorted(frontier):
            s = kept[i]
            if want[i] <= s.mayw:                     # an unknown call only may make it: not
                continue                              # followed (as dataflow._back)
            rd = {k for k in s.reads() if not (k[0] == "v" and k[1] in ptr)} - keys
            if want[i] <= w[i][1]:                    # fread's buffer: from the file, not the count
                rd = {k for k in rd if k[0] != "v"}
            src[i] = rd
            for k in rd:
                for d in an.reach(i, k):
                    if d != i and d in kept and k in w[d][0] and k not in want[d] and above(d, i):
                        want[d].add(k)
                        nxt.add(d)
        frontier = nxt

    names = {}
    for nm, ref in chart.refs.items():
        names.setdefault(ref, nm)

    def named(k, s):
        """How key k is written at site s: a variable or a global by its name, other memory
        by the pointers into it there."""
        if k[0] in ("v", "stk"):
            return [names[("var", k[1])]] if k[1] != var and ("var", k[1]) in names else []
        if k[0] == "g":
            return [names[("obj", k[1])]] if ("obj", k[1]) in names else []
        out = []
        for p in _vars(s.expr, set()):
            if p in ptr and p != var and ("var", p) in names and k in an._pts_var(p, s):
                out.append(names[("var", p)])
        return out

    out = {}

    def put(i, ks, kind):
        s = an.sites[i]
        for k in ks:
            for n in named(k, s):
                for lid in on_lines.get(i, ()):
                    m = out.setdefault(lid, {})
                    m[n] = kind if m.get(n) in (None, kind) else "both"

    for i, c in carry.items():
        if c:
            put(i, (c - keys) | made.get(i, set()), "into")
    for i, rd in src.items():
        put(i, (want[i] - keys) | rd, "from")
    if var is not None and var in ptr:
        copied = set()
        for i in _provenance(an, var, copied):
            s = an.sites[i]
            for p in _vars(s.expr, set()) & copied:
                if ("var", p) in names:
                    for lid in on_lines.get(i, ()):
                        out.setdefault(lid, {})[names[("var", p)]] = "from"

    data = keys | set(into)
    args = {}
    for line in chart.lines:
        if line.call is None:
            continue
        s = an.sites[line.site] if line.site is not None else None
        at = []
        for i, n in enumerate(line.call[1]):
            if n is None:
                continue
            if ("v", n) in data or ("stk", n) in data or (
                    s is not None and n in ptr and an._pts_var(n, s) & data):
                at.append(i)
        if at:
            args[line.id] = tuple(at)
    return out, args

```
