`src/ibr/app/annotate.py`:

```py
"""Comments the plugin writes into the database (all journaled, so Undo reverts them)."""
from ..core.asm_math import insn_math
from ..core.comments import OPX, PFX, _JOIN, _strip_managed
from ..core.libc import _describe


def annotate_all(prog, journal):
    """One-line description on every recognised libc function. Returns the count."""
    n = 0
    for ea in prog.functions():
        name = prog.func_name(ea) or ""
        d = _describe(name)
        if not d:
            continue
        old = prog.func_cmt(ea) or ""
        body = _strip_managed(old)
        new = d if not body else (d + "\n" + body)
        if new == old:
            continue
        try:
            prog.set_func_cmt(ea, new); n += 1
            journal.record("cmts", ea, old, new)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return n


def annotate_math(prog, journal, func_ea):
    """`d = s op t` notes on the instructions of the function at func_ea.
    Returns the count, or -1 when func_ea is not in a function."""
    start = prog.func_start(func_ea)
    if start is None:
        return -1
    n = 0
    arch = prog.arch() or ""
    for ea in prog.func_items(start):
        expr = insn_math(*prog.insn(ea), arch)
        if not expr:
            continue
        tagged = OPX + expr
        old = prog.insn_cmt(ea) or ""
        body = _strip_managed(old)
        new = tagged if not body else (tagged + _JOIN + body)
        if new == old:
            continue
        try:
            prog.set_insn_cmt(ea, new); n += 1
            journal.record("icmts", ea, old, new)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return n


__all__ = ["annotate_all", "annotate_math", "PFX"]

```

`src/ibr/app/detection.py`:

```py
"""Detect the runtime of the program loaded in the database."""
from ..core.detect import detect
from ..core.fingerprint import _present_in_buffer, _scan_window
from ..core.i18n import T


def _present_hook(prog):
    """Scan the loaded executable segments for anchors (database-local).
       Falls back to per-function windows if the image can't be read in bulk."""
    def hook(index):
        buf = prog.image_bytes()
        if buf:
            return _present_in_buffer(buf, index)
        present = set()
        for w in prog.function_heads(32):
            if w:
                _scan_window(w, index, present)
        return present
    return hook


def run_detection(prog):
    """(result, None) or (None, error text)."""
    arch = prog.arch()
    data = prog.input_bytes()
    try:
        if data is not None:
            # IDA can load an ELF yet create no segments (e.g. tampered EI_CLASS):
            # then scan the file on disk instead of an empty database
            if not prog.has_code():
                r = detect(data=data, arch_hint=arch)
                r.setdefault("notes", []).append(T(
                    "IDA loaded no code from this file; the file on disk was scanned instead.",
                    "IDA 没有从该文件加载出代码,已改为扫描磁盘上的原文件。"))
                return r, None
            return detect(data=data, arch_hint=arch, present_hook=_present_hook(prog)), None
        # file is gone: reconstruct enough from the database
        img = prog.header_bytes() + prog.image_bytes()
        return detect(data=img, arch_hint=arch, present_hook=_present_hook(prog)), None
    except Exception as e:
        return None, "detect error: %s" % e

```

`src/ibr/app/signatures.py`:

```py
"""Apply FLIRT signatures and prototypes for the identified libc build.

`prog` is a ports.Program, `sigs` a ports.Signatures, `journal` a core.journal.Journal."""
import os
import re

from ..core.fingerprint import _lib_label
from ..core.i18n import T
from ..core.names import _AUTONAME, _is_dummy_name, canon

# per-arch filename markers, most-preferred first
_ARCH_MARK = {"x86_64": ("amd64", "a64", "x64"), "i386": ("i386", "a32"),
              "aarch64": ("aarch64", "arm64", "arm"), "arm": ("arm32", "arm")}


def _find_sigs(sigs, verstr, arch):
    marks = _ARCH_MARK.get(arch, ())
    out = []
    for fn in sigs.sig_files(arch):
        low = fn.lower()
        if fn.endswith(".sig") and ("glibc" in low or "musl" in low) and verstr in fn:
            out.append(fn)

    def rank(s):
        low = s.lower()
        for i, m in enumerate(marks):
            if m in low:
                return (0, i, s)
        return (1, 0, s)                 # no arch marker: lowest priority
    return sorted(set(out), key=rank)


def _apply_prototypes(prog, sigs):
    """Give FLIRT-named libc functions their real prototype from the type library.
       Without it Hex-Rays guesses the arity (mmap shows up as mmap64(0)), and the
       argument decoding has nothing to work with. User-set types are left alone."""
    n = 0
    for ea in prog.functions():
        name = prog.func_name(ea) or ""
        if _is_dummy_name(name) or prog.has_user_type(ea):
            continue
        tif = None
        # the exact name first: lseek64 must keep its 64-bit off_t on 32-bit targets
        cands = [name.lstrip("_"), name]
        if not name.endswith("_chk"):     # a fortified variant must not get its base's prototype
            cands.append(canon(name))
        for cand in dict.fromkeys(cands):
            tif = sigs.prototype(cand) if cand else None
            if tif:
                break
        if tif is None:
            continue
        if prog.apply_type(ea, tif):
            n += 1
    return n


def _apply_one_sig(prog, sigs, journal, signame):
    before = {ea: (prog.func_name(ea) or "", prog.is_lib(ea), prog.func_cmt(ea) or "")
              for ea in prog.functions()}
    sigs.apply(signame)
    changed = 0
    for ea in prog.functions():
        nm = prog.func_name(ea) or ""
        old_nm, old_lib, old_cmt = before.get(ea, ("", False, ""))
        cmt = prog.func_cmt(ea) or ""
        if cmt != old_cmt:
            journal.record("cmts", ea, old_cmt, cmt)     # FLIRT writes the library name here
        if old_nm != nm:
            journal.record("names", ea, old_nm, nm)
            changed += not _AUTONAME.match(nm)
        if not old_lib and prog.is_lib(ea):
            journal.libflag.add(ea)            # FLIRT marks matches as library functions
    return changed


# glibc release -> the GCC that Ubuntu release builds with (when .comment is stripped)
_GLIBC_GCC = [("2.19", "4.8"), ("2.23", "5"), ("2.27", "7"), ("2.31", "9"), ("2.35", "11"),
              ("2.36", "12"), ("2.38", "13"), ("2.40", "14"), ("2.42", "15")]


def _numver(v):
    try:
        return tuple(int(x) for x in v.split("."))
    except Exception:
        return (0,)


def _pick_libgcc_sig(sigs, r, libc_ver):
    """Name of the libgcc_<gcc>_<arch> signature to apply, or None. The GCC version
    comes from the toolchain string, else from the glibc release's Ubuntu default."""
    suffix = {"x86_64": "amd64", "i386": "i386", "aarch64": "aarch64"}.get(r.get("arch"))
    if not suffix:
        return None
    have = {}
    for fn in sigs.sig_files(r.get("arch")):
        m = re.match(r"libgcc_([\d.]+)_%s\.sig$" % suffix, fn)
        if m:
            have[m.group(1)] = fn[:-4]
    if not have:
        return None
    want = None
    m = re.search(r"gcc (\d+)\.(\d+)", r.get("toolchain") or "", re.I)
    if m:
        want = "%s.%s" % m.groups() if m.group(1) == "4" else m.group(1)
    elif re.match(r"\d+\.\d+$", libc_ver or ""):
        for g, c in _GLIBC_GCC:
            if _numver(g) <= _numver(libc_ver):
                want = c
    if not want:
        return None
    if want in have:
        return have[want]
    w = _numver(want)
    near = min(have, key=lambda v: (abs(_numver(v)[0] - w[0]), -_numver(v)[0]))
    return have[near]


def apply_signatures(prog, sigs, journal, r):
    """Name library functions with the signatures matching detection result `r`.
    Returns the log line."""
    kind = r.get("kind"); best = r.get("best") or ""
    if not prog.has_code():
        return T("IDA loaded no code from this file, so there is nothing to name; see the detection notes.",
                 "IDA 没有从该文件加载出代码,无可命名;见识别结果里的说明。")
    if kind in ("pe", "go", "dynamic", "?"):
        return T("no static glibc/musl signature needed for this type (%s).",
                 "该类型(%s)不需要套静态 glibc/musl 签名。") % kind
    if not r.get("ranking"):
        return T("no libc build was identified (%s), so no signature is applied.",
                 "没有识别出 libc 构建(%s),不套用签名。") % best
    # on a confident call apply the leader; when uncertain, apply the top few and
    # let FLIRT keep only byte-matching functions.
    cands = [v for v, s, h, n in r["ranking"]]
    cands = cands[:1] if r.get("confidence") == "high" else cands[:3]
    arch = r.get("arch")
    sigs.ensure_til(prog.arch())
    types_before = prog.type_snapshot()   # FLIRT itself makes IDA apply some types too
    applied, total_changed = [], 0
    for ver in cands:
        verstr = _lib_label(ver).replace("glibc", "").replace("musl", "").strip().lstrip(">=")
        found = _find_sigs(sigs, verstr, arch)
        if not found:
            continue
        signame = os.path.splitext(found[0])[0]
        total_changed += _apply_one_sig(prog, sigs, journal, signame)
        applied.append(signame)
        if len(applied) == 1 and kind == "static":
            lg = _pick_libgcc_sig(sigs, r, verstr)       # the GCC runtime linked next to libc
            if lg:
                total_changed += _apply_one_sig(prog, sigs, journal, lg)
                applied.append(lg)
    if not applied:
        return T("no FLIRT .sig for %s in IDA sig/pc. Generate one with FLAIR or grab a community set.",
                 "IDA sig/pc 里没有 %s 的签名。用 FLAIR 生成或从社区签名集拿。") % best
    # FLIRT tells look-alike modules apart by the names they reference
    # (__printf_chk vs __wprintf_chk call __vfprintf_internal / __vfwprintf_internal).
    # A callee named later in the same pass leaves its caller unmatched, so apply
    # again until a pass names nothing new.
    for _ in range(2):
        more = sum(_apply_one_sig(prog, sigs, journal, s) for s in applied)
        total_changed += more
        if not more:
            break
    typed = _apply_prototypes(prog, sigs)
    types_after = prog.type_snapshot()
    for ea in set(types_before) | set(types_after):
        b, a = types_before.get(ea), types_after.get(ea)
        bs, as_ = (str(b[0]) if b else None), (str(a[0]) if a else None)
        if bs != as_:
            journal.record("types", ea, b, as_)    # b None -> the function had no type before
    named = sum(1 for ea in prog.functions() if not _AUTONAME.match(prog.func_name(ea) or ""))
    return T("applied %s; named %d functions this run (%d named in total); %d prototypes applied.",
             "已套用 %s;本次命名 %d 个函数(共 %d 个有名字);套用原型 %d 个。") % (
                 ", ".join(applied), total_changed, named, typed)

```

`src/ibr/app/undo.py`:

```py
"""Revert what the plugin wrote, keeping edits the user made since."""
from ..core.comments import _strip_managed
from ..core.i18n import T
from ..core.names import _is_dummy_name


def undo(prog, dec, journal):
    """Revert what the plugin wrote. An item the user changed after the plugin wrote it
    keeps the user's version: a renamed function or retyped prototype is left alone,
    and an edited comment only loses the plugin's notes. Returns the log line.

    `prog` is a ports.Program, `dec` a ports.Decompiler, `journal` a core.journal.Journal."""
    E = journal.entries
    nn = nc = ni = npc = nt = 0
    for ea, (old, new) in list(E["names"].items()):
        try:
            if (prog.func_name(ea) or "") != new:
                continue                          # renamed since: the user's name stays
            # an empty name lets IDA regenerate sub_XXXX
            if prog.set_func_name(ea, "" if _is_dummy_name(old) else old):
                nn += 1
        except Exception:
            pass
    for ea, (old, new) in list(E["cmts"].items()):
        try:
            cur = prog.func_cmt(ea) or ""
            prog.set_func_cmt(ea, (old or "") if cur == new else _strip_managed(cur)); nc += 1
        except Exception:
            pass
    for ea, (old, new) in list(E["icmts"].items()):
        try:
            cur = prog.insn_cmt(ea) or ""
            prog.set_insn_cmt(ea, (old or "") if cur == new else _strip_managed(cur)); ni += 1
        except Exception:
            pass
    byfunc = {}
    for (fea, ea, itp), (old, new) in E["pcmts"].items():
        byfunc.setdefault(fea, []).append(((ea, itp), old, new))
    for fea, items in byfunc.items():
        try:
            cur = dec.pseudo_comments(fea, [loc for loc, _, _ in items])
            if cur is None:
                continue
            dec.set_pseudo_comments(fea, {
                loc: (old or "") if (cur.get(loc) or "") == new else _strip_managed(cur.get(loc) or "")
                for loc, old, new in items})
            npc += len(items)
        except Exception:
            pass
    try:
        for ea, (prev, written) in list(E["types"].items()):
            if prog.type_str(ea) != written:
                continue                          # retyped since: the user's type stays
            # a type IDA had only guessed must not come back as a user type
            prog.restore_type(ea, prev)
            nt += 1
    except Exception:
        pass
    for ea in journal.libflag:
        if prog.is_lib(ea):
            prog.clear_lib(ea)
    journal.clear()
    try:
        prog.wait()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return T("reverted: %d names, %d prototypes, %d func comments, %d insn comments, %d pseudocode comments.",
             "已撤回:%d 个函数名、%d 个原型、%d 处函数注释、%d 处指令注释、%d 处伪代码注释。") % (nn, nt, nc, ni, npc)

```

`src/ibr/core/asm_math.py`:

```py
"""One-line math for arithmetic and vector instructions in the disassembly."""
import re


_BIN = {"add":"+", "sub":"-", "and":"&", "or":"|", "xor":"^", "imul":"*",
        "shl":"<<", "sal":"<<", "shr":">>", "sar":">>(signed)",
        "rol":"<<<(rot)", "ror":">>>(rot)"}
_CARRY = {"adc":"%s + %s + CF", "sbb":"%s - %s - CF"}
# ARM/AArch64/MIPS: "op d, a, b" is d = a OP b; IDA prints "op d, b" when a == d
_RISC_BIN = {"add":"+", "adds":"+", "addu":"+", "addi":"+", "addiu":"+", "daddu":"+", "daddiu":"+",
             "sub":"-", "subs":"-", "subu":"-", "dsubu":"-",
             "and":"&", "ands":"&", "andi":"&", "orr":"|", "or":"|", "ori":"|",
             "eor":"^", "xor":"^", "xori":"^", "mul":"*", "udiv":"/", "sdiv":"/(signed)",
             "lsl":"<<", "sll":"<<", "sllv":"<<", "lsr":">>", "srl":">>", "srlv":">>",
             "asr":">>(signed)", "sra":">>(signed)", "srav":">>(signed)", "ror":">>>(rot)"}
_RISC_FMT = {"bic":"%s & ~%s", "bics":"%s & ~%s", "orn":"%s | ~%s", "eon":"%s ^ ~%s",
             "nor":"~(%s | %s)"}
_RISC_UN = {"neg":"-%s", "negs":"-%s", "negu":"-%s", "mvn":"~%s", "not":"~%s"}
_SHIFT_OP = re.compile(r"^(\w+),\s*(LSL|LSR|ASR|ROR)\s*#?(\w+)$", re.I)
_SHIFT_SYM = {"lsl":"<<", "lsr":">>", "asr":">>(signed)", "ror":">>>(rot)"}
_UN = {"not":"~{d}", "neg":"-{d}", "inc":"{d} + 1", "dec":"{d} - 1"}
_VEC = {"pxor":("^",""), "pand":("&",""), "por":("|",""), "pandn":("andnot",""),
        "paddb":("+","per byte"), "paddw":("+","per 16b"), "paddd":("+","per 32b"), "paddq":("+","per 64b"),
        "psubb":("-","per byte"), "psubw":("-","per 16b"), "psubd":("-","per 32b"), "psubq":("-","per 64b"),
        "pmullw":("*","per 16b"), "pmulld":("*","per 32b"), "pmuludq":("*","per 32->64b"),
        "psllw":("<<","per 16b"), "pslld":("<<","per 32b"), "psllq":("<<","per 64b"),
        "psrlw":(">>","per 16b"), "psrld":(">>","per 32b"), "psrlq":(">>","per 64b"),
        "psraw":(">>(signed)","per 16b"), "psrad":(">>(signed)","per 32b")}
_FP = {"addss":"+","addsd":"+","subss":"-","subsd":"-","mulss":"*","mulsd":"*","divss":"/","divsd":"/"}


def _risc_opnd(o):
    o = o[1:] if o.startswith("#") else o
    sh = _SHIFT_OP.match(o)
    if sh:
        return "(%s %s %s)" % (sh.group(1), _SHIFT_SYM[sh.group(2).lower()], sh.group(3))
    return o


def _risc_math(m, d, s, t):
    if not s:
        return None
    s, t = _risc_opnd(s), _risc_opnd(t)
    if m in _RISC_UN and not t:
        return "%s = %s" % (d, _RISC_UN[m] % s)
    a, b = (s, t) if t else (d, s)
    if m in _RISC_FMT:
        return "%s = %s" % (d, _RISC_FMT[m] % (a, b))
    if m in _RISC_BIN:
        if m in ("eor", "xor") and a == b:
            return "%s = 0" % d
        sym = _RISC_BIN[m]
        if sym in "+-" and re.match(r"^-(0x)?[0-9A-F]+$", b, re.I):    # addiu $sp, -0x20
            sym, b = "-+"[sym == "-"], b[1:]
        return "%s = %s %s %s" % (d, a, sym, b)
    return None


def insn_math(m, d, s, t, arch):
    """`d = ...` for one instruction (mnemonic m, operands d, s, t as printed), or None."""
    m = (m or "").lower()
    if not m:
        return None
    if not d:
        return None
    arch = arch or ""
    if arch.startswith("mips") or arch in ("arm", "aarch64"):
        return _risc_math(m, d, s, t)
    if arch not in ("x86_64", "i386"):
        return None
    if m.startswith("v") and t:
        base = m[1:]
        if base in _VEC:
            sym, g = _VEC[base]; g = (" (%s)" % g) if g else ""
            if base == "pxor" and s == t:
                return "%s = 0" % d
            if base == "pandn":
                return "%s = (~%s) & %s" % (d, s, t)
            return "%s = %s %s %s%s" % (d, s, sym, t, g)
        if base in _FP:
            return "%s = %s %s %s" % (d, s, _FP[base], t)
        if base in _BIN:
            return "%s = %s %s %s" % (d, s, _BIN[base], t)
    if m in _VEC:
        sym, g = _VEC[m]; g = (" (%s)" % g) if g else ""
        if m == "pxor" and d == s:
            return "%s = 0" % d
        if m == "pandn":
            return "%s = (~%s) & %s" % (d, d, s)
        return "%s = %s %s %s%s" % (d, d, sym, s, g)
    if m in _FP:
        return "%s = %s %s %s" % (d, d, _FP[m], s)
    if m in _UN:
        return "%s = %s" % (d, _UN[m].format(d=d))
    if m == "imul" and t:
        return "%s = %s * %s" % (d, s or d, t)    # IDA hides the source when it is d
    if not s:
        return None
    if m in _CARRY:
        return "%s = %s" % (d, _CARRY[m] % (d, s))
    if m in _BIN:
        if m == "xor" and d == s:
            return "%s = 0" % d
        return "%s = %s %s %s" % (d, d, _BIN[m], s)
    if m == "lea":
        # lea computes an address: [base+index*k+disp] -> base+index*k+disp, sym -> &sym
        inner = re.sub(r"^\w+ ptr ", "", s)
        inner = re.sub(r"^\w+:", "", inner)
        if inner.startswith("[") and inner.endswith("]"):
            return "%s = %s" % (d, inner[1:-1])
        disp = re.match(r"^([^\[\]]+)\[([^\[\]]+)\]$", inner)   # ds:1[r8*4]
        if disp:
            off, idx = disp.groups()
            if not re.match(r"^-?[0-9A-F]+h?$", off, re.I):
                return "%s = &%s + %s" % (d, off, idx)
            return "%s = %s" % (d, idx if off == "0" else "%s+%s" % (idx, off))
        return "%s = &%s" % (d, inner)
    return None

```

`src/ibr/core/binfmt.py`:

```py
"""ELF/PE header parsing, tamper detection, packer and language markers."""
import re
import struct


EM_TO_ARCH = {3: "i386", 62: "x86_64", 183: "aarch64", 40: "arm",
              8: "mips", 10: "mips", 21: "ppc", 22: "ppc64", 243: "riscv"}
_ARCH_BITS = {"x86_64": 64, "aarch64": 64, "ppc64": 64, "i386": 32, "arm": 32, "ppc": 32}


def _elf_layout(data):
    """Return (is64, endian, tamper_note). The kernel loads x86/ARM ELFs without
       trusting EI_CLASS/EI_DATA, so CTF binaries rewrite those ident bytes to break
       file(1), readelf and disassemblers. If the declared layout yields no known
       machine but the other byte order does, trust the header fields instead."""
    if len(data) < 0x40:
        return len(data) > 4 and data[4] == 2, ">" if len(data) > 5 and data[5] == 2 else "<", None
    is64 = data[4] == 2
    en = "<" if data[5] == 1 else ">"
    declared = 64 if is64 else 32
    ok = lambda e: (EM_TO_ARCH.get(struct.unpack_from(e + "H", data, 18)[0]),
                    struct.unpack_from(e + "H", data, 16)[0] in (1, 2, 3, 4))

    def fits(bits, e):
        # e_ehsize/e_phentsize are fixed per class (64/56 or 52/32) and sit at different
        # offsets, so they tell the real class even when e_type/e_machine read fine
        off, eh, ph = (0x34, 64, 56) if bits == 64 else (0x28, 52, 32)
        a, b = struct.unpack_from(e + "HH", data, off)
        return a == eh and b in (ph, 0)

    def note(bits, e, arch):
        return ("ELF ident bytes tampered: EI_CLASS/EI_DATA claim %d-bit %s-endian, "
                "header decodes as %d-bit %s-endian %s (to load it in IDA, set file "
                "byte 4 to %d and byte 5 to %d)"
                % (declared, "little" if en == "<" else "big",
                   bits, "little" if e == "<" else "big", arch,
                   2 if bits == 64 else 1, 1 if e == "<" else 2))

    arch, typ_ok = ok(en)
    if arch and typ_ok:
        if fits(declared, en) or not fits(96 - declared, en):
            return is64, en, None
        return 96 - declared == 64, en, note(96 - declared, en, arch)     # only EI_CLASS rewritten
    for e in ("<", ">"):
        arch, typ_ok = ok(e)
        if arch and typ_ok:
            bits = 64 if fits(64, e) else 32 if fits(32, e) else _ARCH_BITS.get(arch, declared)
            return bits == 64, e, note(bits, e, arch)
    return is64, en, None


def _parse_elf_headers(data):
    """Return dict with arch, class, interp, needed count, exec byte ranges."""
    r = {"arch": None, "bits": None, "etype": None, "interp": None,
         "needed": 0, "exec_ranges": [], "machine": None, "tampered": None}
    if len(data) < 64 or data[:4] != b"\x7fELF":
        return r
    is64, en, r["tampered"] = _elf_layout(data)
    r["bits"] = 64 if is64 else 32
    r["etype"] = struct.unpack_from(en + "H", data, 16)[0]
    machine = struct.unpack_from(en + "H", data, 18)[0]
    r["machine"] = machine
    r["arch"] = EM_TO_ARCH.get(machine)
    try:
        if is64:
            phoff = struct.unpack_from(en + "Q", data, 32)[0]
            phentsize, phnum = struct.unpack_from(en + "HH", data, 54)
        else:
            phoff = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, 28)[0]
            phentsize, phnum = struct.unpack_from(en + "HH", data, 42)
        if phentsize < (56 if is64 else 32):
            phnum = 0                    # a forged entry size would re-read one entry 65535 times
        for i in range(phnum):
            off = phoff + i * phentsize
            if off + phentsize > len(data):
                break
            p_type = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, off)[0]
            if is64:
                p_flags = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, off + 4)[0]
                p_offset = struct.unpack_from(en + "Q", data, off + 8)[0]
                p_filesz = struct.unpack_from(en + "Q", data, off + 32)[0]
                dent = 16
            else:
                p_offset = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, off + 4)[0]
                p_filesz = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, off + 16)[0]
                p_flags = struct.unpack_from(en + "I", data, off + 24)[0]
                dent = 8
            if p_type == 3:                                   # PT_INTERP
                s = data[p_offset:p_offset + p_filesz].split(b"\x00", 1)[0]
                r["interp"] = s.decode("latin1", "replace")
            elif p_type == 2:                                 # PT_DYNAMIC
                for j in range(0, p_filesz, dent):
                    o = p_offset + j
                    if o + dent > len(data):
                        break
                    tag = struct.unpack_from(en + ("q" if is64 else "i"), data, o)[0]
                    if tag == 0:
                        break
                    if tag == 1:                              # DT_NEEDED
                        r["needed"] += 1
            elif p_type == 1 and (p_flags & 1):               # PT_LOAD + X
                if (p_offset, p_filesz) not in r["exec_ranges"]:
                    r["exec_ranges"].append((p_offset, p_filesz))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return r


def _comment_toolchain(data):
    """Extract the .comment producer string (GCC/clang/rustc) if present."""
    m = re.search(rb"GCC: \(([^)]*)\) ([0-9][^\x00\n]*)", data)
    if m:
        return "gcc %s (%s)" % (m.group(2).decode("latin1", "replace"),
                                m.group(1).decode("latin1", "replace"))
    m = re.search(rb"(?:Ubuntu |Debian )?clang version ([0-9][^\x00\n]{0,40})", data)
    if m:
        return "clang " + m.group(1).decode("latin1", "replace")
    return None


# map an Ubuntu gcc toolchain string to the glibc it ships (coarse cross-check)
_UBUNTU_GLIBC = [
    ("16.04", "2.23"), ("18.04", "2.27"), ("20.04", "2.31"), ("22.04", "2.35"),
    ("23.04", "2.37"), ("23.10", "2.38"), ("24.04", "2.39"), ("24.10", "2.40"),
    ("25.04", "2.41"), ("25.10", "2.42"),
]
# gcc major -> plausible glibc floor, used only when no Ubuntu tag is embedded
_GCC_GLIBC = {"9": "2.31", "10": "2.31", "11": "2.35", "12": "2.35",
              "13": "2.37", "14": "2.39", "15": "2.41"}


def _glibc_hint_from_comment(cmt):
    if not cmt:
        return None
    for tag, ver in _UBUNTU_GLIBC:
        if tag in cmt:
            return ver
    m = re.search(r"gcc (\d+)\.", cmt)
    if m:
        return _GCC_GLIBC.get(m.group(1))
    return None


# ======================================================================
#  Packer / language detection
# ======================================================================
def detect_packer(data):
    if b"UPX!" in data[:4096] or b"UPX!" in data[-4096:] or b"$Info: This file is packed with the UPX" in data:
        return "UPX (run `upx -d` first; fingerprints are meaningless while packed)"
    if b"PyInstaller" in data or b"pyi-" in data or b"_MEIPASS" in data:
        return "PyInstaller (extract with pyinstxtractor, then decompile the .pyc)"
    if b"Nuitka" in data or b"__nuitka" in data:
        return "Nuitka-compiled Python"
    if b"py2exe" in data:
        return "py2exe"
    return None


def detect_rust(data):
    m = re.search(rb"/rustc/([0-9a-f]{8,40})/", data)
    m2 = re.search(rb"rustc-(\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", data) or re.search(rb"rustc version (\d+\.\d+\.\d+)", data)
    if m or m2 or b"__rust_alloc" in data or b"RUST_BACKTRACE" in data:
        ver = m2.group(1).decode() if m2 else "unknown"
        commit = (" (rustc %s)" % m.group(1).decode()[:12]) if m else ""
        return {"lang": "rust", "version": ver, "note": "Rust program" + commit}
    return None


def detect_go(data):
    # require the buildinfo blob, not merely a "go1.x" substring
    has_buildinf = (b"\xff Go buildinf:" in data) or (b"\xfa Go buildinf:" in data)
    if not has_buildinf and b"runtime.buildVersion" not in data and b"Go build ID:" not in data:
        return None
    m = re.search(rb"go1\.\d{1,2}(?:\.\d{1,3})?", data)
    ver = m.group(0).decode() if m else "unknown"
    return {"lang": "go", "version": ver}


# ---------- Windows PE ----------
def _decode_rich(data, pe_off):
    try:
        head = data[:pe_off]
        ri = head.rfind(b"Rich")
        if ri < 0:
            return None
        key = struct.unpack("<I", head[ri + 4:ri + 8])[0]
        di = head.rfind(struct.pack("<I", 0x536E6144 ^ key), 0, ri)   # "DanS" ^ key
        if di < 0:
            return None
        comps = []
        off = di + 16
        while off + 8 <= ri:
            v = struct.unpack("<I", head[off:off + 4])[0] ^ key
            c = struct.unpack("<I", head[off + 4:off + 8])[0] ^ key
            prodid, build = v >> 16, v & 0xFFFF
            if prodid or build:
                comps.append((prodid, build, c))
            off += 8
        return comps
    except Exception:
        return None


def detect_pe(data):
    r = {"os": "windows", "arch": None, "linker": None, "rich": None, "notes": []}
    try:
        e_lfanew = struct.unpack("<I", data[0x3C:0x40])[0]
        if data[e_lfanew:e_lfanew + 4] != b"PE\x00\x00":
            r["notes"].append("MZ but PE header looks off"); return r
        machine = struct.unpack("<H", data[e_lfanew + 4:e_lfanew + 6])[0]
        r["arch"] = {0x14c: "x86", 0x8664: "x64", 0xaa64: "arm64"}.get(machine, hex(machine))
        opt = e_lfanew + 24
        r["linker"] = "%d.%d" % (data[opt + 2], data[opt + 3])
        if b"_CorExeMain" in data or b"mscoree.dll" in data or b"#~\x00" in data:
            r["notes"].append(".NET / managed PE (decompile with ILSpy / dnSpy).")
        if b"__gnu_exception_handler" in data or b"libgcc" in data or b"mingw" in data.lower():
            r["notes"].append("MinGW-compiled (GCC on Windows).")
        comps = _decode_rich(data, e_lfanew)
        if comps:
            top = max(comps, key=lambda t: t[1])
            r["rich"] = comps
            r["notes"].append("Rich header: highest build=%d (bigger = newer MSVC; cross-ref the MSVC table)." % top[1])
        else:
            r["notes"].append("no Rich header (non-MSVC toolchain, or stripped).")
        r["notes"].append("Windows library funcs: IDA's bundled MSVC/UCRT FLIRT usually names them.")
    except Exception as e:
        r["notes"].append("PE parse error: %s" % e)
    return r

```

`src/ibr/core/chartlayout.py`:

```py
"""Layout of a program flowchart, drawn the way textbooks draw one (GB/T 1526, ISO 5807):
the steps one under another in program order on one straight line, a condition's other
branch in a column to its right, a loop's way back on its left and its way out on its right,
every line horizontal or vertical.

The input is the statement structure (the classes below) with the size of every shape; the
layout follows that structure, so nothing is searched and nothing moves when a different
variable is lit. Pure Python, no IDA."""

VG = 26         # between one step and the next
VL = 34         # the same below a condition, where its exit carries "yes" / "no"
HG = 34         # between a column and the line or column beside it
J = 14          # above a loop's condition, where the way back comes in
LABEL = 18      # height kept free above a horizontal line for its label


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the structure
class Seq:
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.items = list(items)


class Leaf:
    """One shape the flow goes through."""
    def __init__(self, box):
        self.box = box


class Stop:
    """One shape the flow ends in: a return, a goto's connector, the end."""
    def __init__(self, box):
        self.box = box


class If:
    def __init__(self, box, yes, no=None):
        self.box, self.yes, self.no = box, yes, no


class While:
    """The condition first; `step` (a for loop's) runs after the body; forever: no way out
    but a break."""
    def __init__(self, box, body, step=None, forever=False):
        self.box, self.body, self.step, self.forever = box, body, step, forever


class DoWhile:
    def __init__(self, body, box):
        self.body, self.box = body, box


class Switch:
    """cases: [(label, body)]; a case without a break runs on into the next one."""
    def __init__(self, box, cases, default=False):
        self.box, self.cases, self.default = box, list(cases), default


class Break:
    pass


class Continue:
    pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the result
class Path:
    """A line: pts from the start to the arrow's tip. src / dst: the shapes it joins (dst None
    while it is still open). label: "yes" / "no" / a case, written at its start."""
    __slots__ = ("pts", "src", "dst", "label", "arrow")

    def __init__(self, pts, src, label=None, arrow=True):
        self.pts, self.src, self.dst, self.label, self.arrow = list(pts), src, None, label, arrow

    def to(self, *pts):
        for p in pts:
            if p != self.pts[-1]:
                self.pts.append(p)
        return self


class _G:
    """A laid-out piece. Its line runs down x = 0: in at (0, entry), out at (0, h).
    opens: paths that go on to whatever comes next, ending at (0, h); breaks / conts: paths
    that go to the enclosing loop's exit or next round."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.pos, self.paths = {}, []
        self.opens, self.breaks, self.conts = [], [], []
        self.wl = self.wr = self.h = self.entry = 0.0
        self.first = None
        self.side = None        # (left edge, bottom) of a right column that does not come back:
                                # what follows may go beside it, h only covers the main line

    def empty(self):
        return not self.pos

    def jump_only(self):
        return self.empty() and not self.opens and bool(self.breaks or self.conts)

    def take(self, o, dx, dy):
        """o's shapes and lines, moved by (dx, dy); o's open ends are moved, not taken."""
        for k, (x, y) in o.pos.items():
            self.pos[k] = (x + dx, y + dy)
        for p in o.paths + o.opens + o.breaks + o.conts:
            p.pts = [(x + dx, y + dy) for x, y in p.pts]
        self.paths += o.paths
        self.breaks += o.breaks
        self.conts += o.conts


def _leaf(box, sizes, falls=True):
    w, h = sizes[box]
    g = _G()
    g.pos[box] = (-w / 2, 0.0)
    g.wl = g.wr = w / 2
    g.h, g.first = h, box
    if falls:
        g.opens = [Path([(0.0, h)], box)]
    return g


def _lay(n, sizes):
    if n is None:
        return _G()
    if isinstance(n, Seq):
        return _seq(n, sizes)
    if isinstance(n, Leaf):
        return _leaf(n.box, sizes)
    if isinstance(n, Stop):
        return _leaf(n.box, sizes, falls=False)
    if isinstance(n, If):
        return _if(n, sizes)
    if isinstance(n, While):
        return _while(n, sizes)
    if isinstance(n, DoWhile):
        return _do(n, sizes)
    if isinstance(n, Switch):
        return _switch(n, sizes)
    g = _G()
    (g.breaks if isinstance(n, Break) else g.conts).append(Path([(0.0, 0.0)], None))
    return g


def _seq(n, sizes):
    """One under another. A step goes beside the right column of a condition above it (an
    error branch that returns) when it fits on the left of it, so the main line does not wait
    for the column to end."""
    g = _G()
    y, opens, started = 0.0, None, False
    columns = []                                  # (left edge, bottom) of such columns
    for it in n.items:
        if isinstance(it, (Break, Continue)):
            if opens is not None:                 # the flow so far leaves the loop here
                low = max([y1 for _, y1 in columns if y1 > y], default=None)
                if low is not None:               # below a column beside it first, then right
                    y = low + VG / 2
                    for p in opens:
                        p.to((p.pts[-1][0], y))
                (g.breaks if isinstance(it, Break) else g.conts).extend(opens)
                opens = []
            elif not started:
                return _lay(it, sizes)
            continue
        c = _lay(it, sizes)
        if c.empty():
            continue
        if started:
            y += VG
        for x0, y1 in columns:                    # a line leaving to the right would cross it
            if c.wr + HG > x0 or c.breaks or c.conts:
                y = max(y, y1 + VG)
        for p in opens or ():
            p.to((0.0, y + c.entry))
            p.dst = c.first
            g.paths.append(p)
        g.take(c, 0.0, y)
        if not started:
            g.first, g.entry = c.first, c.entry
        if c.side is not None:
            columns.append((c.side[0], y + c.side[1]))
        opens = c.opens
        y += c.h
        g.wl, g.wr = max(g.wl, c.wl), max(g.wr, c.wr)
        started = True
    h = max([y] + [y1 for _, y1 in columns])
    for p in opens or ():
        p.to((0.0, h))
    g.h = h
    g.opens = opens if opens is not None else []
    return g


def _if(n, sizes):
    """The branch that goes on runs straight down; the other one in a column on the right
    (a branch that ends in a return sits there, so the main line stays straight)."""
    D = n.box
    dw, dh = sizes[D]
    yes, no = _lay(n.yes, sizes), _lay(n.no, sizes)
    yes = None if yes.empty() and not yes.jump_only() else yes
    no = None if no.empty() and not no.jump_only() else no
    falls = lambda c: c is not None and (bool(c.opens) or c.empty() and not c.jump_only())
    if no is not None and no.jump_only():         # a break / continue leaves by the right corner
        down, side, dl, sl = yes, no, "yes", "no"
    elif yes is not None and (yes.jump_only() or not falls(yes) and (no is None or falls(no))):
        down, side, dl, sl = no, yes, "no", "yes"
    else:
        down, side, dl, sl = yes, no, "yes", "no"
    g = _G()
    g.pos[D] = (-dw / 2, 0.0)
    g.first = D
    g.wl, g.wr = dw / 2, dw / 2
    right = (dw / 2, dh / 2)
    bottom = (0.0, dh)
    # a branch that only breaks or continues leaves the diamond's corner straight for the loop
    for c, at, lab in ((side, right, sl), (down, bottom, dl)):
        if c is not None and c.jump_only():
            p = Path([at], D, lab)
            (g.breaks if c.breaks else g.conts).append(p)
    if side is not None and side.jump_only():
        side = None
        sl = None
    if down is not None and down.jump_only():
        down = None
        dl = None
    # straight down
    bot = dh
    down_opens = []
    if down is not None:
        y = dh + VL
        g.paths.append(Path([bottom, (0.0, y + down.entry)], D, dl))
        g.paths[-1].dst = down.first
        g.take(down, 0.0, y)
        bot = y + down.h
        down_opens = down.opens
        g.wl, g.wr = max(g.wl, down.wl), max(g.wr, down.wr)
    elif dl is not None:
        bot = dh + VL - VG                        # room for the label
        down_opens = [Path([bottom, (0.0, bot)], D, dl)]
    # the column on the right: beyond everything on the main branch; a column that ends
    # (a return) only beyond what is beside it, so it stays near its condition
    side_opens, side_bot = [], 0.0
    xs = max(dw / 2, g.wr) + HG
    if side is not None and not side.opens and down is not None and not (down.breaks or down.conts):
        top = dh / 2 + LABEL + 8
        lines = down.paths + down.opens + down.breaks + down.conts     # already moved into place
        xs = max(dw / 2, _right_edge(g.pos, down.pos, lines, sizes, top, top + side.h)) + HG
    if side is not None:
        x = xs + side.wl
        y = dh / 2 + LABEL + 8
        p = Path([right, (x, dh / 2), (x, y + side.entry)], D, sl)
        p.dst = side.first
        g.paths.append(p)
        g.take(side, x, y)
        side_bot = y + side.h
        for q in side.opens:
            side_opens.append(q)
        g.wr = max(g.wr, x + side.wr)
        sx = x
    elif sl is not None:
        side_opens = [Path([right, (xs, dh / 2)], D, sl)]
        side_bot = dh / 2
        sx = xs
        g.wr = max(g.wr, xs + 4)
    # breaks and continues from the main branch would cross the column on their way right:
    # they go down the gap between the two first, and leave below
    jumps = [q for q in g.breaks + g.conts if side is not None and q.pts[-1][0] < sx - side.wl]
    if side is not None and not side_opens and not jumps:   # what follows may go beside it
        g.side = (sx - side.wl, side_bot)
        h = bot
    else:
        h = max(bot, side_bot)
    if side_opens:
        h += VG / 2                               # the side branch comes back in below
        for q in side_opens:
            q.to((sx, h), (0.0, h))
    for q in down_opens:
        q.to((0.0, h))
    if jumps:
        lane = sx - side.wl - HG / 2
        h += VG / 2
        for q in jumps:
            x, y = q.pts[-1]
            q.to((lane, y), (lane, h))
        for q in down_opens + side_opens:
            q.to((0.0, h))
    g.opens = down_opens + side_opens
    g.h = h
    return g


def _right_edge(pos, keys, paths, sizes, y0, y1):
    """How far right the shapes `keys` (at pos) and the lines reach between heights y0 and y1."""
    edge = 0.0
    for k in keys:
        (x, y), (w, h) = pos[k], sizes[k]
        if y < y1 and y + h > y0:
            edge = max(edge, x + w)
    for p in paths:
        for (xa, ya), (xb, yb) in zip(p.pts, p.pts[1:]):
            if min(ya, yb) <= y1 and max(ya, yb) >= y0:
                edge = max(edge, xa, xb)
    return edge


def _route_out(paths, xr, ye):
    """Breaks: right to the exit line, down it, and in to the main line below the loop."""
    for p in paths:
        x, y = p.pts[-1]
        p.to((xr, y), (xr, ye), (0.0, ye))


def _while(n, sizes):
    D = n.box
    dw, dh = sizes[D]
    g = _G()
    g.first, g.entry = D, J
    g.pos[D] = (-dw / 2, J)
    body = _lay(n.body, sizes)
    step = _leaf(n.step, sizes) if n.step is not None else None
    y = J + dh + VL
    tail = [Path([(0.0, J + dh)], D, "yes")]
    conts = []
    wl, wr = dw / 2, dw / 2
    for part in (body, step):
        if part is None:
            continue
        if part.jump_only():                      # while (x) break; / continue;
            (g.breaks if part.breaks else conts).extend(tail)
            tail = []
            continue
        if part.empty():
            continue
        for p in tail:
            p.to((0.0, y + part.entry))
            p.dst = part.first
            g.paths.append(p)
        if part is step and conts:                # continue: in from the right, above the step
            yc = y - VG / 2
            xc = max(wr, step.wr) + HG / 2        # inside the way out (wr + HG)
            for p in conts:
                px, py = p.pts[-1]
                p.to((xc, py), (xc, yc), (0.0, yc))
                p.dst = part.first
                g.paths.append(p)
            conts = []
        g.take(part, 0.0, y)
        conts += part.conts
        g.conts = []
        tail = part.opens
        y += part.h + VG
        wl, wr = max(wl, part.wl), max(wr, part.wr)
    yb = y - VG / 2                               # the way back starts here
    xl = -(wl + HG)
    for p in tail:
        p.to((0.0, yb), (xl, yb), (xl, J / 2), (0.0, J / 2))
        p.dst = D
        g.paths.append(p)
    xr = wr + HG                                  # the way out
    if conts:                                     # continue: up the right, in above the condition
        xc = xr + HG
        for p in conts:
            px, py = p.pts[-1]
            p.to((xc, py), (xc, J / 2), (0.0, J / 2))
            p.dst = D
            g.paths.append(p)
    ye = yb + VG / 2 + 4
    outs = []
    if not n.forever:
        outs.append(Path([(dw / 2, J + dh / 2), (xr, J + dh / 2), (xr, ye), (0.0, ye)], D, "no"))
    breaks, g.breaks = g.breaks, []
    _route_out(breaks, xr, ye)
    g.opens = outs + breaks
    g.wl, g.wr = -xl + 4, xr + (HG if conts else 4)
    g.h = ye
    return g


def _do(n, sizes):
    D = n.box
    dw, dh = sizes[D]
    g = _G()
    body = _lay(n.body, sizes)
    yd = J
    tail = []
    if not body.empty():
        g.take(body, 0.0, J)
        g.first, g.entry = body.first, J + body.entry
        tail = body.opens
        yd = J + body.h + VG
    else:
        g.first, g.entry = D, J
    conts = g.conts
    g.conts = []
    for p in tail:
        p.to((0.0, yd))
        p.dst = D
        g.paths.append(p)
    wl, wr = max(dw / 2, body.wl), max(dw / 2, body.wr)
    if conts:                                     # continue: in from the right, above the condition
        xc = wr + HG
        for p in conts:
            px, py = p.pts[-1]
            p.to((xc, py), (xc, yd - VG / 2), (0.0, yd - VG / 2))
            p.dst = D
            g.paths.append(p)
        wr = xc
    g.pos[D] = (-dw / 2, yd)
    xl = -(wl + HG)
    back = Path([(-dw / 2, yd + dh / 2), (xl, yd + dh / 2), (xl, J / 2), (0.0, J / 2)], D, "yes")
    back.dst = g.first
    g.paths.append(back)
    h = yd + dh + VL - VG                         # room for the label
    out = Path([(0.0, yd + dh), (0.0, h)], D, "no")
    breaks, g.breaks = g.breaks, []
    if breaks:
        h += VG / 2
        out.to((0.0, h))
        _route_out(breaks, wr + HG, h)
        wr += HG
    g.opens = [out] + breaks
    g.wl, g.wr, g.h = -xl + 4, wr + 4, h
    return g


def _switch(n, sizes):
    """The cases one under another; from the switch a line down the left branches into each
    (labelled with its values); a break leaves on the right."""
    S = n.box
    sw, sh = sizes[S]
    g = _G()
    g.pos[S] = (-sw / 2, 0.0)
    g.first = S
    cases = [(lab, _lay(c, sizes)) for lab, c in n.cases]
    wl = max([sw / 2] + [c.wl for _, c in cases])
    wr = max([sw / 2] + [c.wr for _, c in cases])
    xd = -(wl + HG)
    y = sh + VG
    opens = []
    trunk = Path([(-sw / 2, sh / 2), (xd, sh / 2)], S, arrow=False)
    for lab, c in cases:
        yb = y + LABEL
        trunk.to((xd, yb))
        if c.jump_only():                         # case k: break;
            p = Path([(xd, yb), (0.0, yb)], S, lab)
            (g.breaks if c.breaks else g.conts).append(p)
            y = yb + VG / 2
            continue
        if c.empty():
            continue
        top = yb + VG / 2
        branch = Path([(xd, yb), (0.0, yb), (0.0, top + c.entry)], S, lab)
        branch.dst = c.first
        g.paths.append(branch)
        for p in opens:                           # no break: on into this case
            p.to((0.0, top + c.entry))
            p.dst = c.first
            g.paths.append(p)
        g.take(c, 0.0, top)
        opens = c.opens
        y = top + c.h + VG / 2
    breaks, g.breaks = g.breaks, []
    ye = y + VG / 2
    for p in opens:
        p.to((0.0, ye))
    xr = wr + HG
    _route_out(breaks, xr, ye)
    outs = opens + breaks
    if not n.default:
        trunk.to((xd, ye), (0.0, ye))
        trunk.label, trunk.arrow = None, True
        outs.append(trunk)
    else:
        g.paths.append(trunk)
    g.opens = outs
    g.wl, g.wr, g.h = -xd + 4, xr + 4, ye
    return g


def layout(root, sizes, end=None, margin=40):
    """root: the structure; sizes: {box: (w, h)}; end: a box to end the flow in when it runs
    off the bottom. Returns (pos {box: (x, y) top-left}, paths, (width, height))."""
    g = _lay(root, sizes)
    paths = list(g.paths)
    if g.opens and end is not None:
        e = _leaf(end, sizes, falls=False)
        y = g.h + VG
        for p in g.opens:
            p.to((0.0, y))
            p.dst = end
            paths.append(p)
        g.take(e, 0.0, y)
        g.h = y + e.h
        g.wl, g.wr = max(g.wl, e.wl), max(g.wr, e.wr)
    else:
        for p in g.opens:
            if len(p.pts) > 1:
                p.arrow = False
                paths.append(p)
    for p in g.breaks + g.conts:                  # outside any loop: nowhere to go
        if len(p.pts) > 1:
            p.arrow = False
            paths.append(p)
    xs = [x for x, _ in g.pos.values()] + [x for p in paths for x, _ in p.pts]
    left = min([-g.wl] + xs)
    dx, dy = margin - left, margin
    pos = {k: (x + dx, y + dy) for k, (x, y) in g.pos.items()}
    for p in paths:
        p.pts = [(x + dx, y + dy) for x, y in p.pts]
    right = max([x + sizes[k][0] for k, (x, _) in pos.items()] + [x for p in paths for x, _ in p.pts] + [0])
    bottom = max([y + sizes[k][1] for k, (_, y) in pos.items()] + [y for p in paths for _, y in p.pts] + [0])
    return pos, paths, (right + margin, bottom + margin)


def route(paths, start, target, jumps=()):
    """The way from `start` to `target`, where it ends: (the shapes on it, the indexes of the
    lines on it). A shape is on the way when the flow can reach it from the start and go on
    from it to the target; a branch that can only end elsewhere (an error that returns) is
    not. jumps: (goto's connector, its label's connector) pairs, which no line joins."""
    succ, pred = {}, {}
    edges = [(p.src, p.dst) for p in paths] + list(jumps)
    for a, b in edges:
        if a is not None and b is not None:
            succ.setdefault(a, set()).add(b)
            pred.setdefault(b, set()).add(a)

    def reach(seed, nxt):
        seen, stack = {seed}, [seed]
        while stack:
            for m in nxt.get(stack.pop(), ()):
                if m not in seen:
                    seen.add(m)
                    stack.append(m)
        return seen
    fwd, back = reach(start, succ), reach(target, pred)
    on = fwd & back
    lines = {i for i, p in enumerate(paths) if p.src in fwd and p.dst in back}
    return on, lines

```

`src/ibr/core/comments.py`:

```py
"""How the plugin's notes sit in comments next to the user's own text."""
PFX = "libc: "
OPX = "op: "
SYS = "sys: "
_MANAGED = (PFX, OPX, SYS)

_JOIN = "  | "        # a note and the comment text that was already there share one line


def _after_join(ln):
    """The text after the first `  | ` outside a quoted string, or ''."""
    q, i = False, 0
    while i < len(ln):
        c = ln[i]
        if q and c == "\\":
            i += 2
            continue
        if c == '"':
            q = not q
        elif not q and ln.startswith(_JOIN, i):
            return ln[i + len(_JOIN):].strip()
        i += 1
    return ""


def _strip_managed(text):
    """Remove our notes (libc:/op:/sys:) from a comment and keep everything else,
    including the user's text joined onto a note's line with `  | `."""
    if not text:
        return ""
    keep = []
    for ln in text.split("\n"):
        while ln.startswith(_MANAGED):
            ln = _after_join(ln)
        if ln:
            keep.append(ln)
    return "\n".join(keep).strip("\n")

```

`src/ibr/core/decoders.py`:

```py
"""Decode flag/enum constants of libc and syscall arguments, per target."""
from .i18n import T


def _bits(val, table):
    parts = [name for bit, name in table if bit and (val & bit) == bit]
    rest = val
    for bit, _ in table:
        if bit and (val & bit) == bit:
            rest &= ~bit
    zero = [name for bit, name in table if bit == 0]
    if not parts and zero:
        parts = [zero[0]]
    if rest:
        parts.append("0x%X" % rest)
    return "|".join(parts) if parts else "0"

# Constant values follow the Linux UAPI headers. Several differ per architecture
# (open/mmap flags and socket types on ARM and MIPS, signal numbers on MIPS and in
# the Windows CRT), so the decoders read the target from _DEC_CTX, which
# collect_notes sets (set_target) for every function it annotates.
_DEC_CTX = {"arch": "", "pe": False}


def set_target(arch, pe):
    _DEC_CTX.update(arch=arch or "", pe=bool(pe))


def _family():
    a = _DEC_CTX["arch"] or ""
    return "mips" if a.startswith("mips") else "arm" if a in ("arm", "aarch64") else "x86"


_O_COMMON = [(0x80000, "O_CLOEXEC"), (0x40000, "O_NOATIME"), (0x200000, "O_PATH")]
_O_FLAGS = {
    "x86": [(0x40, "O_CREAT"), (0x80, "O_EXCL"), (0x100, "O_NOCTTY"), (0x200, "O_TRUNC"),
            (0x400, "O_APPEND"), (0x800, "O_NONBLOCK"), (0x1000, "O_DSYNC"), (0x2000, "O_ASYNC"),
            (0x4000, "O_DIRECT"), (0x8000, "O_LARGEFILE"), (0x10000, "O_DIRECTORY"),
            (0x20000, "O_NOFOLLOW"), (0x100000, "O_SYNC"), (0x400000, "O_TMPFILE")],
    "arm": [(0x40, "O_CREAT"), (0x80, "O_EXCL"), (0x100, "O_NOCTTY"), (0x200, "O_TRUNC"),
            (0x400, "O_APPEND"), (0x800, "O_NONBLOCK"), (0x1000, "O_DSYNC"), (0x2000, "O_ASYNC"),
            (0x4000, "O_DIRECTORY"), (0x8000, "O_NOFOLLOW"), (0x10000, "O_DIRECT"),
            (0x20000, "O_LARGEFILE"), (0x100000, "O_SYNC"), (0x400000, "O_TMPFILE")],
    "mips": [(0x8, "O_APPEND"), (0x10, "O_DSYNC"), (0x80, "O_NONBLOCK"), (0x100, "O_CREAT"),
             (0x200, "O_TRUNC"), (0x400, "O_EXCL"), (0x800, "O_NOCTTY"), (0x1000, "O_ASYNC"),
             (0x2000, "O_LARGEFILE"), (0x4000, "O_SYNC"), (0x8000, "O_DIRECT"),
             (0x10000, "O_DIRECTORY"), (0x20000, "O_NOFOLLOW"), (0x400000, "O_TMPFILE")],
}


_O_WIN = [(0x8, "_O_APPEND"), (0x10, "_O_RANDOM"), (0x20, "_O_SEQUENTIAL"), (0x40, "_O_TEMPORARY"),
          (0x80, "_O_NOINHERIT"), (0x100, "_O_CREAT"), (0x200, "_O_TRUNC"), (0x400, "_O_EXCL"),
          (0x4000, "_O_TEXT"), (0x8000, "_O_BINARY")]


def _dec_oflags(v):
    if _DEC_CTX["pe"]:                       # MSVC/MinGW CRT _open()
        base = {0: "_O_RDONLY", 1: "_O_WRONLY", 2: "_O_RDWR"}.get(v & 3, "0x%X" % (v & 3))
        extra = _bits(v & ~3, _O_WIN)
        return base + ("|" + extra if extra and extra != "0" else "")
    base = {0: "O_RDONLY", 1: "O_WRONLY", 2: "O_RDWR"}.get(v & 3, "O_ACCMODE")
    extra = _bits(v & ~3, _O_FLAGS[_family()] + _O_COMMON)
    return base + ("|" + extra if extra and extra != "0" else "")


def _o_creat_bit():
    return 0x100 if _DEC_CTX["pe"] or _family() == "mips" else 0x40


_PROT = [(1, "PROT_READ"), (2, "PROT_WRITE"), (4, "PROT_EXEC"), (0, "PROT_NONE")]


def _dec_prot(v):
    s = _bits(v, _PROT)
    return s + (" [RWX]" if (v & 7) == 7 else "")


_MAP_FLAGS = {
    "x86": [(0x10, "MAP_FIXED"), (0x20, "MAP_ANONYMOUS"), (0x40, "MAP_32BIT"),
            (0x100, "MAP_GROWSDOWN"), (0x800, "MAP_DENYWRITE"), (0x1000, "MAP_EXECUTABLE"),
            (0x2000, "MAP_LOCKED"), (0x4000, "MAP_NORESERVE"), (0x8000, "MAP_POPULATE"),
            (0x10000, "MAP_NONBLOCK"), (0x20000, "MAP_STACK"), (0x40000, "MAP_HUGETLB"),
            (0x100000, "MAP_FIXED_NOREPLACE")],
    "mips": [(0x10, "MAP_FIXED"), (0x400, "MAP_NORESERVE"), (0x800, "MAP_ANONYMOUS"),
             (0x1000, "MAP_GROWSDOWN"), (0x2000, "MAP_DENYWRITE"), (0x4000, "MAP_EXECUTABLE"),
             (0x8000, "MAP_LOCKED"), (0x10000, "MAP_POPULATE"), (0x20000, "MAP_NONBLOCK"),
             (0x40000, "MAP_STACK"), (0x80000, "MAP_HUGETLB")],
}


def _dec_map(v):
    kind = {1: "MAP_SHARED", 2: "MAP_PRIVATE", 3: "MAP_SHARED_VALIDATE"}.get(v & 3)
    fl = [(b, n) for b, n in _MAP_FLAGS["mips" if _family() == "mips" else "x86"]
          if not (n == "MAP_32BIT" and _family() != "x86")]
    extra = _bits(v & ~3, fl) if v & ~3 else ""
    return "|".join(p for p in (kind, extra) if p and p != "0") or "0"


_SIG_LINUX = {1:"SIGHUP",2:"SIGINT",3:"SIGQUIT",4:"SIGILL",5:"SIGTRAP",6:"SIGABRT",7:"SIGBUS",
              8:"SIGFPE",9:"SIGKILL",10:"SIGUSR1",11:"SIGSEGV",12:"SIGUSR2",13:"SIGPIPE",
              14:"SIGALRM",15:"SIGTERM",16:"SIGSTKFLT",17:"SIGCHLD",18:"SIGCONT",19:"SIGSTOP",
              20:"SIGTSTP",21:"SIGTTIN",22:"SIGTTOU",23:"SIGURG",24:"SIGXCPU",25:"SIGXFSZ",
              26:"SIGVTALRM",27:"SIGPROF",28:"SIGWINCH",29:"SIGIO",30:"SIGPWR",31:"SIGSYS"}
_SIG_MIPS = {1:"SIGHUP",2:"SIGINT",3:"SIGQUIT",4:"SIGILL",5:"SIGTRAP",6:"SIGABRT",7:"SIGEMT",
             8:"SIGFPE",9:"SIGKILL",10:"SIGBUS",11:"SIGSEGV",12:"SIGSYS",13:"SIGPIPE",
             14:"SIGALRM",15:"SIGTERM",16:"SIGUSR1",17:"SIGUSR2",18:"SIGCHLD",19:"SIGPWR",
             20:"SIGWINCH",21:"SIGURG",22:"SIGIO",23:"SIGSTOP",24:"SIGTSTP",25:"SIGCONT",
             26:"SIGTTIN",27:"SIGTTOU",28:"SIGVTALRM",29:"SIGPROF",30:"SIGXCPU",31:"SIGXFSZ"}
_SIG_WIN = {2:"SIGINT",4:"SIGILL",6:"SIGABRT",8:"SIGFPE",11:"SIGSEGV",15:"SIGTERM",
            21:"SIGBREAK",22:"SIGABRT"}


def _dec_sig(v):
    t = _SIG_WIN if _DEC_CTX["pe"] else _SIG_MIPS if _family() == "mips" else _SIG_LINUX
    return t.get(v, str(v))


def _dec_handler(v):
    # SIG_DFL / SIG_IGN / SIG_ERR are the constants 0, 1, -1 cast to a function pointer
    return {0: "SIG_DFL", 1: "SIG_IGN"}.get(v, "SIG_ERR" if v in (0xFFFFFFFF, (1 << 64) - 1) else "0x%X" % v)


_WHENCE = {0:"SEEK_SET",1:"SEEK_CUR",2:"SEEK_END",3:"SEEK_DATA",4:"SEEK_HOLE"}
_PTRACE = {0:"PTRACE_TRACEME",1:"PTRACE_PEEKTEXT",2:"PTRACE_PEEKDATA",3:"PTRACE_PEEKUSER",
           4:"PTRACE_POKETEXT",5:"PTRACE_POKEDATA",6:"PTRACE_POKEUSER",7:"PTRACE_CONT",
           8:"PTRACE_KILL",9:"PTRACE_SINGLESTEP",12:"PTRACE_GETREGS",13:"PTRACE_SETREGS",
           14:"PTRACE_GETFPREGS",15:"PTRACE_SETFPREGS",16:"PTRACE_ATTACH",17:"PTRACE_DETACH",
           24:"PTRACE_SYSCALL",0x4200:"PTRACE_SETOPTIONS",0x4201:"PTRACE_GETEVENTMSG",
           0x4202:"PTRACE_GETSIGINFO",0x4206:"PTRACE_SEIZE",0x4207:"PTRACE_INTERRUPT"}
_AF = {0:"AF_UNSPEC",1:"AF_UNIX",2:"AF_INET",10:"AF_INET6",16:"AF_NETLINK",17:"AF_PACKET",
       31:"AF_BLUETOOTH",38:"AF_ALG",40:"AF_VSOCK"}


def _dec_socktype(v):
    mips = _family() == "mips"
    kind = ({1:"SOCK_DGRAM",2:"SOCK_STREAM",3:"SOCK_RAW",4:"SOCK_RDM",5:"SOCK_SEQPACKET"} if mips else
            {1:"SOCK_STREAM",2:"SOCK_DGRAM",3:"SOCK_RAW",4:"SOCK_RDM",5:"SOCK_SEQPACKET",10:"SOCK_PACKET"})
    out = [kind.get(v & 0xF, str(v & 0xF))]
    if v & (0x80 if mips else 0x800):
        out.append("SOCK_NONBLOCK")
    if v & 0x80000:
        out.append("SOCK_CLOEXEC")
    return "|".join(out)


_ACCESS = [(4,"R_OK"),(2,"W_OK"),(1,"X_OK"),(0,"F_OK")]
_PRCTL = {1:"PR_SET_PDEATHSIG",2:"PR_GET_PDEATHSIG",3:"PR_GET_DUMPABLE",4:"PR_SET_DUMPABLE",
          15:"PR_SET_NAME",16:"PR_GET_NAME",21:"PR_GET_SECCOMP",22:"PR_SET_SECCOMP",
          38:"PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS",39:"PR_GET_NO_NEW_PRIVS",59:"PR_SET_SYSCALL_USER_DISPATCH",
          0x59616d61:"PR_SET_PTRACER"}
_FCNTL = {0:"F_DUPFD",1:"F_GETFD",2:"F_SETFD",3:"F_GETFL",4:"F_SETFL",5:"F_GETLK",6:"F_SETLK",
          7:"F_SETLKW",8:"F_SETOWN",9:"F_GETOWN",1030:"F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC"}
# MIPS keeps the old SVR4 numbers for the lock and owner requests
_FCNTL_MIPS = {**_FCNTL, 5: "5", 8: "8", 9: "9", 14: "F_GETLK", 23: "F_GETOWN", 24: "F_SETOWN"}


def _dec_fcntl(v):
    return (_FCNTL_MIPS if _family() == "mips" else _FCNTL).get(v, str(v))


_AF_WIN = {0: "AF_UNSPEC", 1: "AF_UNIX", 2: "AF_INET", 23: "AF_INET6"}


_FUTEX = {0: "FUTEX_WAIT", 1: "FUTEX_WAKE", 2: "FUTEX_FD", 3: "FUTEX_REQUEUE",
          4: "FUTEX_CMP_REQUEUE", 5: "FUTEX_WAKE_OP", 6: "FUTEX_LOCK_PI", 7: "FUTEX_UNLOCK_PI",
          8: "FUTEX_TRYLOCK_PI", 9: "FUTEX_WAIT_BITSET", 10: "FUTEX_WAKE_BITSET"}


def _dec_futex(v):
    s = _FUTEX.get(v & 0x7F, str(v & 0x7F))
    if v & 128:
        s += "|FUTEX_PRIVATE_FLAG"
    if v & 256:
        s += "|FUTEX_CLOCK_REALTIME"
    return s


def _dec_char(v):
    if 32 <= v < 127:
        return "'%s'" % chr(v) if chr(v) not in "'\\" else "'\\%s'" % chr(v)
    return {10: "'\\n'", 9: "'\\t'", 13: "'\\r'", 0: "'\\0'"}.get(v, str(v))


def _lut(d):
    return lambda v: d.get(v, "0x%X" % v if v > 9 else str(v))

# function key -> {arg index: decoder(intval)->str}
ARG_DEC = {
    "open":   {1: _dec_oflags}, "openat": {2: _dec_oflags},
    "mmap":   {2: _dec_prot, 3: _dec_map}, "mprotect": {2: _dec_prot},
    "mremap": {3: lambda v: _bits(v, [(1, "MREMAP_MAYMOVE"), (2, "MREMAP_FIXED"), (4, "MREMAP_DONTUNMAP")])},
    "lseek":  {2: _lut(_WHENCE)}, "fseek": {2: _lut(_WHENCE)},
    "kill":   {1: _dec_sig}, "raise": {0: _dec_sig},
    "signal": {0: _dec_sig, 1: _dec_handler}, "sigaction": {0: _dec_sig},
    "ptrace": {0: _lut(_PTRACE)}, "prctl": {0: _lut(_PRCTL)},
    "socket": {0: lambda v: (_AF_WIN if _DEC_CTX["pe"] else _AF).get(v, str(v)),
               1: _dec_socktype},
    "setvbuf": {2: lambda v: {0: T("_IOFBF (full)", "_IOFBF(全缓冲)"),
                              1: T("_IOLBF (line)", "_IOLBF(行缓冲)"),
                              2: T("_IONBF (unbuffered)", "_IONBF(无缓冲)")}.get(v, str(v))},
    "access": {1: lambda v: _bits(v, _ACCESS)},
    "fcntl":  {1: _dec_fcntl}, "futex": {1: _dec_futex},
    "putchar": {0: _dec_char}, "putc": {0: _dec_char}, "fputc": {0: _dec_char},
}
# functions whose first fd arg reads nicely as stdin/stdout/stderr
_FD_FIRST = {"read", "write", "close", "dup", "dup2", "fstat", "lseek", "fsync",
             "ftruncate", "fcntl", "sendfile", "pread", "pwrite", "dprintf"}
_FD_NAMES = {0: "stdin", 1: "stdout", 2: "stderr"}
```

`src/ibr/core/detect.py`:

```py
"""Identify the C runtime / compiler of a binary from its bytes."""
import re

from .binfmt import (_UBUNTU_GLIBC, _comment_toolchain, _glibc_hint_from_comment, _parse_elf_headers,
                     detect_go, detect_packer, detect_pe, detect_rust)
from .fingerprint import _arch_db, _lib_label, _load_fp, _present_in_buffer, _score


# ---------- Linux ELF ----------
def detect_elf(data, arch_hint=None, force=None, top=5, present_hook=None):
    r = {"os": "linux", "kind": None, "arch": None, "banner": None, "glibc_min": None,
         "build_id": None, "toolchain": None, "ranking": [], "best": None,
         "confidence": "low", "notes": []}
    hdr = _parse_elf_headers(data)
    r["arch"] = arch_hint or hdr["arch"]
    r["toolchain"] = _comment_toolchain(data)
    if hdr["tampered"]:
        r["notes"].append(hdr["tampered"] + ".")

    m = re.search(rb"\x04\x00\x00\x00\x14\x00\x00\x00\x03\x00\x00\x00GNU\x00(.{20})", data, re.S)
    if m:
        r["build_id"] = m.group(1).hex()
    m = re.search(rb"GNU C Library[^\x00\n]{0,80}?(?:version|release)[^\x00\n]{0,20}?(\d+\.\d+)", data)
    if not m:
        m = re.search(rb"\bglibc[ -](\d+\.\d+)\b", data)
    if m:
        try:
            r["banner"] = m.group(1).decode()
        except Exception:
            pass
    reqs = [int(x) for x in re.findall(rb"GLIBC_2\.(\d+)", data)]
    if reqs:
        r["glibc_min"] = max(reqs)

    # static vs dynamic from the program headers, not from a string search
    if force:
        r["kind"] = force
    elif hdr["interp"] is not None:
        r["kind"] = "dynamic"
    elif hdr["needed"] > 0:
        r["kind"] = "dynamic"       # a shared object / library: imports its libc
    else:
        r["kind"] = "static"        # includes static-pie (ET_DYN, no interp, no NEEDED)

    if r["toolchain"]:
        r["notes"].append("toolchain: " + r["toolchain"])

    if r["banner"]:
        r["best"] = "glibc " + r["banner"]; r["confidence"] = "high"
        r["notes"].append("version banner present, exact."); return r

    if r["kind"] == "dynamic":
        interp = hdr["interp"] or ""
        if "ld-musl" in interp or b"/ld-musl" in data:
            r["best"] = "musl (dynamic)"; r["confidence"] = "medium"
            r["notes"].append("dynamically linked against musl (Alpine).")
            return r
        if r["glibc_min"] is not None:
            r["best"] = "glibc >=2.%s" % r["glibc_min"]; r["confidence"] = "medium"
            r["notes"].append("dynamic: needs glibc >= 2.%s (lower bound, not exact)." % r["glibc_min"])
        hint = _glibc_hint_from_comment(r["toolchain"])
        if hint:
            r["notes"].append("toolchain suggests ~glibc %s." % hint)
        if hdr["needed"]:
            r["notes"].append("links %d shared object(s); fingerprinting a dynamic target is skipped." % hdr["needed"])
        if r["build_id"]:
            r["notes"].append("build-id present; resolve the exact build at https://libc.rip .")
        if not r["best"]:
            r["best"] = "dynamic (libc version not pinned)"
        return r

    # ---- static: fingerprint against the matching architecture ----
    if r["arch"] not in _load_fp():
        r["notes"].append("no fingerprint DB for arch=%s; static libc version not identified." % r["arch"])
        r["best"] = "static %s (unsupported arch)" % (r["arch"] or "?")
        return r
    index, vers = _arch_db(r["arch"])
    if present_hook is not None:
        present = present_hook(index)               # IDA: scan the loaded image
    else:
        present = set()
        if hdr["exec_ranges"]:
            for off, sz in hdr["exec_ranges"]:
                present |= _present_in_buffer(data[off:off + sz], index)
        else:
            present = _present_in_buffer(data, index)
    ranking, scored = _score(present, vers, top)
    r["ranking"] = [(v, round(s, 3), h, n) for (v, s, h, n) in ranking]
    if not present:
        # nothing matched at all: never promote an all-zero ranking to a verdict
        r["ranking"] = []
        r["best"] = "static %s (no libc code recognised)" % r["arch"]
        r["confidence"] = "low"
        r["notes"].append("no function-byte anchor matched: packed/encrypted code, a libc "
                          "build outside the DB, or no code loaded.")
        return r
    if scored:
        bestver, bests = scored[0][0], scored[0][1]
        second = scored[1][1] if len(scored) > 1 else 0.0
        r["best"] = _lib_label(bestver)
        ratio = (bests / second) if second > 0 else 999.0
        margin = bests - second
        if ratio >= 1.15 and margin >= 0.03:
            r["confidence"] = "high"
        elif ratio >= 1.06 and margin >= 0.015:
            r["confidence"] = "medium"
        else:
            r["confidence"] = "low"
        r["notes"].append("static (%s): function byte-anchor match; clear leader = most likely build." % r["arch"])
        r["notes"].append("lead over #2: %.1f pts (x%.2f)." % (margin * 100, ratio))
        hint = _glibc_hint_from_comment(r["toolchain"])
        if hint and not bestver.startswith("musl"):
            agree = "agrees" if hint == bestver else "cf. toolchain ~%s" % hint
            r["notes"].append("toolchain cross-check: %s." % agree)
        # an explicit Ubuntu release tag in .comment is independent evidence; when it
        # names the same glibc as the fingerprint leader, a narrow lead is still trusted
        tagged = r["toolchain"] and any(t in r["toolchain"] for t, _ in _UBUNTU_GLIBC)
        if bests < 0.05:
            r["confidence"] = "low"
            r["notes"].append("hit-rate very low: build may be outside the DB.")
        elif r["confidence"] == "low" and tagged and hint == bestver:
            r["confidence"] = "medium"
            r["notes"].append("narrow lead, raised to medium: the Ubuntu toolchain tag names the same glibc.")
        elif r["confidence"] == "low":
            r["notes"].append("top candidates are close; try FLIRT for each.")
    return r


def detect(path=None, data=None, force=None, top=5, arch_hint=None, present_hook=None):
    """Identify the runtime/compiler of a binary. Returns a result dict."""
    if data is None:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            data = f.read()
    pack = detect_packer(data)
    if data[:2] == b"MZ":
        go = detect_go(data); rust = detect_rust(data)
        r = detect_pe(data)
        if pack:
            r["notes"].insert(0, "packer: " + pack)
        if go:
            r["best"] = "Go " + go["version"]; r["notes"].insert(0, "Go program (PE), version embedded.")
            r["confidence"] = "high"
        elif rust:
            r["best"] = "Rust " + rust["version"]; r["notes"].insert(0, rust["note"] + " (PE).")
            r["confidence"] = "high"
        else:
            r["best"] = "MSVC (see Rich/linker)"; r["confidence"] = "medium"
        r["ranking"] = []; r["kind"] = "pe"
        return r
    if data[:4] == b"\x7fELF":
        go = detect_go(data); rust = detect_rust(data)
        if go:
            return {"os": "linux", "kind": "go", "best": "Go " + go["version"], "ranking": [],
                    "confidence": "high", "build_id": None, "arch": _parse_elf_headers(data)["arch"],
                    "notes": ["Go program (ELF), version string embedded, exact.",
                              "Go ships its own runtime; glibc version is irrelevant."]}
        r = detect_elf(data, arch_hint=arch_hint, force=force, top=top, present_hook=present_hook)
        if rust:
            r["notes"].insert(0, rust["note"] + "; libc below is the system allocator backend.")
        if pack:
            r["notes"].insert(0, "packer: " + pack)
        return r
    if pack:
        return {"os": "?", "kind": None, "best": pack, "ranking": [], "confidence": "medium",
                "notes": ["packed binary"]}
    return {"os": "?", "kind": None, "best": None, "ranking": [], "confidence": "low",
            "notes": ["not an ELF/PE file"]}

```

`src/ibr/core/fingerprint.py`:

```py
"""Byte-anchor fingerprint database: decode, index and score libc builds.

fingerprint.bin holds 29 builds each for x86_64 / i386 / aarch64: glibc 2.19-2.44 + musl.
Rebuild with tools/build-db.py -> tools/build-fingerprint.py (see tools/ for provenance)."""
from . import resources


_FP_CACHE = None       # {arch: {"table":[bytes...], "vers":{key:[idx...]}}}
_ARCH_CACHE = {}       # {arch: (prefix_index, {key:set(idx)})}


def _rd_uvarint(b, p):
    shift = val = 0
    while True:
        x = b[p]; p += 1
        val |= (x & 0x7F) << shift
        if not (x & 0x80):
            return val, p
        shift += 7


def _rd_str(b, p):
    n = b[p]; p += 1
    return b[p:p + n].decode(), p + n


def _decode_fp(blob):
    """Decode the compact IBIFP2 blob into {arch: {"table":[...], "vers":{...}}}."""
    if blob[:6] != b"IBIFP2":
        raise ValueError("bad fingerprint blob")
    p = 6
    narch = blob[p]; p += 1
    out = {}
    for _ in range(narch):
        arch, p = _rd_str(blob, p)
        ntab, p = _rd_uvarint(blob, p)
        table = []
        for _ in range(ntab):
            ln = blob[p]; p += 1
            table.append(blob[p:p + ln]); p += ln
        nver, p = _rd_uvarint(blob, p)
        vers = {}
        for _ in range(nver):
            key, p = _rd_str(blob, p)
            nidx, p = _rd_uvarint(blob, p)
            idx = []; prev = 0
            for _ in range(nidx):
                d, p = _rd_uvarint(blob, p)
                prev += d; idx.append(prev)
            vers[key] = idx
        out[arch] = {"table": table, "vers": vers}
    return out


def _load_fp():
    global _FP_CACHE
    if _FP_CACHE is None:
        try:
            _FP_CACHE = _decode_fp(resources.load("fingerprint.bin"))
        except Exception:
            _FP_CACHE = {}
    return _FP_CACHE


# Each anchor (10..24 bytes) is indexed by its *rarest* 8-byte window rather than its
# prefix. Anchors start in function prologues, so prefixes collide massively (x86_64:
# 12k anchors -> 4.5k distinct 8-byte prefixes, largest bucket 1124 anchors, all hit
# at every function entry). Keying on the window least shared with other anchors
# gives ~11.6k distinct keys, largest bucket 7, and ~300x less verification work.
_Q = 8


def _arch_db(arch):
    """Return (index, {key:set(idx)}) for an arch, or (None, None).
       index maps an 8-byte key to [(anchor_id, anchor_bytes, key_offset_in_anchor)]."""
    if arch in _ARCH_CACHE:
        return _ARCH_CACHE[arch]
    fp = _load_fp().get(arch)
    if not fp:
        _ARCH_CACHE[arch] = (None, None)
        return None, None
    table = fp["table"]
    freq = {}
    for a in table:
        for j in range(len(a) - _Q + 1):
            w = a[j:j + _Q]
            freq[w] = freq.get(w, 0) + 1
    index = {}
    for i, a in enumerate(table):
        j = min(range(len(a) - _Q + 1), key=lambda j: (freq[a[j:j + _Q]], -j))
        index.setdefault(a[j:j + _Q], []).append((i, a, j))
    vers = {k: set(v) for k, v in fp["vers"].items()}
    _ARCH_CACHE[arch] = (index, vers)
    return index, vers


def _lib_label(key):
    if key.startswith("musl"):
        return "musl " + key.split("_", 1)[-1]
    return "glibc " + key


# ======================================================================
#  Scanning — find which anchors are present in a byte buffer
# ======================================================================
def _scan_window(window, index, present):
    """Record ids of anchors occurring anywhere in window."""
    get = index.get
    for p in range(len(window) - _Q + 1):
        bucket = get(window[p:p + _Q])
        if bucket:
            for i, a, j in bucket:
                s = p - j
                if i not in present and s >= 0 and window[s:s + len(a)] == a:
                    present.add(i)


def _present_in_buffer(buf, index):
    present = set()
    if buf and index is not None:
        _scan_window(buf, index, present)
    return present


def _score(present, vers, top):
    scored = []
    for ver, ids in vers.items():
        if not ids:
            continue
        hit = len(present & ids)
        scored.append((ver, hit / len(ids), hit, len(ids)))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[1], reverse=True)
    return scored[:top], scored

```

`src/ibr/core/i18n.py`:

```py
"""Output language. One setting for the whole process, chosen in the panel."""
LANG = "en"


def set_lang(lang):
    global LANG
    LANG = lang if lang in ("en", "zh") else "en"


def T(en, zh):
    return zh if LANG == "zh" else en


def pick(pair):
    """The (en, zh) pair's entry for the current language."""
    return pair[1] if LANG == "zh" else pair[0]
```

`src/ibr/core/journal.py`:

```py
"""Undo journal: what the plugin changed, so Undo can put it back."""


class Journal:
    """Each entry is [value before the plugin's first change, the plugin's latest value].
    The first is what Undo restores (so running twice still undoes to the original); the
    second tells Undo whether the user has edited the item since.

    kinds: names, cmts (function comments), icmts (instruction comments),
    pcmts (pseudocode comments, keyed (func_ea, ea, position)), types; plus libflag,
    the functions FLIRT marked as library code."""

    KINDS = ("names", "cmts", "icmts", "pcmts", "types")

    def __init__(self):
        self.entries = {k: {} for k in self.KINDS}
        self.libflag = set()

    def record(self, kind, key, old, new):
        e = self.entries[kind].get(key)
        if e is None:
            self.entries[kind][key] = [old, new]
        else:
            e[1] = new

    def clear(self):
        for k in self.KINDS:
            self.entries[k].clear()
        self.libflag = set()

```

```py
# argument-aware templates (en, zh). {0},{1},... = rendered/decoded actual args.
TMPL = {
 "sleep":("sleep {0} sec","睡眠 {0} 秒"), "usleep":("sleep {0} us","睡眠 {0} 微秒"),
 "nanosleep":("high-res sleep","高精度睡眠"), "alarm":("SIGALRM in {0}s","{0} 秒后 SIGALRM"),
 "setvbuf":("{0} buffering: {2}","{0} 缓冲方式:{2}"),
 "fopen":("open {0} (mode {1})","打开 {0}(模式 {1})"),
 "freopen":("reopen {0} (mode {1})","重开 {0}(模式 {1})"),
 "open":("open {0} ({1}{mode})","打开 {0}({1}{mode})"), "openat":("openat {1} ({2}{mode})","相对打开 {1}({2}{mode})"),
 "close":("close {0}","关闭 {0}"), "fclose":("close stream {0}","关闭文件流 {0}"),
 "read":("read {2} bytes from {0}","从 {0} 读 {2} 字节"),
 "write":("write {2} bytes to {0}","向 {0} 写 {2} 字节"),
 "lseek":("seek {0} to {pos}","{0} 定位到 {pos}"),
 "fread":("read {n} bytes -> {0}","读 {n} 字节 -> {0}"),
 "fwrite":("write {n} bytes from {0}","写 {n} 字节(来自 {0})"),
 "fseek":("seek {0} to {pos}","{0} 定位到 {pos}"), "fgets":("read a line -> {0} (max {1})","读一行 -> {0}(上限 {1})"),
 "malloc":("alloc {0} bytes","分配 {0} 字节"), "calloc":("alloc {n} bytes, zeroed","分配 {n} 字节并清零"),
 "realloc":("resize {0} -> {1} bytes","把 {0} 重分配为 {1} 字节"), "free":("free {0}","释放 {0}"),
 "aligned_alloc":("alloc {1} bytes, {0}-aligned","分配 {1} 字节,{0} 对齐"),
 "memcpy":("copy {2} bytes {1} -> {0}","复制 {2} 字节 {1} -> {0}"),
 "memmove":("move {2} bytes {1} -> {0}","搬移 {2} 字节 {1} -> {0}"),
 "memset":("fill {0} with {1}, {2} bytes","把 {0} 填成 {1},共 {2} 字节"),
 "memcmp":("compare {2} bytes {0},{1}","比较 {2} 字节 {0},{1}"),
 "mmap":("map {1} bytes, {2}, {3}","映射 {1} 字节,{2},{3}"), "mprotect":("protect {1} bytes @ {0}: {2}","{0} 起 {1} 字节权限: {2}"),
 "munmap":("unmap {1} bytes @ {0}","解除映射 {0} 起 {1} 字节"),
 "mremap":("remap {0}: {1} -> {2} bytes","重映射 {0}: {1} -> {2} 字节"),
 "sbrk":("{n}","{n}"), "brk":("set program break to {0}","把堆顶设为 {0}"),
 "strlen":("length of {0}","{0} 的长度"), "strcpy":("copy {1} -> {0}","复制 {1} -> {0}"),
 "strncpy":("copy {2} bytes {1} -> {0}","复制 {2} 字节 {1} -> {0}"),
 "strcat":("append {1} to {0}","把 {1} 接到 {0}"), "strcmp":("compare {0}, {1}","比较 {0}, {1}"),
 "strncmp":("compare first {2} bytes of {0}, {1}","比较 {0}, {1} 的前 {2} 字节"),
 "strstr":("find {1} in {0}","在 {0} 里找 {1}"), "strchr":("find char {1} in {0}","在 {0} 里找字符 {1}"),
 "strtol":("{0} -> long (base {2})","{0} 转 long(进制 {2})"),
 "atoi":("{0} -> int","{0} 转 int"), "atol":("{0} -> long","{0} 转 long"),
 "puts":("print {0} + newline","打印 {0} 并换行"), "fputs":("write {0}","写字符串 {0}"),
 "putchar":("output char {0}","输出字符 {0}"),
 "system":("run shell: {0}","执行 shell 命令:{0}"), "popen":("run: {0}","执行命令:{0}"),
 "getenv":("read env {0}","读环境变量 {0}"),
 "setenv":("set env {0}={1}","设环境变量 {0}={1}"),
 "exit":("exit, status {0}","退出进程,状态码 {0}"), "_exit":("exit now, status {0}","立即退出,状态码 {0}"),
 "signal":("on {0} -> {1}","{0} 的处理设为 {1}"), "kill":("send {1} to pid {0}","给 pid {0} 发 {1}"),
 "raise":("raise {0}","自发 {0}"), "sigaction":("set handler for {0}","设置 {0} 的处理"),
 "ptrace":("ptrace {0}","ptrace {0}"), "prctl":("prctl {0}","prctl {0}"),
 "socket":("socket {0}/{1}","套接字 {0}/{1}"),
 "connect":("connect {0} -> {1}","{0} 连接到 {1}"), "bind":("bind {0} to {1}","{0} 绑定到 {1}"),
 "listen":("listen on {0} (backlog {1})","在 {0} 上监听(队列 {1})"), "accept":("accept on {0}","在 {0} 上接受连接"),
 "dup2":("dup {0} -> {1}","复制 fd {0} -> {1}"), "fcntl":("fcntl {0}: {1}","fcntl {0}: {1}"),
 "execve":("exec {0}","执行程序 {0}"),
 "access":("access {0} ({1})","检查 {0} 的访问 ({1})"),
 "qsort":("sort {1} items of {2} bytes","排序 {1} 个元素(每个 {2} 字节)"),
 "srand":("seed rand = {0}","随机种子 = {0}"), "srandom":("seed random = {0}","随机种子 = {0}"),
 "getrandom":("random {1} bytes -> {0}","取 {1} 随机字节 -> {0}"),
 "memalign":("alloc {1} bytes, {0}-aligned","分配 {1} 字节,{0} 对齐"),
}


def _describe(name):
    d = (DESC_ZH if i18n.LANG == "zh" else DESC).get(canon(name))
    return (PFX + d) if d else None
```
