`src/ibr/core/names.py`:

```py
"""Function-name normalisation: thunks, fortified variants, IDA placeholder names."""
import re


def canon(name):
    n = name or ''
    n = re.sub(r'^\.+', '', n)               # import-thunk notation: .open -> open
    n = re.sub(r'^j_', '', n)                # jump thunk: j_open -> open
    if re.fullmatch(r'_+[eE]xit(?:@.*)?', n):
        return "_exit"                       # _exit/_Exit skip atexit handlers; exit does not
    n = re.sub(r'^_+', '', n); n = re.sub(r'@.*$', '', n)
    n = re.sub(r'^isoc\d+_', '', n)          # __isoc99_scanf / __isoc23_strtol
    n = re.sub(r'_chk$', '', n); n = re.sub(r'_unlocked$', '', n)
    n = re.sub(r'^IO_', '', n); n = re.sub(r'^new_', '', n)
    n = re.sub(r'^libc_', '', n); n = re.sub(r'_internal$', '', n)
    n = re.sub(r'_nocancel$', '', n)         # __read_nocancel / __open_nocancel in static glibc
    n = re.sub(r'64$', '', n)
    return _ALIASES.get(n, n)


# glibc names the signal() implementation __bsd_signal and exports it under aliases
_ALIASES = {"bsd_signal": "signal", "ssignal": "signal", "sysv_signal": "signal"}


# __*_chk and friends carry extra leading/trailing args; map to (base, kept-arg-indices).
# A kept index of -1 means "insert a placeholder" (not used); None list = all args.
_VARIANTS = {
    "__printf_chk":    ("printf",    [1]),                 # (flag, fmt, ...)
    "__fprintf_chk":   ("fprintf",   [0, 2]),              # (fp, flag, fmt, ...)
    "__dprintf_chk":   ("dprintf",   [0, 2]),
    "__sprintf_chk":   ("sprintf",   [0, 3]),              # (s, flag, slen, fmt, ...)
    "__snprintf_chk":  ("snprintf",  [0, 1, 4]),           # (s, maxlen, flag, slen, fmt, ...)
    "__vprintf_chk":   ("vprintf",   [1]),
    "__vfprintf_chk":  ("vfprintf",  [0, 2]),
    "__vsnprintf_chk": ("vsnprintf", [0, 1, 4]),
    "__memcpy_chk":    ("memcpy",    [0, 1, 2]),           # (dst, src, len, destlen)
    "__memmove_chk":   ("memmove",   [0, 1, 2]),
    "__mempcpy_chk":   ("mempcpy",   [0, 1, 2]),
    "__memset_chk":    ("memset",    [0, 1, 2]),
    "__strcpy_chk":    ("strcpy",    [0, 1]),
    "__strncpy_chk":   ("strncpy",   [0, 1, 2]),
    "__strcat_chk":    ("strcat",    [0, 1]),
    "__strncat_chk":   ("strncat",   [0, 1, 2]),
    "__stpcpy_chk":    ("stpcpy",    [0, 1]),
    "__syslog_chk":    ("syslog",    [0, 2]),              # (pri, flag, fmt, ...)
    "__vsprintf_chk":  ("vsprintf",  [0, 3]),              # (s, flag, slen, fmt, ap)
    "__fread_chk":     ("fread",     [0, 2, 3, 4]),        # (ptr, ptrlen, size, n, fp)
    "__read_chk":      ("read",      [0, 1, 2]),           # (fd, buf, nbytes, buflen)
    "__fgets_chk":     ("fgets",     [0, 2, 3]),           # (buf, size, n, fp)
}


def resolve_variant(name):
    """Return (base_key, kept_indices_or_None) for a possibly fortified name."""
    n = (name or "").lstrip("._")            # drop import-thunk dots / leading underscores
    key = "__" + n
    if key in _VARIANTS:
        return _VARIANTS[key]
    return canon(name), None


# IDA's placeholder names: FLIRT's "library code, name unknown" and plain auto-names
_AUTONAME = re.compile(r"^(?:sub|nullsub|j_sub|unknown_libname)_[0-9A-Fa-f]+$")


_DUMMY_RE = re.compile(r'^(sub_|loc_|locret_|unk_|byte_|word_|dword_|qword_|off_|asc_|'
                       r'flt_|dbl_|tbyte_|packreal_|stru_|custdata_|algn_|def_|nullsub_|'
                       r'j_|unknown_libname|__imp_)')


def _is_dummy_name(nm):
    return (not nm) or bool(_DUMMY_RE.match(nm))

```

`src/ibr/core/nest.py`:

```py
"""A called function's chart opened inside its call in the flowchart, the way a map shows a
town's streets when you zoom into it: where the panel goes, how small the chart is drawn in
it, and how far it is open at a zoom. Geometry only (no Qt, no IDA).

The panel hangs under the call's shape (whose text, with the arguments, stays in sight),
centred on it, at least MIN_W wide, and holds the whole called chart scaled to its width
(never larger than drawn on its own). How far a call is zoomed into is how large its chart
draws it on screen (zoom x the chart's own scale), and only calls near the middle of the
window open: the chart is made (decompiled) from PREFETCH on, fades in between OPEN and FULL,
and is dropped under CLOSE, so only the few calls being looked at cost anything."""

PAD = 10            # between the panel's edge and the chart in it
GAP = 6             # between the call and its panel
MIN_W = 240         # a panel is at least this wide, whatever its call's width
PREFETCH = 1.8      # make the chart when the call is drawn this many times its size
OPEN = 2.4          # start showing it here...
FULL = 3.2          # ...fully shown here
CLOSE = 1.6         # drop it under this
MIDDLE = 0.25       # a call opens when it reaches the window less this much off each side
MAX_OPEN = 8        # panels open at once, at most
MAX_DEPTH = 6       # calls inside calls, at most


def panel(box, size):
    """box: (x, y, w, h) of the call's shape; size: (W, H) of the called chart.
    Returns (x, y, w, h, scale): the panel and the scale its chart is drawn at."""
    x, y, w, h = box
    W, H = max(size[0], 1.0), max(size[1], 1.0)
    pw = max(w, MIN_W)
    s = min(1.0, (pw - 2 * PAD) / W)
    if s >= 1.0:
        pw = max(w, W + 2 * PAD)
    return x + w / 2 - pw / 2, y + h + GAP, pw, H * s + 2 * PAD, s


def opening(r):
    """How opaque the panel is when its call is drawn r times its size: 0 up to OPEN, 1 from
    FULL on."""
    return min(1.0, max(0.0, (r - OPEN) / (FULL - OPEN)))


def middle(rect, view):
    """rect (x, y, w, h, on screen) reaches the middle of a view (w, h)."""
    x, y, w, h = rect
    vw, vh = view
    return (x + w > vw * MIDDLE and x < vw * (1 - MIDDLE) and
            y + h > vh * MIDDLE and y < vh * (1 - MIDDLE))

```

`src/ibr/core/notes.py`:

```py
"""Call-site notes: arguments, printf/scanf binding, syscalls, segment reads, magic.

Calls are ports.Expr trees; `prog` is a ports.Program."""
import re

from . import i18n
from .comments import OPX, PFX, SYS
from .decoders import (ARG_DEC, MAGIC, _DEC_CTX, _FD_FIRST, _FD_NAMES, _SEG_ELF, _SEG_PE, _SYS_ARGC,
                       _dec_char, _o_creat_bit, set_target)
from .i18n import T
from .libc import DESC, DESC_ZH, TMPL
from .names import resolve_variant
from .render import _arg_int, _arg_text, _cstr_arg, _fmt_num, _global_ea, _is_float
from .simd import _vec_call_note
from .syscalls import _syscalls

# format-string functions: index of the format argument (after variant mapping)
_FMT_FUNCS = {"printf": 0, "vprintf": 0, "fprintf": 1, "vfprintf": 1, "dprintf": 1,
              "sprintf": 1, "vsprintf": 1, "snprintf": 2, "vsnprintf": 2, "syslog": 1,
              "scanf": 0, "fscanf": 1, "sscanf": 1}
# printf: position, width, precision, conversion (MSVC I64/I32/w and %S/%C included)
_CONV = re.compile(r"%(?:(\d+)\$)?[-+ #0']*(\*(?:\d+\$)?|\d+)?(?:\.(\*(?:\d+\$)?|\d+))?"
                   r"(?:hh|h|ll|l|j|z|t|L|q|I64|I32|I|w)?([diouxXeEfFgGaAcCsSpnm%])")
# scanf: '*' suppresses the assignment (no argument), %[set], %ms allocates
_SCONV = re.compile(r"%(\*)?(?:(\d+)\$)?\d*(?:hh|h|ll|l|j|z|t|L|q|m|I64|I32|I|w)?"
                    r"([diouxXeEfFgGaAcCsSpn%]|\[\^?\]?[^\]]*\])")


def _fmt_note(prog, base, args, A):
    """printf/scanf family: show the format and bind each conversion to its argument,
    e.g. `printf "size: %zu bytes..." (%zu = n)`. Only as many arguments as the format
    consumes are used, so Hex-Rays' guessed extra variadic args never show up."""
    fi = _FMT_FUNCS[base]
    if fi >= len(args):
        return None
    fmt = _cstr_arg(prog, args[fi])
    gea = _global_ea(args[fi])
    bug = T("%s: the format (%s) is not a literal, check for a format-string bug",
            "%s 的格式串 %s 不是字面量, 注意格式化字符串漏洞") % (base, A[fi])
    if not fmt and gea is not None and prog.is_writable(gea) and not base.startswith("v"):
        if prog.is_byte_buffer(gea):
            return bug                # printf(global_buf): an empty .bss/.data byte buffer
    if fmt is None:
        if base.startswith("v") or gea is not None:
            # a va_list wrapper passing its format through, or a read-only global we
            # cannot decode: neither is evidence of a format-string bug
            return T("%s %s", "%s 格式串 %s") % (base, A[fi])
        return bug
    q = fmt.replace("\n", "\\n").replace("\t", "\\t").replace("\r", "\\r")
    q = '"%s"' % (q if len(q) <= 24 else q[:24] + "...")
    binds, k, rest, rest_raw = [], 0, A[fi + 1:], args[fi + 1:]
    # SysV x86-64 / AArch64 pass floating-point varargs in vector registers. Whether
    # Hex-Rays shows them depends on the call (an optimised stripped call often drops
    # them), so a float conversion takes a slot only when that argument is a float.
    fp_apart = not _DEC_CTX["pe"] and _DEC_CTX["arch"] in ("x86_64", "aarch64")
    if not base.startswith("v"):                      # v*printf take a va_list
        scan = base.endswith("scanf")
        for m in (_SCONV if scan else _CONV).finditer(fmt):
            extra = []
            if scan:
                supp, pos, conv = m.groups()
                if conv == "%" or supp:               # %*d reads and discards: no argument
                    continue
            else:
                pos, wid, prec, conv = m.groups()
                if conv == "%":
                    continue
                for star, what in ((wid, T("width", "宽度")), (prec, T("precision", "精度"))):
                    if star and star.startswith("*"):
                        if k < len(rest):
                            extra.append("%s %s" % (what, rest[k]))
                        k += 1
                if conv == "m":                       # %m: strerror(errno), no argument
                    continue
                if fp_apart and conv in "eEfFgGaA" and not pos and \
                        not (k < len(rest_raw) and _is_float(rest_raw[k])):
                    continue
            i = int(pos) - 1 if pos else k
            if 0 <= i < len(rest):
                val = rest[i]
                if conv == "c" and not scan:
                    iv = _arg_int(rest_raw[i])
                    if iv is not None:
                        val = _dec_char(iv & 0xFF)
                spec = m.group(0).replace("\n", "\\n").replace("\t", "\\t").replace("\r", "\\r")
                binds.append("%s %s %s%s" % (spec, "->" if scan else "=", val,
                                              " (%s)" % ", ".join(extra) if extra else ""))
            if not pos:
                k += 1
    if len(binds) > 4:
        binds = binds[:4] + ["..."]
    tail = " (%s)" % ", ".join(binds) if binds else ""
    dst = (A[0] if A else "?").replace("%", "%%")    # spliced into the templates below
    cap = (A[1] if len(A) > 1 else "?").replace("%", "%%")
    en, zh = {
        "printf": ("printf %s", "格式打印 %s"), "vprintf": ("vprintf %s", "格式打印 %s"),
        "fprintf": ("fprintf to " + dst + " %s", "向 " + dst + " 格式打印 %s"),
        "vfprintf": ("vfprintf to " + dst + " %s", "向 " + dst + " 格式打印 %s"),
        "dprintf": ("dprintf to " + dst + " %s", "向 " + dst + " 格式打印 %s"),
        "sprintf": ("sprintf into " + dst + " %s", "格式化写入 " + dst + ": %s"),
        "vsprintf": ("vsprintf into " + dst + " %s", "格式化写入 " + dst + ": %s"),
        "snprintf": ("snprintf into %s (max %s) %%s" % (dst, cap),
                     "格式化写入 %s (最多 %s 字节): %%s" % (dst, cap)),
        "vsnprintf": ("vsnprintf into %s (max %s) %%s" % (dst, cap),
                      "格式化写入 %s (最多 %s 字节): %%s" % (dst, cap)),
        "syslog": ("syslog %s", "写 syslog %s"),
        "scanf": ("scanf %s", "格式读入 %s"),
        "fscanf": ("fscanf from " + dst + " %s", "从 " + dst + " 格式读入 %s"),
        "sscanf": ("sscanf " + dst + " with %s", "按 %s 解析 " + dst),
    }[base]
    return T(en, zh) % q + tail


def _call_note(prog, e, nm):
    base, keep = resolve_variant(nm)
    try:
        raw = list(e.args)
    except Exception:
        raw = []
    args = [raw[i] for i in keep if i < len(raw)] if keep is not None else raw
    if keep is not None and base in _FMT_FUNCS and keep:
        args = args + raw[max(keep) + 1:]           # the variadic args after the format
    # render each arg, decoding known flag/enum/fd positions
    A = []
    decoders = ARG_DEC.get(base, {})
    for i, a in enumerate(args):
        iv = _arg_int(a)
        if i in decoders and iv is not None:
            A.append(decoders[i](iv))
        elif base in _FD_FIRST and i == 0 and iv in _FD_NAMES:
            A.append("fd %d (%s)" % (iv, _FD_NAMES[iv]))
        else:
            A.append(_arg_text(prog, a))
    # raw syscall: name it from the number, show as many args as the call takes
    if base == "syscall" and A:
        iv = _arg_int(args[0])
        if iv is not None:
            name = _syscalls(_DEC_CTX["arch"] or "x86_64").get(iv)
            if name:
                argc = _SYS_ARGC.get(name)
                if argc is None:                # unknown arity: Hex-Rays' extra args are guesses
                    return SYS + "%s (#%d)" % (name, iv)
                dec = ARG_DEC.get(name) or ARG_DEC.get(name.replace("rt_", "")) or {}
                sa = []
                for k, a in enumerate(args[1:1 + argc]):
                    v = _arg_int(a)
                    if k in dec and v is not None:
                        sa.append(dec[k](v))
                    elif k == 0 and name in _FD_FIRST and v in _FD_NAMES:
                        sa.append("%d (%s)" % (v, _FD_NAMES[v]))
                    else:
                        sa.append(A[1 + k])
                return SYS + "%s(%s) (#%d)" % (name, ", ".join(sa), iv)
    if base in _FMT_FUNCS:
        n = _fmt_note(prog, base, args, A)
        if n:
            return PFX + n
    pair = TMPL.get(base)
    if pair:
        t = pair[1] if i18n.LANG == "zh" else pair[0]
        try:
            return PFX + _dedup_words(t.format(*A, **_derived(base, args, A)))
        except Exception:
            pass
    d = (DESC_ZH if i18n.LANG == "zh" else DESC).get(base)
    return (PFX + d) if d else None


def _dedup_words(s):
    # templates like "close fd {0}" / "send {1} to pid {0}" meet args named fd / pid;
    # quoted string arguments are left exactly as they are
    parts = re.split(r'("(?:[^"\\]|\\.)*")', s)
    return "".join(p if i % 2 else re.sub(r"\b([A-Za-z_]\w*) \1\b", r"\1", p)
                   for i, p in enumerate(parts))


def _derived(base, args, A):
    """Template fields computed from several args: {n} (a byte count or phrase) and
    {mode} (the permission bits of an O_CREAT open)."""
    K = {"n": "", "mode": ""}
    if base in ("fread", "fwrite", "calloc"):
        i, j = (1, 2) if base != "calloc" else (0, 1)
        if j < len(args):
            x, y = _arg_int(args[i]), _arg_int(args[j])
            if x is not None and y is not None:
                K["n"] = _fmt_num(x * y, 8)
            elif x == 1 or y == 1:
                K["n"] = A[j] if x == 1 else A[i]
            else:
                par = lambda s: "(%s)" % s if " " in s and s != "..." else s     # (n + 1) x 16
                K["n"] = "%s x %s" % (par(A[i]), par(A[j]))
    elif base == "sbrk" and args:
        v = _arg_int(args[0])
        if v is not None:
            v = v - (1 << 64) if v >= (1 << 63) else v
        K["n"] = (T("query the program break", "查询当前堆顶") if v == 0 else
                  T("shrink the heap by %d bytes", "堆缩小 %d 字节") % -v if v is not None and v < 0 else
                  T("grow the heap by %s bytes", "堆扩大 %s 字节") % (A[0] if v is None else v))
    elif base in ("lseek", "fseek") and len(args) >= 3:
        # SEEK_CUR/SEEK_END offsets are relative: "to 0 (SEEK_END)" read like the start
        off, wh = _arg_int(args[1]), _arg_int(args[2])
        if off is not None:
            try:
                bits = 8 * args[1].size
            except Exception:
                bits = 64
            bits = bits if bits in (32, 64) else 64
            off &= (1 << bits) - 1             # numval() may already be sign-extended
            off = off - (1 << bits) if off >= 1 << (bits - 1) else off
        where = {1: T("the current position", "当前位置"), 2: T("the end", "末尾")}.get(wh)
        if where is None:
            K["pos"] = A[1] if wh == 0 else "%s (%s)" % (A[1], A[2])
        elif off == 0:
            K["pos"] = where
        elif off is None:
            K["pos"] = "%s + %s" % (where, A[1])
        else:
            K["pos"] = "%s %s %d" % (where, "-" if off < 0 else "+", abs(off))
    elif base in ("open", "openat"):
        fi = 1 if base == "open" else 2
        fl = _arg_int(args[fi]) if fi < len(args) else None
        md = _arg_int(args[fi + 1]) if fi + 1 < len(args) else None
        if fl is not None and fl & _o_creat_bit() and md is not None:
            K["mode"] = T(", mode 0%o", ", 权限 0%o") % md
    return K


def _segread_note(helper, e):
    m = re.match(r"__(read|write)(fs|gs)(byte|word|dword|qword)$", helper or "")
    if not m:
        return None
    try:
        off = _arg_int(e.args[0])
    except Exception:
        off = None
    if off is None:
        return None
    seg = m.group(2)
    signed =off - (1 << 32) if off >= 0x80000000 and off < (1 << 32) else \
        off - (1 << 64) if off >= (1 << 63) else off
    if signed < 0:
        what = ("thread-local variable", "线程局部变量")
    else:
        what = (_SEG_PE if _DEC_CTX["pe"] else _SEG_ELF).get((seg, signed))
        if not what:
            return None
    where = "%s:%s0x%X" % (seg, "-" if signed < 0 else "", abs(signed))
    return OPX + "%s %s %s" % (T("read", "读") if m.group(1) == "read" else T("write", "写"),
                               where, what[1] if i18n.LANG == "zh" else what[0])


def _magic_note(e):
    """If any immediate arg is a known magic constant, note it."""
    try:
        for a in e.args:
            iv = _arg_int(a)
            if iv is not None:
                key = iv & 0xFFFFFFFF
                if key in MAGIC:
                    return OPX + (MAGIC[key][1] if i18n.LANG == "zh" else MAGIC[key][0])
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def collect_notes(prog, calls):
    """Notes for one function. `calls` are its call expressions in pre-order (an
    intrinsic before the ones it folds), each with a valid address.
    Returns (by_ea, by_idx):
       by_ea  {ea: text}     — for persisting as user comments (keyed by address)
       by_idx {index: text}  — for live rendering (keyed by the call's item index,
                                so parent blocks that share an ea don't match)."""
    by_ea, by_idx = {}, {}
    set_target(prog.arch() or "", prog.is_pe())
    used = set()          # intrinsic calls already folded into an enclosing note
    for e in calls:
        got = []
        callee = e.x
        if callee.op == "helper":
            if e.uid in used:
                continue
            try:
                n = _vec_call_note(prog, e, used)
            except Exception:
                n = None
            n = n or _segread_note(callee.helper, e)
            if n:
                got.append(n)
        elif callee.op == "obj":
            nm = prog.func_name(callee.obj_ea) or prog.name_at(callee.obj_ea) or ""
            n = _call_note(prog, e, nm)
            if n:
                got.append(n)
        mg = _magic_note(e)
        if mg:
            got.append(mg)
        if got:
            text = "   ".join(dict.fromkeys(got))
            slot = by_ea.setdefault(e.ea, [])
            for g in got:
                if g not in slot:
                    slot.append(g)
            by_idx[e.index] = text
    return {ea: "   ".join(lst) for ea, lst in by_ea.items()}, by_idx

```

`src/ibr/core/render.py`:

```py
"""Argument text for notes: numbers, strings, variables, else the decompiler's own text.

Expressions are ports.Expr objects; `prog` is a ports.Program (strings and names)."""
import re


def _fmt_num(val, size):
    if size and 0 < size <= 8:
        val &= (1 << (8 * size)) - 1          # numval() is 64-bit: -1 in an int is 2**64-1
        signed = val - (1 << (8 * size)) if val >= (1 << (8 * size - 1)) else val
    else:
        signed = val - (1 << 64) if val >= (1 << 63) else val
    if -65536 <= signed < 0:
        return str(signed)
    if val > 9999:
        return "0x%X" % val
    return str(val)


def _decode_text(b):
    """bytes -> str for display: UTF-8, else GBK (common in Chinese CTF binaries)."""
    for enc in ("utf-8", "gbk"):
        try:
            s = b.decode(enc)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if all(ch.isprintable() or ch in "\n\r\t" for ch in s):
            return s
    return None


def _raw_cstr(prog, ea):
    """Full C string at ea (typed string literal, or untyped readable bytes), else None.
    Code addresses are never read as text (a function's first bytes can look printable)."""
    b = prog.cstr_bytes(ea)
    return _decode_text(b) if b else None


def _str_at(prog, ea):
    """Return a quoted, shortened C string at ea, else None."""
    s = _raw_cstr(prog, ea)
    if s is None:
        return None
    s = s.replace("\n", "\\n").replace("\t", "\\t").replace("\r", "\\r")
    if len(s) > 24:
        s = s[:24] + "..."
    return '"%s"' % s


_VALUE_CAST = re.compile(r"\((?:(?:unsigned|signed|const|volatile) )*(?:__int\d+|int|char|short|"
                         r"long long|long|_BYTE|_WORD|_DWORD|_QWORD|_OWORD|_BOOL\d|bool|size_t|"
                         r"float|double|__m64|__m128[id]?|__m256[id]?|__m512[id]?)\)")


def _strip_casts(a):
    while a.op == "cast" and a.x is not None:
        a = a.x
    return a


def _arg_text(prog, a):
    try:
        a = _strip_casts(a)
        op = a.op
        if op == "num":
            return _fmt_num(a.value, a.size)
        if op == "str":
            s = a.string
            if len(s) > 24:
                s = s[:24] + "..."
            return '"%s"' % s
        if op == "obj":
            return _str_at(prog, a.obj_ea) or prog.name_at(a.obj_ea) or ("0x%X" % a.obj_ea)
        if op == "var":
            return a.var_name
        if op == "ref" and a.x is not None:
            inner = a.x
            if inner.op == "var":
                return "&" + inner.var_name
            if inner.op == "obj":
                return _str_at(prog, inner.obj_ea) or ("&" + (prog.name_at(inner.obj_ea) or "0x%X" % inner.obj_ea))
        # fall back to the decompiler's own rendering: handles buf+8, s->field, arr[i], &x ...
        # Value casts go (pointer casts stay: they decide pointer arithmetic). Text
        # still too long becomes "...": cutting it mid-parenthesis reads worse, and
        # the full expression is right there in the call.
        txt = _VALUE_CAST.sub("", a.render())
        return txt if len(txt) <= 36 else "..."
    except Exception:
        pass
    return "?"


def _is_float(a):
    try:
        return a.op == "fnum" or bool(a.is_float)
    except Exception:
        return False


def _arg_int(a):
    """Return the integer value of a constant arg (after casts), else None."""
    try:
        a = _strip_casts(a)
        if a.op == "num":
            return a.value
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _global_ea(a):
    """Address of a global the argument names directly (x or &x), else None."""
    a = _strip_casts(a)
    if a.op == "obj":
        return a.obj_ea
    if a.op == "ref" and a.x is not None and a.x.op == "obj":
        return a.x.obj_ea
    return None


def _cstr_arg(prog, a):
    """Full text of a string-literal argument (untruncated), else None."""
    try:
        a = _strip_casts(a)
        if a.op == "str":
            return a.string
        ea = _global_ea(a)
        return _raw_cstr(prog, ea) if ea is not None else None
    except Exception:
        return None

```

`src/ibr/core/report.py`:

```py
"""Detection result as panel text."""
from .fingerprint import _lib_label
from .i18n import T


def _fmt(r):
    if not r:
        return ""
    conf = {"high": T("high", "高"), "medium": T("medium", "中"),
            "low": T("low", "低")}.get(r.get("confidence"), r.get("confidence"))
    # verdict first: it is the line the user opened the panel for
    lines = ["%s   [%s %s]" % (r.get("best"), T("confidence", "置信度"), conf),
             "%s / %s / %s" % (r.get("os"), r.get("kind"), r.get("arch") or "?")]
    for v, s, h, n in r.get("ranking", []):
        lines.append("  %-12s %5.1f%%  %d/%d" % (_lib_label(v), s * 100, h, n))
    for note in r.get("notes", []):
        lines.append("  . " + note)
    if r.get("build_id"):
        lines.append("build-id: %s" % r["build_id"])
    return "\n".join(lines)

```

```py
"""Data files (fingerprint DB, syscall tables), read from ibr/data/."""
import os

DATA = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "data")


def load(name):
    """Raw bytes of a data file."""
    with open(os.path.join(DATA, name), "rb") as f:
        return f.read()
```

`src/ibr/core/simd.py`:

```py
"""MMX/SSE/AVX intrinsic calls folded into plain infix expressions.

Calls are ports.Expr trees; `prog` is a ports.Program."""
import re

from .comments import OPX
from .i18n import T
from .render import _arg_text, _strip_casts


# ---------- MMX/SSE/AVX intrinsic naming (parsed, not enumerated) ----------
_VEC_OP = {"add":"+", "sub":"-", "mul":"*", "mullo":"*", "mulhi":"* hi", "and":"&", "andnot":"andnot",
           "or":"|", "xor":"^", "sll":"<<", "slli":"<<", "srl":">>", "srli":">>",
           "sra":">>(signed)", "srai":">>(signed)", "min":"min", "max":"max", "avg":"avg",
           "cmpeq":"==", "cmpgt":">", "unpacklo":"interleave lo", "unpackhi":"interleave hi",
           "shuffle":"shuffle", "shufflelo":"shuffle lo", "shufflehi":"shuffle hi",
           "packs":"pack (sat)", "packus":"pack (usat)", "blend":"blend", "blendv":"blend"}
_VEC_LANE = {"epi8":"per byte","epu8":"per u8","epi16":"per 16b","epu16":"per u16",
             "epi32":"per 32b","epu32":"per u32","epi64":"per 64b","epu64":"per u64",
             "ps":"per f32","pd":"per f64","si128":"128b","si256":"256b","si512":"512b","si64":"64b",
             "pi8":"per byte","pi16":"per 16b","pi32":"per 32b"}
_CRYPTO = {"aesenc":"AES encrypt round","aesenclast":"AES encrypt last round",
           "aesdec":"AES decrypt round","aesdeclast":"AES decrypt last round",
           "aesimc":"AES inverse mix columns","aeskeygenassist":"AES key schedule",
           "clmulepi64":"carry-less multiply (GHASH/GCM)","crc32":"CRC32 accumulate",
           "sha1rnds4":"SHA-1 4 rounds","sha1nexte":"SHA-1 next E","sha256rnds2":"SHA-256 2 rounds",
           "sha256msg1":"SHA-256 msg sched 1","sha256msg2":"SHA-256 msg sched 2"}


_INFIX = {"add":"+", "sub":"-", "mul":"*", "mullo":"*", "and":"&", "or":"|", "xor":"^",
          "sll":"<<", "slli":"<<", "srl":">>", "srli":">>", "sra":">>", "srai":">>"}
_MMX_OP = {"pxor":"xor", "por":"or", "pand":"and", "pandn":"andnot", "mull":"mullo", "mulh":"mulhi"}
_VEC_FN_ZH = {"interleave lo":"交错低半", "interleave hi":"交错高半", "shuffle":"重排",
              "shuffle lo":"重排低半", "shuffle hi":"重排高半", "pack (sat)":"饱和打包",
              "pack (usat)":"无符号饱和打包", "blend":"混合"}
# lane key -> (bits, English unit, Chinese unit), for the plain-language tail
_LANE_UNIT = {"per byte": (8, "byte", "字节"), "per u8": (8, "unsigned byte", "无符号字节"),
              "per 16b": (16, "16-bit lane", "16 位"), "per u16": (16, "unsigned 16-bit lane", "无符号 16 位"),
              "per 32b": (32, "32-bit lane", "32 位"), "per u32": (32, "unsigned 32-bit lane", "无符号 32 位"),
              "per 64b": (64, "64-bit lane", "64 位"), "per u64": (64, "unsigned 64-bit lane", "无符号 64 位"),
              "per f32": (32, "float", "f32"), "per f64": (64, "double", "f64"),
              "64b": (64, "64-bit lane", "64 位"), "128b": (128, "128-bit lane", "128 位"),
              "256b": (256, "256-bit lane", "256 位"), "512b": (512, "512-bit lane", "512 位")}
_BITWISE_SYMS = {"^", "&", "|", "&~", "andnot"}       # no lanes: a bit is a bit


def _reg_bits(h):
    """Register width of an intrinsic: _m_* and *_pi8/16/32/_si64 are MMX."""
    if h.startswith("m_") or re.search(r"_(?:pi\d+|si64)$", h):
        return 64
    return 512 if h.startswith("mm512_") else 256 if h.startswith("mm256_") else 128

_M64_WHOLE = re.compile(r"^(.+?)(->|\.)m64_[iu]64$")


def _vec_operand(t):
    """`p->m64_u64` is the whole 64-bit value at p, `x.m64_u64` is x."""
    m = _M64_WHOLE.match(t)
    if not m:
        return t
    if m.group(2) == ".":
        return m.group(1)
    return "*" + (m.group(1) if re.match(r"^\w+$", m.group(1)) else "(%s)" % m.group(1))


def _intrin_note(helper, args=()):
    """Explain an MMX/SSE/AVX/crypto intrinsic call. `args` are the operands as
    rendered by Hex-Rays, so the note reads `a ^ b (per 64b)` rather than just `^`."""
    h = (helper or "").lstrip("_")
    a = [x for x in args if x]
    # crypto first (highest value)
    for k, v in sorted(_CRYPTO.items(), key=lambda kv: -len(kv[0])):    # aesenclast before aesenc
        if k in h:
            return OPX + v + ("(%s)" % ", ".join(a) if a else "")
    if h == "m_empty":
        return OPX + T("emms (clear MMX state)", "emms(清空 MMX 状态)")
    op = lane = None
    m = re.match(r"mm(?:256|512)?_(?:mask_|maskz_)?(\w+?)_(epi\d+|epu\d+|ps|pd|si\d+|pi\d+)$", h)
    if m and m.group(1) in _VEC_OP:
        op, lane = m.group(1), _VEC_LANE.get(m.group(2), m.group(2))
    else:
        # legacy _m_* MMX intrinsics (pxor, psrlwi, paddb, ...); bare pxor/por/pand are 64-bit
        m = re.match(r"m_p?(pxor|por|pand|pandn|add|sub|mull|mulh|sll|srl|sra)([bwdq])?i?$", h)
        if not m:
            return None
        op = _MMX_OP.get(m.group(1), m.group(1))
        lane = {"b":"per byte", "w":"per 16b", "d":"per 32b", "q":"per 64b"}.get(m.group(2), "64b")
    lk = ("signed " + lane) if op in ("sra", "srai") else lane
    tail = _lane_tail({(lk, _reg_bits(h)): [_INFIX.get(op) or _VEC_OP.get(op, op)]})
    if len(a) >= 2:
        x, y = a[0], a[1]
        if op == "xor" and x == y:
            return OPX + "%s ^ %s = 0 (%s)" % (x, y, T("zeroing", "清零"))
        if op in _INFIX:
            return OPX + "%s %s %s%s" % (x, _INFIX[op], y, tail)
        if op == "andnot":
            return OPX + "~%s & %s%s" % (x, y, tail)
        if op == "mulhi":
            return OPX + T("high half of %s * %s", "%s * %s 的高半部分") % (x, y) + tail
        if op in ("cmpeq", "cmpgt"):
            return OPX + "%s %s %s ? ~0 : 0%s" % (x, _VEC_OP[op], y, tail)
        return OPX + "%s(%s)%s" % (_VEC_OP[op], ", ".join(a), tail)
    return OPX + _VEC_OP.get(op, op) + tail

# data-movement intrinsics, folded into the expression as the value they produce
_V_LOAD = re.compile(r"mm(?:256|512)?_(?:load[lu]?|loadl|lddqu)_\w+$")
_V_SCALAR = re.compile(r"mm(?:256|512)?_cvtsi(?:32|64)_si(?:64|128|256)$|m_from_int(?:64)?$|mm_cvtsi64_m64$")
_V_ZERO = re.compile(r"mm(?:256|512)?_setzero_\w+$")
_V_SPLAT = re.compile(r"mm(?:256|512)?_set1_\w+$")


def _vec_op(h):
    """(op, lane) for an arithmetic/logic/compare intrinsic name, else None."""
    m = re.match(r"mm(?:256|512)?_(?:mask_|maskz_)?(\w+?)_(epi\d+|epu\d+|ps|pd|si\d+|pi\d+)$", h)
    if m and m.group(1) in _VEC_OP:
        return m.group(1), _VEC_LANE.get(m.group(2), m.group(2))
    m = re.match(r"m_p?(pxor|por|pand|pandn|add|sub|mull|mulh|sll|srl|sra)([bwdq])?i?$", h)
    if m:
        return (_MMX_OP.get(m.group(1), m.group(1)),
                {"b":"per byte", "w":"per 16b", "d":"per 32b", "q":"per 64b"}.get(m.group(2), "64b"))
    return None


_BINOP = re.compile(r" (\|\||&&|<<|>>|==|!=|<=|>=|[|^&+\-*/%<>?:]) ")
_ASSOC = {"|", "^", "&", "+", "*"}


def _scan(t, limit=None):
    """Walk `t` outside string literals, yielding (index, char, open brackets)."""
    stack, q, i = [], None, 0
    end = len(t) if limit is None else min(limit, len(t))
    while i < end:
        c = t[i]
        if q:
            if c == "\\":
                i += 1
            elif c == q:
                q = None
        elif c in "\"'":
            q = c
        elif c in "([{":
            stack.append(")]}"["([{".index(c)])
        elif c in ")]}" and stack:
            stack.pop()
        else:
            yield i, c, stack
        i += 1


def _top_ops(t):
    """Binary operators of `t` that are outside every bracket."""
    return {m.group(1) for i, c, st in _scan(t) if c == " " and not st
            for m in [_BINOP.match(t, i)] if m}


def _operand(t, sym=None, left=False):
    """Operand text, bracketed only when needed. A chain of one associative
    operator (`a | b | c`) and a term with no operator outside brackets
    (`*(p + 4)`, `rotl(x, 1)`) need none."""
    if t[1]:
        return t[0]
    ops = _top_ops(t[0])
    if not ops or (sym in _ASSOC and ops == {sym}) or (sym == "-" and left and ops <= {"+", "-"}):
        return t[0]
    return "(%s)" % t[0]


def _clip(t, n=140):
    """Shorten at an operator or comma and close what is still open, so a long
    chain reads `a | b | (c & ...)` instead of stopping mid-bracket."""
    if len(t) <= n:
        return t
    cuts = []
    for i, c, st in _scan(t, n - 8):
        m = _BINOP.match(t, i) if c == " " else None
        if c == "," and t[i + 1:i + 2] == " ":
            cuts.append((i + 2, len(st), "".join(reversed(st))))
        elif m and m.group(1) not in ("?", ":"):
            cuts.append((m.end(), len(st), "".join(reversed(st))))
    if not cuts:
        return t[:n - 3] + "..."
    late = [c for c in cuts if c[0] >= n // 2] or cuts        # the shallowest cut in the second half
    end, _, closers = [c for c in late if c[1] == min(d for _, d, _ in late)][-1]
    return t[:end] + "..." + closers


def _vec_text(h, a, lanes):
    """Render one intrinsic over already-rendered operands [(text, atomic)] as
    (text, atomic), recording its lane in `lanes`; None if it does not fold."""
    p = _operand
    if _V_ZERO.match(h):
        return "0", True
    if a and _V_SPLAT.match(h):
        return "splat(%s)" % a[0][0], True
    if a and _V_SCALAR.match(h):
        return a[0]
    if len(a) == 1 and _V_LOAD.match(h):        # 2-arg loadl/loadh_pd merge into a vector
        t = a[0][0]
        if t.startswith("&") and re.match(r"&[\w.]+$", t):
            return t[1:], True                         # *&x is x
        return "*" + p(a[0]), True
    vo = _vec_op(h)
    if vo and vo[0] in ("shuffle", "shufflelo", "shufflehi") and a:
        sh = _shuffle_text(h, a)
        if sh:
            return sh, True
    if not vo or len(a) < 2:
        return None
    op, lane = vo
    if op in ("unpacklo", "unpackhi") and lane in ("per 64b", "per f64"):
        if op == "unpacklo" and lane == "per 64b":
            # low qword of each: how compilers build a 2-element array, e.g. {"sh", "-c"}
            return "{%s, %s}" % (a[0][0], a[1][0]), True
        k = 0 if op == "unpacklo" else 1
        ix2 = lambda t: t[0] if t[1] and not t[0].startswith(("*", "&")) else "(%s)" % t[0]
        return "{%s[%d], %s[%d]}" % (ix2(a[0]), k, ix2(a[1]), k), True
    x, y = a[0], a[1]
    if op == "xor" and x[0] == y[0]:
        return "0", True
    atomic = False
    reg = _reg_bits(h)
    if op in ("sll", "slli", "srl", "srli") and lane in ("128b", "256b", "512b"):
        # _mm_slli_si128 / _mm_srli_si128 (pslldq/psrldq) shift by BYTES, per 128-bit lane
        sym = _INFIX[op]
        txt = "%s %s %s %s" % (p(x), sym, y[0], T("bytes", "字节"))
        syms = lanes.setdefault(("128b", reg), [])
        if sym not in syms:
            syms.append(sym)
        return txt, False
    if op in ("or", "xor") and not x[1] and not y[1]:
        # (v << n) | (v >> (w - n)) on w-bit lanes is a rotate (ChaCha, BLAKE, SipHash...)
        rot = None
        for a1, a2 in ((x[0], y[0]), (y[0], x[0])):
            m1 = re.fullmatch(r"(.+) << (\d+)", a1)
            m2 = re.fullmatch(r"(.+) >> (\d+)", a2)
            if m1 and m2 and m1.group(1) == m2.group(1):
                w = int(m1.group(2)) + int(m2.group(2))
                # both shifts logical: (v << n) | (v >>(signed) m) sign-fills, no rotate
                keys = [k for k in lanes if k[0] == "per %db" % w and "<<" in lanes[k] and ">>" in lanes[k]]
                if w in (16, 32, 64) and keys:
                    rot = (m1.group(1), int(m1.group(2)), w, keys[0])
        if rot:
            base, n, w, lk = rot
            # rotating by half the lane swaps its two halves (bswap16 for 16-bit lanes)
            lanes[lk] = [s for s in lanes[lk] if s not in ("<<", ">>")] + ["swap" if 2 * n == w else "rotl"]
            return "rotl(%s, %d)" % (base, n), True
    if op in ("or", "xor", "add") and "0" in (x[0], y[0]):
        return y if x[0] == "0" else x          # 0 | v is v (accumulators start at zero)
    if op in ("packs", "packus") and lane in ("per 16b", "per 32b"):
        # narrow two vectors into one, every lane clamped: pack_i8(a, b) for packs_epi16
        half = 8 if lane == "per 16b" else 16
        lo, hi = (-(1 << (half - 1)), (1 << (half - 1)) - 1) if op == "packs" else (0, (1 << half) - 1)
        syms = lanes.setdefault((lane, reg), [])
        if "clamp %d..%d" % (lo, hi) not in syms:
            syms.append("clamp %d..%d" % (lo, hi))
        return "pack_%s%d(%s, %s)" % ("i" if op == "packs" else "u", half, x[0], y[0]), True
    if op in _INFIX:
        sym = _INFIX[op]
        txt = "%s %s %s" % (p(x, sym, True), sym, p(y, sym))
    elif op == "andnot":
        sym, txt = "&~", "~%s & %s" % (p(x), p(y, "&"))
    elif op in ("cmpeq", "cmpgt"):
        sym, txt = _VEC_OP[op], "%s %s %s ? ~0 : 0" % (p(x), _VEC_OP[op], p(y))
    elif op == "mulhi":
        sym, txt, atomic = "hi*", T("hi(%s * %s)", "高半(%s * %s)") % (p(x), p(y)), True
    else:                                   # min/max/avg/shuffle/unpack/blend
        sym = T(_VEC_OP[op], _VEC_FN_ZH.get(_VEC_OP[op], _VEC_OP[op]))
        txt, atomic = "%s(%s)" % (sym, ", ".join(t[0] for t in a)), True
    if op in ("sra", "srai"):
        lane = "signed " + lane
    syms = lanes.setdefault((lane, reg), [])
    if sym not in syms:
        syms.append(sym)
    return txt, atomic


def _shuffle_text(h, a):
    """Spell out the lane selection of a shuffle with a constant immediate."""
    m = re.match(r"mm(?:256|512)?_shuffle(lo|hi)?_(epi32|epi16|ps|pd)$", h)
    if not m or len(a) < 2:
        return None
    imm = a[-1][0]
    try:
        imm = int(imm, 0) if not imm.endswith("u") else int(imm[:-1], 0)
    except ValueError:
        return None
    ix = lambda t: t[0] if t[1] and not t[0].startswith(("*", "&", "-", "~")) else "(%s)" % t[0]
    if m.group(1):                                  # shufflelo/hi_epi16: 4 of 8 words
        off = 0 if m.group(1) == "lo" else 4
        order = " ".join(str(off + ((imm >> (2 * i)) & 3)) for i in range(4))
        return "%s(%s, [%s])" % (T("shuffle " + m.group(1), "重排" + ("低半" if off == 0 else "高半")),
                                 a[0][0], order)
    if m.group(2) == "epi32":
        src = ix(a[0])
        idx = [(imm >> (2 * i)) & 3 for i in range(4)]
        if len(set(idx)) == 1:
            return "splat(%s[%d])" % (src, idx[0])
        if len(src) > 16:                           # do not spell a long source out 4 times
            return "%s(%s, [%s])" % (T("shuffle", "重排"), a[0][0], " ".join(map(str, idx)))
        return "{%s}" % ", ".join("%s[%d]" % (src, i) for i in idx)
    if m.group(2) == "ps" and len(a) >= 3:
        x, y = ix(a[0]), ix(a[1])
        idx = [(imm >> (2 * i)) & 3 for i in range(4)]
        if len(x) + len(y) > 32:
            return "%s(%s, %s, [%d %d | %d %d])" % (T("shuffle", "重排"), a[0][0], a[1][0], *idx)
        return "{%s[%d], %s[%d], %s[%d], %s[%d]}" % (x, idx[0], x, idx[1], y, idx[2], y, idx[3])
    if m.group(2) == "pd" and len(a) >= 3:
        return "{%s[%d], %s[%d]}" % (ix(a[0]), imm & 1, ix(a[1]), (imm >> 1) & 1)
    return None


def _vec_render(prog, x, used, lanes, depth=0):
    """Operand text with nested MMX/SSE intrinsic calls folded into one infix
    expression, e.g. `v7 & (((*v5 ^ v3) + v4) >> 3)`. Folded calls go into `used`
    so they do not get a note of their own."""
    y = _strip_casts(x)
    if depth < 32 and y.op == "call" and y.x is not None and y.x.op == "helper":
        sub_used, sub_lanes = set(), {}
        sub = [_vec_render(prog, a, sub_used, sub_lanes, depth + 1) for a in y.args]
        r = _vec_text((y.x.helper or "").lstrip("_"), sub, sub_lanes)
        if r is not None:
            used.update(sub_used)
            used.add(y.uid)
            for k, v in sub_lanes.items():
                lanes.setdefault(k, []).extend(s for s in v if s not in lanes.get(k, []))
            return r
    t = _vec_operand(_arg_text(prog, x))
    return t, " " not in t


def _lane_tail(lanes):
    """Plain-language conditions after the expression, e.g. `(+ per byte, no carry
    between bytes)`. Bitwise operators and lanes as wide as the register say
    nothing worth a note, so they get none."""
    parts = []
    groups = []
    for (lane, reg), syms in lanes.items():
        signed = lane.startswith("signed ")
        base = lane[7:] if signed else lane
        bits, en, zh = _LANE_UNIT.get(base, (0, base, base))
        syms = [s for s in syms if s not in _BITWISE_SYMS]
        if syms and (bits < reg or base.startswith("per f")):
            groups.append((signed, base, bits, en, zh, syms))
    for signed, base, bits, en, zh, syms in groups:
        for s in [s for s in syms if s.startswith("clamp ")]:
            parts.append(T("each %s clamped to %s" % (en, s[6:]), "每个 %s饱和到 %s" % (zh, s[6:])))
        syms = [s for s in syms if not s.startswith("clamp ")]
        if not syms:
            continue
        if "swap" in syms:
            parts.append(T("swaps the two bytes of every 16-bit lane", "每个 16 位内交换两个字节") if bits == 16
                         else T("swaps the two halves of every %s" % en, "每个 %s 内交换高低两半" % zh))
            syms = [s for s in syms if s != "swap"]
            if not syms:
                continue
        if len(groups) == 1:
            # a named operation (min, interleave lo, ...) is already in the expression
            syms = [s for s in syms if not re.match(r"^[^\W\d]", s)] or [""]
        ops = ((T("signed ", "有符号 ") if signed else "") + " ".join(syms)).strip()
        zu = "%s%s%s" % (" " if zh[:1].isascii() else "", zh, " " if zh[-1:].isascii() else "")
        txt = T(("%s per %s" % (ops, en)).strip(), ("%s 按%s分别算" % (ops, zu)).strip())
        if "+" in syms or "-" in syms:
            txt += T(", no carry between %ss" % ("byte" if bits == 8 else "lane"), ", 互不进位")
        parts.append(txt)
    return " (%s)" % "; ".join(parts) if parts else ""


def _vec_call_note(prog, e, used):
    """Note for an intrinsic call `e`, or None. Nested foldable intrinsics are
    rendered inline and recorded in `used`."""
    h = (e.x.helper or "").lstrip("_")
    sub_used, lanes = set(), {}
    sub = [_vec_render(prog, a, sub_used, lanes) for a in e.args]
    note = None
    if _vec_op(h):
        r = _vec_text(h, sub, lanes)
        if r is not None:
            if r[0] == "0" and len(sub) >= 2 and sub[0][0] == sub[1][0]:
                note = OPX + "%s ^ %s = 0 (%s)" % (sub[0][0], sub[1][0], T("zeroing", "清零"))
            else:
                note = OPX + _clip(r[0]) + _lane_tail(lanes)
    if note is None:
        note = _intrin_note(e.x.helper, [t for t, _ in sub])
    if note:
        used.update(sub_used)
    return note

```

`src/ibr/core/syscalls.py`:

```py
"""Syscall number -> name, per architecture (generated by tools/gen-syscalls.py)."""
import json

from . import resources

_SYS_CACHE = None


def _syscalls(arch):
    global _SYS_CACHE
    if _SYS_CACHE is None:
        try:
            raw = json.loads(resources.load("syscalls.json"))
            _SYS_CACHE = {}
            for a, s in raw.items():
                d = {}
                for tok in s.split(" "):
                    num, _, nm = tok.partition(":")
                    d[int(num)] = nm
                _SYS_CACHE[a] = d
        except Exception:
            _SYS_CACHE = {}
    return _SYS_CACHE.get(arch, {})

```

`src/ibr/ports.py`:

```py
"""Ports: what the application needs from a disassembler.

core/ and app/ only talk to these interfaces. adapters/ida implements them on top of
idaapi and Hex-Rays; tests implement them with plain Python objects. A method that is
not supported raises NotImplementedError."""


class Expr:
    """One decompiler expression, as the note logic reads it.

    op        "call", "helper", "num", "fnum", "str", "obj", "var", "ref", "cast" or "other"
    x         operand of cast/ref, callee of a call (an Expr), else None
    args      call arguments (list of Expr)
    value     integer value of a "num" (64-bit, as the decompiler stores it)
    size      byte size of the expression's type, 0 if unknown
    is_float  the expression has a floating-point type
    string    text of a "str" literal
    obj_ea    address of an "obj"
    var_name  name of a "var"
    helper    name of a "helper" (intrinsics: _mm_xor_si128, __readfsqword, ...)
    ea        address the expression belongs to
    index     position in the function's item list (identifies a printed line)
    uid       identity of the expression within one decompilation
    """
    op = "other"
    x = None
    args = ()
    value = 0
    size = 0
    is_float = False
    string = ""
    obj_ea = None
    var_name = ""
    helper = ""
    ea = None
    index = None
    uid = None

    def render(self):
        """The decompiler's own text for this expression (no colour tags, one line)."""
        raise NotImplementedError


class Tree:
    """One item of a decompiled function, statement or expression, as the analyses read it.
    A plain data record: adapters build it once per decompilation.

    kind  statements:
            "block"    body = statements
            "expr"     x = the expression
            "if"       x = condition, body = [then] or [then, else]
            "for"      init, x = condition (or None), step, body = [loop body]
            "while"    x = condition, body = [loop body]
            "do"       body = [loop body], x = condition
            "switch"   x = value, body = case statements in order, each with `values`
                       (its case values; none for default)
            "return"   x = value or None
            "goto"     target = label number
            "break", "continue", "other"
          expressions:
            "asg"      x = y; op "" for =, else the operator of a compound assignment ("+", "^", ...)
            "inc", "dec"   ++x / x++ / --x / x--
            "var"      local variable number `var`, `name`
            "obj"      global at obj_ea, `name`, `decl` (its declaration, "" for a function),
                       `text` as the decompiler prints it; a string literal: strlit, `string`;
                       a function: `lib` when it is a library function or an import
            "num"      `value`, and `text` as the decompiler prints it ("0x3Fu")
            "str", "helper" (an intrinsic's name in `name`)
            "call"     x = callee (an "obj" or "helper" with a name), args
            "ptr"      *x
            "idx"      x[y]
            "mem"      x.f (op ".") or x->f (op "->"), the field's name in `name`
            "ref"      &x
            "cast"     (type)x
            "bin"      x op y;  "un"  op x;  "tern"  x ? y : z;  "comma"  x, y
            "other"    args = operands
    label  label number this statement carries (a goto target), or None
    ptr    the expression's type is a pointer
    mem    the expression is memory rather than a value: an array or structure
    index  item number in the printed function: lines carry it (see the adapter)
    """
    kind = "other"
    op = ""
    x = y = z = None
    init = step = None
    args = ()
    body = ()
    label = None
    target = None
    var = None
    name = ""
    ea = None
    index = None
    ptr = False
    mem = False
    value = 0
    text = ""
    string = ""
    obj_ea = None
    decl = ""
    strlit = False
    lib = False
    values = ()

    def __init__(self, kind, **kw):
        self.kind = kind
        for k, v in kw.items():
            setattr(self, k, v)


class Program:
    """The disassembler database."""

    # ---- target ----
    def arch(self):
        """'x86_64', 'i386', 'aarch64', 'arm', a MIPS processor name, ..., or None."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def is_pe(self):
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- bytes ----
    def input_bytes(self):
        """The input file as loaded, or None when it is no longer on disk."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def header_bytes(self):
        """The first page of the image (stands in for the file when it is gone)."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def image_bytes(self):
        """The executable segments, concatenated."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def has_code(self):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def function_heads(self, n):
        """The first n bytes of every function (fallback for image_bytes)."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- functions ----
    def functions(self):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def func_start(self, ea):
        """Start of the function containing ea, or None."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def func_name(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def set_func_name(self, ea, name):
        """Rename; an empty name gives the function back its default name. True on success."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def is_lib(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def clear_lib(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- comments ----
    def func_cmt(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def set_func_cmt(self, ea, text):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def insn_cmt(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def set_insn_cmt(self, ea, text):
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- types ----
    def type_snapshot(self):
        """{ea: (type, user-specified?)} for every function with a stored type. The type is
        opaque to the caller except for str()."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def type_str(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def has_user_type(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def apply_type(self, ea, tif):
        """Set a function's prototype as a user type. True on success."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def restore_type(self, ea, saved):
        """Drop the function's type, then put back `saved` ((type, user?) or None)."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- data ----
    def name_at(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def cstr_bytes(self, ea):
        """Bytes of the C string at ea, or None if ea is code or a non-byte data item."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def is_writable(self, ea):
        raise NotImplementedError

    def is_byte_buffer(self, ea):
        """ea is unformatted or byte data (an empty buffer, not a pointer or int)."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ---- instructions ----
    def func_items(self, ea):
        """Instruction addresses of the function starting at ea."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def insn(self, ea):
        """(mnemonic, operand 0, operand 1, operand 2) as printed."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def wait(self):
        """Let pending auto-analysis finish."""
        raise NotImplementedError


class Signatures:
    """FLIRT signatures and type libraries."""

    def sig_files(self, arch):
        """File names of the installed .sig files for the arch's signature directory."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def apply(self, signame):
        """Apply a signature now (and wait for it)."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def ensure_til(self, arch):
        """Load the arch's libc type library."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def prototype(self, sym):
        """A function type for the symbol from the type libraries, or None."""
        raise NotImplementedError


class Decompiler:
    """Decompiled code: its statement tree, and the comments stored with it."""

    def function_tree(self, func_ea):
        """(body as a Tree "block", {var number: name}) of one function, or None."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, locs):
        """{loc: current text} for the given comment locations of one function."""
        raise NotImplementedError

    def set_pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, texts):
        """Write {loc: text} for one function and save them."""
        raise NotImplementedError
```
