`tests/fakes.py`:

```py
"""Plain-Python stand-ins for the ports, so core/ and app/ run without IDA."""
import itertools

from ibr import ports

_uid = itertools.count(1)


class E(ports.Expr):
    """A decompiler expression. `text` is what render() returns."""

    def __init__(self, op, text="", **kw):
        self.op, self.text, self.uid = op, text, next(_uid)
        for k, v in kw.items():
            setattr(self, k, v)

    def render(self):
        return self.text


def num(v, size=4):
    return E("num", str(v), value=v & ((1 << 64) - 1), size=size)


def var(name, size=8, is_float=False):
    return E("var", name, var_name=name, size=size, is_float=is_float)


def fvar(name):
    return var(name, 8, True)


def string(s):
    return E("str", '"%s"' % s, string=s)


def obj(ea, text=None):
    return E("obj", text or "0x%X" % ea, obj_ea=ea)


def ref(x):
    return E("ref", "&" + x.text, x=x)


def cast(x, text=None):
    return E("cast", text or x.text, x=x)


def other(text):
    """An expression the note logic only knows by its text (a[i], p->f, x + 1 ...)."""
    return E("other", text)


def call(callee_ea, *args, ea=0x401000, index=None):
    return E("call", x=obj(callee_ea), args=list(args), ea=ea, index=index if index is not None else next(_uid))


def hcall(helper, *args, ea=0x401000, index=None):
    return E("call", x=E("helper", helper=helper), args=list(args), ea=ea,
             index=index if index is not None else next(_uid))


def preorder(e):
    """The calls of an expression tree in pre-order, as the IDA adapter yields them."""
    out = []

    def walk(x):
        if x is None:
            return
        if x.op == "call":
            out.append(x)
        if x.op in ("cast", "ref"):
            walk(x.x)
        for a in getattr(x, "args", ()) or ():
            walk(a)
    walk(e)
    return out


class FakeProgram(ports.Program):
    def __init__(self, arch="x86_64", pe=False, names=None, strings=None, writable=(), data_items=()):
        self._arch, self._pe = arch, pe
        self.names = dict(names or {})          # ea -> function name
        self.strings = dict(strings or {})      # ea -> bytes of a C string
        self.writable = set(writable)
        self.data_items = set(data_items)       # formatted non-byte data (pointers, ints)
        self.fcmts, self.icmts, self.types, self.user_types = {}, {}, {}, set()
        self.lib = set()
        self.default_names = {}
        self.items = {}                         # func ea -> [(ea, mnem, d, s, t)]

    def arch(self):
        return self._arch

    def is_pe(self):
        return self._pe

    def has_code(self):
        return True

    def functions(self):
        return sorted(self.names)

    def func_start(self, ea):
        return ea if ea in self.names else None

    def func_name(self, ea):
        return self.names.get(ea, "")

    def set_func_name(self, ea, name):
        self.names[ea] = name or "sub_%X" % ea
        return True

    def is_lib(self, ea):
        return ea in self.lib

    def clear_lib(self, ea):
        self.lib.discard(ea)

    def func_cmt(self, ea):
        return self.fcmts.get(ea, "")

    def set_func_cmt(self, ea, text):
        self.fcmts[ea] = text

    def insn_cmt(self, ea):
        return self.icmts.get(ea, "")

    def set_insn_cmt(self, ea, text):
        self.icmts[ea] = text

    def type_snapshot(self):
        return {ea: (t, ea in self.user_types) for ea, t in self.types.items()}

    def type_str(self, ea):
        return self.types.get(ea)

    def has_user_type(self, ea):
        return ea in self.user_types

    def apply_type(self, ea, tif):
        self.types[ea] = tif
        self.user_types.add(ea)
        return True

    def restore_type(self, ea, saved):
        self.types.pop(ea, None)
        self.user_types.discard(ea)
        if saved is not None:
            self.types[ea] = saved[0]
            if saved[1]:
                self.user_types.add(ea)

    def name_at(self, ea):
        return self.names.get(ea, "")

    def cstr_bytes(self, ea):
        if ea in self.data_items:
            return None
        return self.strings.get(ea)

    def is_writable(self, ea):
        return ea in self.writable

    def is_byte_buffer(self, ea):
        return ea not in self.data_items

    def func_items(self, ea):
        return [i[0] for i in self.items.get(ea, [])]

    def insn(self, ea):
        for items in self.items.values():
            for i in items:
                if i[0] == ea:
                    return i[1:]
        return ("", "", "", "")

    def wait(self):
        pass


class FakeSignatures(ports.Signatures):
    """apply() renames functions as a signature would: {signame: {ea: name}}."""

    def __init__(self, prog, files, effects, protos=None):
        self.prog, self.files, self.effects, self.protos = prog, files, effects, protos or {}

    def sig_files(self, arch):
        return list(self.files)

    def apply(self, signame):
        for ea, name in self.effects.get(signame, {}).items():
            self.prog.names[ea] = name
            self.prog.lib.add(ea)
            self.prog.fcmts.setdefault(ea, "")

    def ensure_til(self, arch):
        pass

    def prototype(self, sym):
        return self.protos.get(sym)


class FakeDecompiler(ports.Decompiler):
    def __init__(self):
        self.cmts = {}                          # (func_ea, (ea, itp)) -> text

    def pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, locs):
        return {loc: self.cmts.get((func_ea, loc), "") for loc in locs}

    def set_pseudo_comments(self, func_ea, texts):
        for loc, t in texts.items():
            self.cmts[(func_ea, loc)] = t

```

`tests/test_architecture.py`:

```py
"""The dependency rule of the ports-and-adapters layout, checked on the import statements."""
import ast
import os

PKG = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "src", "ibr")
IDA = ("ida", "idaapi", "idautils", "idc", "PyQt5", "PySide6", "sip")
# layer -> layers it may import
ALLOWED = {"core": {"core"}, "app": {"core", "app", "ports"}, "analysis": {"core", "ports", "analysis"},
           "ports": {"ports"}}


def imports(path):
    """(top-level module, ibr layer or None) for every import in the file."""
    tree = ast.parse(open(path, encoding="utf-8").read())
    rel = os.path.relpath(path, PKG).replace(os.sep, "/").split("/")
    out = []
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            out += [(a.name.split(".")[0], None) for a in node.names]
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            if node.level == 0:
                out.append((node.module.split(".")[0], None))
                continue
            base = rel[:-1][:len(rel) - 1 - (node.level - 1)] if node.level > 1 else rel[:-1]
            target = (base + (node.module or "").split(".")) if node.module else base
            target = [t for t in target if t]
            if not target:                    # `from .. import x` at the package root
                target = [a.name for a in node.names][:1]
            out.append(("ibr", target[0].replace(".py", "")))
    return out


def test_inner_layers_do_not_reach_outwards():
    bad = []
    for dirpath, _, files in os.walk(PKG):
        for fn in files:
            if not fn.endswith(".py"):
                continue
            path = os.path.join(dirpath, fn)
            rel = os.path.relpath(path, PKG).replace(os.sep, "/")
            layer = rel.split("/")[0].replace(".py", "")
            if layer not in ALLOWED:
                continue
            for mod, ibr_layer in imports(path):
                if mod.startswith(IDA) or (ibr_layer is not None and ibr_layer not in ALLOWED[layer]
                                           and ibr_layer != "__init__"):
                    bad.append("%s imports %s" % (rel, ibr_layer or mod))
    assert not bad, bad

```

`tests/test_binfmt.py`:

```py
"""Header parsing and detection on synthetic and (when present) real binaries."""
import os
import struct

import pytest

from ibr.core.binfmt import _elf_layout
from ibr.core.detect import detect
from ibr.core.fingerprint import _load_fp


def elf64(machine=62):
    h = bytearray(0x40)
    h[:7] = b"\x7fELF\x02\x01\x01"
    struct.pack_into("<HH", h, 16, 2, machine)          # ET_EXEC, e_machine
    struct.pack_into("<HHH", h, 0x34, 64, 56, 0)        # e_ehsize, e_phentsize, e_phnum
    return bytes(h)


def test_consistent_header():
    assert _elf_layout(elf64()) == (True, "<", None)


def test_ei_class_alone_tampered():
    h = bytearray(elf64()); h[4] = 1
    is64, en, note = _elf_layout(bytes(h))
    assert (is64, en) == (True, "<")
    assert "claim 32-bit little-endian" in note and "64-bit little-endian x86_64" in note


def test_ei_data_tampered():
    h = bytearray(elf64()); h[5] = 2
    is64, en, note = _elf_layout(bytes(h))
    assert (is64, en) == (True, "<") and "set file byte 4 to 2 and byte 5 to 1" in note


def test_short_and_foreign_inputs():
    assert _elf_layout(b"\x7fELF\x02")[2] is None
    assert detect(data=b"hello")["notes"] == ["not an ELF/PE file"]


def test_fingerprint_db_loads():
    assert {"x86_64", "i386", "aarch64"} <= set(_load_fp())


SAMPLE = r"D:\CTF\tool\glibc-detect\devtools\samples\t_x86s"


@pytest.mark.skipif(not os.path.exists(SAMPLE), reason="sample binaries not present")
def test_static_glibc_sample():
    r = detect(path=SAMPLE)
    assert r["kind"] == "static" and r["best"].startswith("glibc") and r["confidence"] in ("high", "medium")

```

`tests/test_comments_asm.py`:

```py
"""Comment bookkeeping and disassembly math."""
from ibr.core.asm_math import insn_math
from ibr.core.comments import _strip_managed


def test_strip_keeps_user_text():
    assert _strip_managed("libc: allocate heap") == ""
    assert _strip_managed("libc: allocate heap\nmy note") == "my note"
    assert _strip_managed("op: eax = 0  | key loop") == "key loop"
    # a `  | ` inside a quoted string is part of the note, not a join
    assert _strip_managed('libc: printf "a  | b"  | mine') == "mine"
    assert _strip_managed("my own  | text") == "my own  | text"


def test_x86_math():
    assert insn_math("xor", "eax", "eax", "", "x86_64") == "eax = 0"
    assert insn_math("add", "rax", "rbx", "", "x86_64") == "rax = rax + rbx"
    assert insn_math("lea", "rax", "[rbx+rcx*4+8]", "", "x86_64") == "rax = rbx+rcx*4+8"
    assert insn_math("imul", "eax", "", "7", "i386") == "eax = eax * 7"
    assert insn_math("mov", "eax", "ebx", "", "x86_64") is None


def test_three_operand_math():
    assert insn_math("ADD", "X0", "X2", "X20", "aarch64") == "X0 = X2 + X20"
    assert insn_math("add", "X0", "X1", "", "aarch64") == "X0 = X0 + X1"
    assert insn_math("bic", "W0", "W1", "W2", "aarch64") == "W0 = W1 & ~W2"
    assert insn_math("ADD", "X0", "X1", "X2,LSL#3", "aarch64") == "X0 = X1 + (X2 << 3)"
    assert insn_math("addiu", "$sp", "-0x20", "", "mipsb") == "$sp = $sp - 0x20"
    assert insn_math("xor", "$v0", "$a0", "$a0", "mipsl") == "$v0 = 0"

```

`tests/test_dataflow.py`:

```py
"""Data flow of one variable (analysis/dataflow.py) on hand-built statement trees."""
import itertools

from ibr.analysis.dataflow import var_flow
from ibr.ports import Tree

_ids = itertools.count(1)


def var(n, ptr=False, mem=False):
    return Tree("var", var=n, name="v%d" % n, ptr=ptr, mem=mem)


def num(v):
    return Tree("num", value=v)


def asg(x, y, op=""):
    return Tree("asg", x=x, y=y, op=op)


def call(name, *args, ptr=False):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("obj", name=name, obj_ea=0x1000 + len(name)), args=list(args),
                ptr=ptr, index=next(_ids))


def bin_(op, x, y):
    return Tree("bin", op=op, x=x, y=y)


def deref(p):
    return Tree("ptr", x=p)


def st(e, label=None):
    return Tree("expr", x=e, label=label)


def block(*body):
    return Tree("block", body=list(body))


def kept(flow):
    """{statement: role} by the statement objects themselves."""
    return {s.stmt: flow.role[s.id] for s in flow.sites if s.role != "join"}


def test_a_reused_variable_stays_apart():
    a = st(asg(var(0), call("atoi", Tree("str", string="7", index=next(_ids)))))
    b = st(asg(var(1), bin_("^", var(0), num(5))))
    c = st(asg(var(0), num(3)))
    d = st(asg(var(2), bin_("*", var(0), num(2))))
    got = kept(var_flow(block(a, b, c, d), var=1))
    assert got == {a: "src", b: "self"}


def test_memory_through_a_copied_pointer():
    m = st(asg(var(0, ptr=True), call("malloc", num(16), ptr=True)))
    r = st(call("read", num(0), var(0, ptr=True), num(16)))
    q = st(asg(var(1, ptr=True), var(0, ptr=True)))
    x = st(asg(var(2), bin_("^", deref(var(1, ptr=True)), num(0x11))))
    n = st(asg(var(3), num(5)))
    got = kept(var_flow(block(m, r, q, x, n), var=2))
    assert got == {m: "src", r: "src", x: "self"}       # the pointer copy itself is not data


def test_loop_body_and_where_the_buffer_goes():
    buf, p, f = var(0, ptr=True), var(1, ptr=True), var(5, ptr=True)
    m = st(asg(buf, call("malloc", num(64), ptr=True)))
    rd = st(call("fread", buf, num(1), num(64), f))
    cp = st(asg(p, buf))
    key = st(asg(var(2), num(0x1234)))
    x = st(asg(var(3), bin_("^", deref(p), var(2))))
    y = st(asg(var(4), bin_("<<", var(3), num(1))))
    store = st(asg(deref(p), bin_("+", var(4), var(2))))
    inc = st(Tree("inc", x=p, op="pre"))
    loop = Tree("do", body=[block(x, y, store, inc)], x=bin_("!=", p, var(6, ptr=True)))
    out = st(call("write", num(1), buf, num(64)))
    done = st(call("puts", Tree("str", string="done", index=next(_ids))))
    flow = var_flow(block(m, rd, cp, key, loop, out, done), var=3)
    got = kept(flow)
    assert got[x] == "self" and got[y] == "self" and got[store] == "dst" and got[out] == "dst"
    assert got[rd] == "src" and got[key] == "src" and got[m] == "src"
    assert cp not in got and inc not in got and done not in got
    assert not flow.may
    # from v4's side, v3 is where it comes from, although the loop also carries v4 into v3
    got = kept(var_flow(block(m, rd, cp, key, loop, out, done), var=4))
    assert got[x] == "src" and got[y] == "self" and got[store] == "self" and got[out] == "dst"


def test_both_writes_reach_after_a_break():
    one = st(asg(var(0), num(1)))
    brk = Tree("if", x=var(9), body=[Tree("break")])
    two = st(asg(var(0), num(2)))
    loop = Tree("while", x=var(8), body=[block(brk, two)])
    use = st(asg(var(1), var(0)))
    got = kept(var_flow(block(one, loop, use), var=1))
    assert got == {one: "src", two: "src", use: "self"}


def test_goto_and_dead_code():
    one = st(asg(var(0), num(1)))
    jump = Tree("goto", target=7)
    two = st(asg(var(0), num(2)))                        # dead: right after the goto
    use = st(asg(var(1), var(0)), label=7)
    got = kept(var_flow(block(one, jump, two, use), var=1))
    assert got == {one: "src", use: "self"}


def test_unknown_call_may_change_memory():
    b = var(0, mem=True)
    init = st(call("memset", b, num(0), num(16)))
    unk = st(call("sub_401000", b))
    use = st(asg(var(1), Tree("idx", x=b, y=num(3))))
    flow = var_flow(block(init, unk, use), var=1)
    got = kept(flow)
    assert got == {init: "src", unk: "src", use: "self"} and flow.may


def test_a_variable_with_no_writes_shows_where_it_goes():
    use = st(asg(var(1), bin_("+", var(0), num(1))))
    other = st(asg(var(2), num(4)))
    got = kept(var_flow(block(use, other), var=0))
    assert got == {use: "self"}


def test_graph_keeps_layers_clean():
    from ibr.analysis.dataflow import Flow, flow_graph

    class S:
        def __init__(self, i):
            self.id, self.role = i, "stmt"
    sites = [S(i) for i in range(4)]
    # 0 -> 1 -> 2 and a direct 0 -> 2; 3 -> 1 goes back in program order (a loop)
    flow = Flow(sites, [(0, 1), (1, 2), (0, 2), (3, 1), (2, 3)], {}, False, False)
    ids, edges = flow_graph(flow)
    assert ids == [0, 1, 2, 3] and edges == [(0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 3)]

```

`tests/test_expr.py`:

```py
"""Expressions as C and as math (analysis/expr.py) on hand-built statement trees."""
import itertools

from ibr.analysis.dataflow import analyze
from ibr.analysis.expr import Terms, _mtext, _lane_tag, _wrap, c_text
from ibr.core.i18n import set_lang
from ibr.ports import Tree

_ids = itertools.count(1)


def var(n, ptr=False, mem=False):
    return Tree("var", var=n, name="v%d" % n, ptr=ptr, mem=mem)


def num(v, text=""):
    return Tree("num", value=v, text=text)


def s_(t):
    return Tree("str", string=t, index=next(_ids))


def ref(x):
    return Tree("ref", x=x)


def asg(x, y, op=""):
    return Tree("asg", x=x, y=y, op=op)


def call(name, *args):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("obj", name=name, obj_ea=0x1000 + len(name)), args=list(args),
                index=next(_ids))


def helper(name, *args):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("helper", name=name), args=list(args), index=next(_ids))


def bin_(op, x, y):
    return Tree("bin", op=op, x=x, y=y)


def st(e):
    return Tree("expr", x=e)


def block(*body):
    return Tree("block", body=list(body))


def math(e):
    """The term of e, read at the statement that holds it."""
    an = analyze(block(st(e)))
    s = next(s for s in an.sites if s.expr is not None and s.role != "join")
    m = Terms(an).norm(e, s)
    return _mtext(m) + (_lane_tag(m) if hasattr(m, "lane") else "")


def setup_module():
    set_lang("en")


def test_arithmetic_is_written_as_math():
    assert math(asg(var(2), bin_("+", bin_("*", var(1), num(3)), num(7)))) == "v1 * 3 + 7"
    assert math(asg(var(2), bin_("*", bin_("+", var(1), num(3)), num(7)))) == "(v1 + 3) * 7"


def test_a_shift_pair_is_a_rotation():
    e = asg(var(1), bin_("|", bin_("<<", var(0), num(3)), bin_(">>", var(0), num(29, "29"))))
    assert math(e) == "v0 <<< 3"
    # not a rotation when the widths do not add up
    e = asg(var(1), bin_("|", bin_("<<", var(0), num(3)), bin_(">>", var(0), num(20, "20"))))
    assert math(e) == "v0 << 3 | v0 >> 20"


def test_intrinsics_are_folded_with_their_lane_width():
    assert math(asg(var(2), helper("_m_pxor", var(0), var(1)))) == "v0 ^ v1"
    assert math(asg(var(2), helper("_m_paddb", var(0), var(1)))) == "v0 + v1 (per byte)"
    assert math(asg(var(2), helper("_m_pxor", var(0), var(0)))) == "0"


def test_printf_shows_only_what_its_format_prints():
    e = call("__printf_chk", num(2), s_("size %d\n"), var(3), var(4), var(5))
    assert c_text(e) == '__printf_chk("size %d\\n", v3)'


def test_a_long_condition_breaks_before_and_or():
    inner = " && ".join("v%d == 0x%X" % (i, 0x1000 + i) for i in range(6))
    lines = _wrap("if ( ", inner, " )")
    assert len(lines) == 6 and lines[0] == "if ( v0 == 0x1000" and lines[-1] == "  && v5 == 0x1005 )"

```

`tests/test_flowchart.py`:

```py
"""The program flowchart (analysis/flowchart.py, core/chartlayout.py) on hand-built trees."""
import itertools
import random

from ibr.analysis.dataflow import analyze, var_flow
from ibr.analysis.flowchart import build_chart, lit, marks
from ibr.core import chartlayout as cl
from ibr.core.i18n import set_lang
from ibr.ports import Tree

_ids = itertools.count(1)


def var(n, ptr=False, mem=False):
    return Tree("var", var=n, name="v%d" % n, ptr=ptr, mem=mem, index=next(_ids))


def num(v):
    return Tree("num", value=v, text=str(v))


def s_(t):
    return Tree("str", string=t, index=next(_ids))


def asg(x, y, op=""):
    return Tree("asg", x=x, y=y, op=op)


def call(name, *args, lib=True):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("obj", name=name, obj_ea=0x1000 + len(name), lib=lib), args=list(args),
                index=next(_ids))


def helper(name, *args):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("helper", name=name), args=list(args), index=next(_ids))


def bin_(op, x, y):
    return Tree("bin", op=op, x=x, y=y)


def st(e):
    return Tree("expr", x=e, ea=next(_ids))


def block(*body):
    return Tree("block", body=list(body))


def iff(c, then, other=None):
    return Tree("if", x=c, body=[then] + ([other] if other is not None else []), ea=next(_ids))


def ret(e=None):
    return Tree("return", x=e, ea=next(_ids))


def setup_module():
    set_lang("en")


def texts(chart):
    return [(chart.boxes[l.box].kind, l.text) for l in chart.lines]


def overlaps(pos, sizes):
    bad = []
    ks = list(pos)
    for i, a in enumerate(ks):
        for b in ks[i + 1:]:
            (x1, y1), (x2, y2) = pos[a], pos[b]
            (w1, h1), (w2, h2) = sizes[a], sizes[b]
            if x1 < x2 + w2 - 0.5 and x2 < x1 + w1 - 0.5 and y1 < y2 + h2 - 0.5 and y2 < y1 + h1 - 0.5:
                bad.append((a, b))
    return bad


def sizes_of(chart):
    out = {}
    for b in chart.boxes:
        w = max(len(t) for l in b.lines for t in l.text.split("\n")) * 8 + 24
        h = sum(len(l.text.split("\n")) for l in b.lines) * 18 + 12
        if b.kind in ("decision", "switch"):
            w, h = w * 1.6, h + 30
        out[b.id] = (w, h)
    return out


def butterfly():
    """The shape of Butterfly's encoder: read a file, a guard, a do-while over 8-byte words
    with a break, a check that prints."""
    v18, v19, v28 = var(18, ptr=True), var(19, ptr=True), var(28, ptr=True)
    body = block(
        st(asg(var(18, ptr=True), call("sub_412620", bin_("+", var(7), num(8)), lib=False))),
        st(asg(var(19, ptr=True), v18)),
        st(asg(var(20), call("fread", var(18, ptr=True), num(1), var(7), var(16)))),
        st(call("fclose", var(16))),
        iff(bin_("!=", var(7), var(20)), block(st(call("puts", s_("Error"))), ret(num(1)))),
        st(asg(var(28, ptr=True), var(19, ptr=True))),
        Tree("do", x=bin_("!=", var(28, ptr=True), var(21, ptr=True)), ea=next(_ids), body=[block(
            st(asg(var(29), helper("_m_pxor", Tree("ptr", x=var(28, ptr=True)), var(42)))),
            st(asg(Tree("ptr", x=var(28, ptr=True)), helper("_m_paddb", var(29), var(42)))),
            iff(bin_("==", var(28, ptr=True), var(22, ptr=True)), Tree("break")),
            st(Tree("inc", op="pre", x=var(28, ptr=True))),
        )]),
        st(helper("_m_empty")),
        iff(call("sub_401CA0", var(11), var(19, ptr=True), var(7), lib=False),
            block(st(call("printf", s_("Successfully encoded to: %s\n"), var(11), var(31))),
                  st(call("snprintf", var(42, mem=True), num(256), s_("%s.key"), var(11))))),
        ret(num(0)),
    )
    return body, v28


def test_statements_in_order_with_math_and_shapes():
    body, _ = butterfly()
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body), title="f()")
    assert texts(chart) == [
        ("start", "f()"),
        ("call", "v18 = sub_412620(v7 + 8)"),
        ("process", "v19 = v18"),
        ("io", "v20 = fread(v18, 1, v7, v16)"),
        ("decision", "v7 != v20"),
        ("io", 'puts("Error")'),
        ("return", "return 1"),
        ("process", "v28 = v19"),
        ("decision", "v28 != v21"),               # a do-while's condition: made before its body
        ("process", "v29 = *v28 ^ v42"),
        ("process", "*v28 = v29 + v42 (per byte)"),
        ("decision", "v28 == v22"),
        ("process", "++v28"),
        ("decision", "sub_401CA0(v11, v19, v7)"),
        ("io", 'printf("Successfully encoded to: %s\\n", v11)'),
        ("process", 'snprintf(v42, 256, "%s.key", v11)'),
        ("return", "return 0"),
        ("end", "End"),
    ]
    # the two steps of the loop body share one box; fclose and _m_empty are not drawn
    loop = [b for b in chart.boxes if b.kind == "process" and len(b.lines) == 2]
    assert len(loop) == 1


def test_a_pointer_shows_where_it_points():
    body, v28 = butterfly()
    an = analyze(body)
    chart = build_chart(body, an)
    on = {chart.lines[i].text for i in lit(chart, an, var_flow(body, var=28, an=an), 28)}
    for t in ("v28 = v19", "v19 = v18", "v18 = sub_412620(v7 + 8)",      # where it points
              "v20 = fread(v18, 1, v7, v16)",                             # what is there
              "v29 = *v28 ^ v42", "*v28 = v29 + v42 (per byte)", "++v28",
              "v28 != v21", "v28 == v22",
              "sub_401CA0(v11, v19, v7)",                                 # where it goes
              'printf("Successfully encoded to: %s\\n", v11)'):           # what that prints
        assert t in on, t
    assert 'puts("Error")' not in on
    assert 'snprintf(v42, 256, "%s.key", v11)' not in on      # writes a buffer: not a print


def by_line(chart, m):
    return {chart.lines[lid].text: kinds for lid, kinds in m.items()}


def test_names_computed_from_it_and_what_it_comes_from():
    """An in-place encryption: buf = malloc; fread(buf); p = buf; do { t = *p ^ k;
    *p = t + k; ++p } while (p != end); puts(buf). Names are marked line by line. Following
    the key k marks what is computed from it: t, the buffer written through p, read by puts.
    Following t marks, on its own line and above, what makes it (k, the buffer p points at,
    filled from the file), and below what it makes; the buffer written back below is not a
    source, though the next pass reads it."""
    p, buf = (lambda: var(4, ptr=True)), (lambda: var(1, ptr=True))
    body = block(
        st(asg(buf(), call("malloc", var(2)))),
        st(call("fread", buf(), num(1), var(2), var(6, ptr=True))),
        st(asg(var(3), num(0x5A))),
        st(asg(p(), buf())),
        Tree("do", x=bin_("!=", p(), var(7, ptr=True)), ea=next(_ids), body=[block(
            st(asg(var(5), bin_("^", Tree("ptr", x=p()), var(3)))),
            st(asg(Tree("ptr", x=p()), bin_("+", var(5), var(3)))),
            st(Tree("inc", op="pre", x=p())),
        )]),
        st(call("puts", buf())),
    )
    an = analyze(body)
    chart = build_chart(body, an)

    def around(n):
        return by_line(chart, marks(chart, an, var_flow(body, var=n, an=an, may=True), n))
    assert around(3) == {"v5 = *v4 ^ v3": {"v5": "into"},
                         "*v4 = v5 + v3": {"v4": "into", "v5": "into"},
                         "puts(v1)": {"v1": "into"}}
    assert around(5) == {"fread(v1, 1, v2, v6)": {"v1": "from", "v6": "from"},
                         "v3 = 90": {"v3": "from"},
                         "v5 = *v4 ^ v3": {"v4": "from", "v3": "from"},
                         "*v4 = v5 + v3": {"v4": "into"},
                         "puts(v1)": {"v1": "into"}}
    # the followed pointer's memory: t is made from it, then makes it again
    assert around(4)["v5 = *v4 ^ v3"] == {"v5": "both", "v3": "from"}


def test_a_source_used_again_later_is_not_marked_there():
    """As in Butterfly: the sources of v29 appear again after it (the loop's check, a call,
    a print). There they make nothing that v29 is made from: not marked. The call that reads
    the buffer v29 went into marks that buffer's pointer as where it goes."""
    P = lambda n: var(n, ptr=True)
    body = block(
        st(asg(P(19), call("malloc", var(7)))),
        st(call("fread", P(19), num(1), var(7), P(6))),
        st(asg(var(3), num(0x5A))),
        st(asg(P(28), P(19))),
        Tree("do", x=bin_("!=", P(28), P(8)), ea=next(_ids), body=[block(
            st(asg(var(29), bin_("^", Tree("ptr", x=P(28)), var(3)))),
            st(asg(Tree("ptr", x=P(28)), bin_("+", var(29), var(3)))),
            st(Tree("inc", op="pre", x=P(28))),
        )]),
        st(call("check", P(19), var(7), lib=False)),
        st(call("printf", s_("%d %d"), var(7), var(3))),
    )
    an = analyze(body)
    chart = build_chart(body, an)
    got = by_line(chart, marks(chart, an, var_flow(body, var=29, an=an, may=True), 29))
    assert got == {"fread(v19, 1, v7, v6)": {"v19": "from", "v6": "from"},
                   "v3 = 90": {"v3": "from"},
                   "v29 = *v28 ^ v3": {"v28": "from", "v3": "from"},
                   "*v28 = v29 + v3": {"v28": "into"},
                   "check(v19, v7)": {"v19": "into"}}


def test_a_count_does_not_make_what_a_call_fills():
    """fread(v18, 1, v7, f): the buffer comes from the file, not from the count v7."""
    body = block(
        st(asg(var(18, ptr=True), call("malloc", var(7)))),
        st(asg(var(20), call("fread", var(18, ptr=True), num(1), var(7), var(16, ptr=True)))),
        st(asg(var(30), Tree("ptr", x=var(18, ptr=True)))),
        st(call("puts", var(30))),
    )
    an = analyze(body)
    chart = build_chart(body, an)
    count = by_line(chart, marks(chart, an, var_flow(body, var=7, an=an, may=True), 7))
    assert all("v30" not in m and "v18" not in m for m in count.values()), count
    data = by_line(chart, marks(chart, an, var_flow(body, var=30, an=an, may=True), 30))
    assert data == {"v20 = fread(v18, 1, v7, v16)": {"v18": "from", "v16": "from"},   # the buffer, the file
                    "v30 = *v18": {"v18": "from"}}


def test_through_unknown_calls_to_the_check_and_back_to_the_input():
    """babyre: getline(Str); memset(buf); sub_1(buf, Str); sub_2(buf, key); a loop compares
    key with the answer and prints "No" through an unnamed printf. Following buf lights the
    input it is made from and the check it ends in."""
    buf, key, ans, k = var(1, mem=True), var(2, mem=True), var(3, mem=True), var(4)
    idx = lambda a, i: Tree("idx", x=a, y=i)
    body = block(
        st(call("getline", var(5, mem=True), num(33), lib=False)),
        iff(bin_("==", call("strlen", var(5, mem=True)), num(32)), block(
            st(call("memset", var(1, mem=True), num(0), num(32))),
            st(call("sub_1", var(1, mem=True), var(5, mem=True), lib=False)),
            st(call("sub_2", var(1, mem=True), var(2, mem=True), lib=False)),
            Tree("for", init=asg(var(4), num(0)), x=bin_("<", var(4), num(32)),
                 step=Tree("inc", op="pre", x=var(4)), ea=next(_ids), body=[block(
                     iff(bin_("!=", idx(var(3, mem=True), var(4)), idx(var(2, mem=True), var(4))),
                         block(st(call("sub_3", s_("No"), lib=False)), ret(num(0)))))]),
            st(call("sub_3", s_("Yes"), lib=False)),
            ret(num(0))),
            block(st(call("sub_3", s_("Wrong length"), lib=False)), ret(num(1)))),
    )
    an = analyze(body)
    chart = build_chart(body, an)
    flow = var_flow(body, var=1, an=an, may=True)
    on = {chart.lines[i].text for i in lit(chart, an, flow, 1)}
    for t in ("getline(v5, 33)", "sub_1(v1, v5)", "sub_2(v1, v2)", "v3[v4] != v2[v4]",
              'sub_3("No")', 'sub_3("Yes")', "return 0", "strlen(v5) == 32"):
        assert t in on, t
    assert 'sub_3("Wrong length")' not in on                   # the other branch of the if


def test_layout_never_overlaps_and_the_main_line_is_straight():
    body, _ = butterfly()
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    sizes = sizes_of(chart)
    pos, paths, (w, h) = chart.layout(sizes)
    assert overlaps(pos, sizes) == []
    centre = lambda b: pos[b][0] + sizes[b][0] / 2
    main = [b.id for b in chart.boxes if b.kind in ("start", "call") or b.lines[0].text == "v28 = v19"]
    assert len({round(centre(b)) for b in main}) == 1
    for p in paths:                               # only horizontal and vertical lines
        for (x1, y1), (x2, y2) in zip(p.pts, p.pts[1:]):
            assert abs(x1 - x2) < 0.01 or abs(y1 - y2) < 0.01
    # the error branch sits on the right of its condition, labelled "yes"
    cond = next(b for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text == "v7 != v20")
    err = next(b for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text == 'puts("Error")')
    assert pos[err.id][0] > pos[cond.id][0] + sizes[cond.id][0] / 2
    assert any(p.src == cond.id and p.label == "yes" and p.dst == err.id for p in paths)


def test_the_way_to_a_step():
    """A click on the success print: the way there (the main line, the loop, the check's
    yes), ending there: no error branch that returns, no check's no, nothing after it."""
    body, _ = butterfly()
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    sizes = sizes_of(chart)
    pos, paths, _ = chart.layout(sizes)
    box = lambda t: next(b.id for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text.startswith(t))
    goal, check = box('printf("Successfully'), box("sub_401CA0")
    on, lines = cl.route(paths, chart.boxes[0].id, goal, chart.jumps)
    for t in ("v18 = sub_412620", "v7 != v20", "v28 = v19", "v29 = *v28", "v28 == v22", "++v28",
              "v28 != v21", "sub_401CA0"):
        assert box(t) in on, t
    assert box('puts("Error")') not in on
    assert box("return 0") not in on                           # after it: not part of the way
    taken = {(paths[i].src, paths[i].label) for i in lines}
    assert (check, "yes") in taken and (check, "no") not in taken
    assert (box("v7 != v20"), "no") in taken and (box("v7 != v20"), "yes") not in taken


def test_a_goto_leads_to_its_label():
    body = block(st(asg(var(1), num(0))), Tree("goto", target=7),
                 Tree("expr", x=asg(var(2), num(1)), label=7, ea=next(_ids)), ret(var(2)))
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    pos, paths, _ = chart.layout(sizes_of(chart))
    goal = next(b.id for b in chart.boxes if b.kind == "return")
    on, _ = cl.route(paths, chart.boxes[0].id, goal, chart.jumps)
    assert next(b.id for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text == "v1 = 0") in on


def test_loops_go_back_on_the_left_and_out_on_the_right():
    body, _ = butterfly()
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    sizes = sizes_of(chart)
    pos, paths, _ = chart.layout(sizes)
    cond = next(b for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text == "v28 != v21")
    back = [p for p in paths if p.src == cond.id and p.label == "yes"]
    assert len(back) == 1 and min(x for x, _ in back[0].pts) < pos[cond.id][0]
    brk = next(b for b in chart.boxes if b.lines[0].text == "v28 == v22")
    out = [p for p in paths if p.src == brk.id and p.label == "yes"]
    assert len(out) == 1 and max(x for x, _ in out[0].pts) > pos[brk.id][0] + sizes[brk.id][0]


def random_body(rnd, depth=0):
    items = []
    for _ in range(rnd.randint(1, 4)):
        r = rnd.random()
        if depth < 3 and r < 0.2:
            items.append(iff(bin_("<", var(1), num(rnd.randint(0, 9))), random_body(rnd, depth + 1),
                             random_body(rnd, depth + 1) if rnd.random() < 0.5 else None))
        elif depth < 3 and r < 0.3:
            items.append(Tree("while", x=bin_("<", var(2), num(5)), body=[random_body(rnd, depth + 1)]))
        elif depth < 3 and r < 0.4:
            items.append(Tree("do", x=bin_("<", var(2), num(5)), body=[random_body(rnd, depth + 1)]))
        elif depth < 3 and r < 0.45:
            items.append(Tree("for", init=asg(var(3), num(0)), x=bin_("<", var(3), num(9)),
                              step=Tree("inc", op="post", x=var(3)), body=[random_body(rnd, depth + 1)]))
        elif depth < 3 and r < 0.5:
            cases = []
            for k in range(rnd.randint(1, 4)):
                c = random_body(rnd, depth + 1)
                if rnd.random() < 0.7:
                    c.body.append(Tree("break"))
                c.values = [k] if k else []
                cases.append(c)
            items.append(Tree("switch", x=var(4), body=cases))
        elif r < 0.55 and depth:
            items.append(Tree(rnd.choice(["break", "continue"])))
        elif r < 0.6:
            items.append(ret(num(1)))
        elif r < 0.75:
            items.append(st(call("printf", s_("x" * rnd.randint(1, 50)), var(1))))
        else:
            items.append(st(asg(var(rnd.randint(1, 5)), bin_("+", var(1), num(rnd.randint(0, 99))))))
    return block(*items)


def through(a, b, r):
    """Segment a-b (horizontal or vertical) passes through rectangle r."""
    (x1, y1), (x2, y2) = a, b
    x0, y0, x3, y3 = r
    if abs(y1 - y2) < 0.01:
        return y0 < y1 < y3 and max(min(x1, x2), x0) < min(max(x1, x2), x3)
    return x0 < x1 < x3 and max(min(y1, y2), y0) < min(max(y1, y2), y3)


def test_random_functions_never_overlap():
    rnd = random.Random(11)
    for _ in range(150):
        body = random_body(rnd)
        chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
        sizes = sizes_of(chart)
        pos, paths, _ = chart.layout(sizes)
        assert overlaps(pos, sizes) == []
        for p in paths:
            assert p.src is not None
            for (x1, y1), (x2, y2) in zip(p.pts, p.pts[1:]):
                assert abs(x1 - x2) < 0.01 or abs(y1 - y2) < 0.01
            for k, (x, y) in pos.items():          # no line through a shape it does not join
                if k not in (p.src, p.dst):
                    r = (x + 1, y + 1, x + sizes[k][0] - 1, y + sizes[k][1] - 1)
                    assert not any(through(a, b, r) for a, b in zip(p.pts, p.pts[1:]))


def test_a_switch_branches_from_the_left():
    cases = [block(st(asg(var(1), num(5))), Tree("break")), block(st(asg(var(1), num(6))))]
    cases[0].values, cases[1].values = [1, 2], []
    body = block(Tree("switch", x=var(4), body=cases))
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    sizes = sizes_of(chart)
    pos, paths, _ = chart.layout(sizes)
    labels = {p.label for p in paths if p.label}
    assert {"case 1, 2", "default"} <= labels

```

`tests/test_nest.py`:

```py
"""Charts opened inside calls: which shape opens which function (analysis/flowchart.py) and
where the panel goes at which zoom (core/nest.py)."""
import itertools

from ibr.analysis.dataflow import analyze
from ibr.analysis.flowchart import build_chart
from ibr.core import nest
from ibr.core.i18n import set_lang
from ibr.ports import Tree

_ids = itertools.count(1)


def var(n, ptr=False):
    return Tree("var", var=n, name="v%d" % n, ptr=ptr, index=next(_ids))


def call(name, *args, lib=False, ea=None):
    return Tree("call", x=Tree("obj", name=name, obj_ea=ea or 0x1000 + len(name), lib=lib),
                args=list(args), index=next(_ids))


def st(e):
    return Tree("expr", x=e, ea=next(_ids))


def setup_module():
    set_lang("en")


def test_the_shape_of_a_call_opens_its_function():
    """A statement, an assignment and a condition that call the program's own function open
    it, with the argument variables (&buf counts as buf); a library call does not."""
    body = Tree("block", body=[
        st(call("encrypt", var(1, ptr=True), Tree("ref", x=var(2)), Tree("num", value=8, text="8"),
                ea=0x401000)),
        st(Tree("asg", x=var(3), y=call("check", var(1, ptr=True), ea=0x402000))),
        Tree("if", x=call("verify", var(3), ea=0x403000), body=[
            Tree("block", body=[st(call("puts", Tree("str", string="ok", index=next(_ids)), lib=True))])],
             ea=next(_ids)),
    ])
    chart = build_chart(body, analyze(body))
    opens = {b.lines[0].text: b.callee().call for b in chart.boxes if b.callee() is not None}
    assert opens == {"encrypt(v1, &v2, 8)": (0x401000, (1, 2, None)),
                     "v3 = check(v1)": (0x402000, (1,)),
                     "verify(v3)": (0x403000, (3,))}, opens


def test_a_panel_hangs_under_its_call_and_holds_the_chart_at_its_width():
    x, y, w, h, s = nest.panel((100, 50, 300, 34), (1200, 3000))
    assert (x, w) == (100, 300) and y == 50 + 34 + nest.GAP     # the call's text stays in sight
    assert abs(s - (300 - 2 * nest.PAD) / 1200) < 1e-9 and abs(h - (3000 * s + 2 * nest.PAD)) < 1e-9
    # a narrow call: the panel is MIN_W wide, centred on it
    x, y, w, h, s = nest.panel((100, 50, 60, 34), (1200, 3000))
    assert w == nest.MIN_W and abs((x + w / 2) - 130) < 1e-9
    # a small chart is not drawn larger than on its own
    x, y, w, h, s = nest.panel((0, 0, 300, 34), (150, 200))
    assert s == 1.0 and w == 300 and h == 200 + 2 * nest.PAD


def test_a_call_opens_as_it_is_zoomed_into_near_the_middle():
    assert nest.opening(1.0) == 0 and nest.opening(nest.OPEN) == 0     # not at the usual zoom
    assert 0 < nest.opening((nest.OPEN + nest.FULL) / 2) < 1
    assert nest.opening(nest.FULL) == 1
    assert 1 < nest.CLOSE < nest.PREFETCH < nest.OPEN < nest.FULL     # made before shown, kept a while
    view = (1000, 800)
    assert nest.middle((400, 350, 200, 40), view)
    assert not nest.middle((900, 350, 200, 40), view)               # at the edge: not looked at
    assert not nest.middle((400, 10, 200, 40), view)

```

`tests/test_notes.py`:

```py
"""Call-site notes on hand-built expression trees (shapes taken from real Hex-Rays output)."""
from fakes import FakeProgram, call, cast, fvar, num, obj, ref, string, var

from ibr.core import i18n
from ibr.core.notes import collect_notes

F = {0x1000: "printf", 0x1010: "_isoc99_scanf", 0x1020: "__syslog_chk", 0x1030: "lseek64",
     0x1040: "_exit", 0x1050: "open", 0x1060: "mprotect", 0x1070: "syscall", 0x1080: "calloc",
     0x1090: "__printf_chk", 0x10A0: "setvbuf", 0x4C0000: "gbuf"}


def note(e, **kw):
    prog = FakeProgram(names=F, **kw)
    by_ea, _ = collect_notes(prog, [e])
    return by_ea.get(e.ea)


def test_printf_binds_each_conversion():
    e = call(0x1000, string("%d %s %d\n"), var("v1"), var("v3"), var("v2"))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: printf "%d %s %d\\n" (%d = v1, %s = v3, %d = v2)'


def test_printf_positional():
    e = call(0x1000, string("%2$s %1$d\n"), var("a1"), var("a2"))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: printf "%2$s %1$d\\n" (%2$s = a2, %1$d = a1)'


def test_printf_star_precision_and_char():
    e = call(0x1000, string("%.*s|%5.2f|%c\n"), var("a1"), var("a2"), fvar("a3"), num(113))
    assert note(e) == "libc: printf \"%.*s|%5.2f|%c\\n\" (%.*s = a2 (precision a1), %5.2f = a3, %c = 'q')"


def test_float_arg_hidden_by_decompiler_takes_no_slot_on_sysv():
    # x86-64 passes doubles in xmm registers; an optimised call often shows only the ints
    e = call(0x1000, string("%d %f %s\n"), var("a1"), var("a2"))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: printf "%d %f %s\\n" (%d = a1, %s = a2)'
    assert note(e, pe=True) == 'libc: printf "%d %f %s\\n" (%d = a1, %f = a2)'


def test_scanf_suppressed_and_scanset():
    assert note(call(0x1010, string("%*d %d"), ref(var("v1")))) == 'libc: scanf "%*d %d" (%d -> &v1)'
    e = call(0x1010, string("%99[^\n] %d"), var("v3"), ref(var("v2")))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: scanf "%99[^\\n] %d" (%99[^\\n] -> v3, %d -> &v2)'


def test_fortified_variants_drop_their_extra_args():
    e = call(0x1020, num(6), num(1), string("user %s id %d"), var("a2"), var("a1"))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: syslog "user %s id %d" (%s = a2, %d = a1)'
    e = call(0x1090, num(2), string("%d\n"), var("v1"))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: printf "%d\\n" (%d = v1)'


def test_writable_global_format_is_a_bug():
    e = call(0x1000, obj(0x4C0000, "gbuf"))
    assert note(e, writable={0x4C0000}) == \
        "libc: printf: the format (gbuf) is not a literal, check for a format-string bug"
    # a read-only global we cannot decode is not evidence of a bug
    assert note(e) == "libc: printf gbuf"


def test_seek_reads_relative_offsets():
    fd = var("fd")
    assert note(call(0x1030, fd, num(0, 8), num(2))) == "libc: seek fd to the end"
    assert note(call(0x1030, fd, num(-16, 8), num(1))) == "libc: seek fd to the current position - 16"
    assert note(call(0x1030, fd, num(128, 8), num(0))) == "libc: seek fd to 128"


def test_exit_variants_are_not_merged():
    assert note(call(0x1040, var("n4"))) == "libc: exit now, status n4"


def test_constants_are_decoded_per_architecture():
    e = call(0x1050, string("flag"), num(0x241), num(0o644))
    assert note(e) == 'libc: open "flag" (O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC, mode 0644)'
    e = call(0x1050, string("flag"), num(0x301), num(0o644))
    assert note(e, arch="mipsb") == 'libc: open "flag" (O_WRONLY|O_CREAT|O_TRUNC, mode 0644)'
    e = call(0x1060, var("p"), num(0x1000), num(7))
    assert note(e) == "libc: protect 4096 bytes @ p: PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE|PROT_EXEC [RWX]"


def test_raw_syscall_named_from_its_number():
    e = call(0x1070, num(59), var("path"), var("argv"), num(0, 8))
    assert note(e) == "sys: execve(path, argv, 0) (#59)"


def test_calloc_parenthesises_compound_counts():
    e = call(0x1080, cast(var("n"), "(n + 1)"), num(16))
    e.args[0] = type(e.args[0])("other", "n + 1")
    assert note(e) == "libc: alloc (n + 1) x 16 bytes, zeroed"


def test_chinese_output():
    i18n.set_lang("zh")
    assert note(call(0x1030, var("fd"), num(0, 8), num(2))) == "libc: fd 定位到 末尾"
    assert note(call(0x10A0, var("stdout"), num(0), num(2), num(0))) == "libc: stdout 缓冲方式:_IONBF(无缓冲)"

```

`tests/test_package.py`:

```py
"""The release zip: its layout, the entry file as a CLI and a module, real paths in tracebacks."""
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import zipfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
PKG = os.path.join(ROOT, "src", "ibr")


def build(tmp_path):
    out = str(tmp_path / "ida_better_reverse.zip")
    subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(ROOT, "tools", "package.py"), out], check=True,
                   capture_output=True)
    return out


def unpack(tmp_path):
    user = tmp_path / "idausr"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(build(tmp_path)) as z:
        z.extractall(user)
    return str(user / "plugins" / "ida_better_reverse.py")


def test_layout(tmp_path):
    out = build(tmp_path)
    names = zipfile.ZipFile(out).namelist()
    src = set()
    for dirpath, dirs, files in os.walk(PKG):
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d != "__pycache__"]
        for fn in files:
            if not fn.endswith(".pyc"):
                rel = os.path.relpath(os.path.join(dirpath, fn), os.path.dirname(PKG))
                src.add("plugins/ida_better_reverse/" + rel.replace(os.sep, "/"))
    assert set(names) == src | {"plugins/ida_better_reverse.py", "plugins/ida_better_reverse/LICENSE"}
    assert not any("__pycache__" in n or n.endswith(".pyc") for n in names)
    assert all(b"\r\n" not in zipfile.ZipFile(out).read(n) for n in names if n.endswith(".py"))
    assert open(build(tmp_path), "rb").read() == open(out, "rb").read()       # reproducible


def test_cli_runs(tmp_path):
    entry = unpack(tmp_path)
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, entry, "--help"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "--repair" in r.stdout
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, entry, str(tmp_path / "missing")], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 1 and "cannot read" in r.stderr


def test_module_api(tmp_path):
    entry = unpack(tmp_path)
    code = ("import importlib.util\n"
            "s = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ida_better_reverse', %r)\n"
            "m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); s.loader.exec_module(m)\n"
            "import ibr\n"
            "from ibr.core import resources\n"
            "m.set_lang('zh'); print(m.LANG, m.__version__, ibr.__version__)\n"
            "print(callable(m.detect), m.canon('__isoc99_scanf'))\n"
            "print(len(resources.load('fingerprint.bin')))\n"
            "print(ibr.__file__)\n") % entry
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
    lang, v1, v2, is_callable, canon, size, path = r.stdout.split()
    assert lang == "zh" and v1 == v2 and is_callable == "True" and canon == "scanf"
    assert int(size) == os.path.getsize(os.path.join(PKG, "data", "fingerprint.bin"))
    assert path.startswith(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(entry), "ida_better_reverse", "ibr"))


def test_traceback_names_the_module(tmp_path):
    entry = unpack(tmp_path)
    code = ("import importlib.util, traceback\n"
            "s = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ida_better_reverse', %r)\n"
            "m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); s.loader.exec_module(m)\n"
            "try:\n    m.detect(path=%r)\n"
            "except OSError:\n    traceback.print_exc()\n") % (entry, str(tmp_path / "missing"))
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert os.path.join("plugins", "ida_better_reverse", "ibr", "core", "detect.py") in r.stderr
    assert 'with open(path, "rb") as f:' in r.stderr


def test_old_single_file_does_not_win(tmp_path):
    # 2.3 and earlier install an import hook for `ibr` (marked _ibr_bundle) when IDA loads
    # them; with an old copy still in another plugins/ folder, it must not serve our imports
    entry = unpack(tmp_path)
    code = ("import sys, importlib.abc, importlib.util\n"
            "class Old(importlib.abc.MetaPathFinder):\n"
            "    _ibr_bundle = True\n"
            "    base = 'OLD/ida_better_reverse.py'\n"
            "    def find_spec(self, name, path=None, target=None):\n"
            "        if name == 'ibr' or name.startswith('ibr.'):\n"
            "            raise ImportError('old bundle served ' + name)\n"
            "sys.meta_path.insert(0, Old())\n"
            "s = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('ida_better_reverse', %r)\n"
            "m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); s.loader.exec_module(m)\n"
            "import ibr; print(ibr.__file__)\n") % entry
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
    assert "OLD/ida_better_reverse.py" in r.stdout
    assert os.path.join("plugins", "ida_better_reverse", "ibr") in r.stdout.splitlines()[-1]


def test_missing_folder_is_explained(tmp_path):
    lone = tmp_path / "ida_better_reverse.py"
    lone.write_bytes(open(os.path.join(ROOT, "ida_better_reverse.py"), "rb").read())
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(lone), "--help"], capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode != 0 and "ida_better_reverse/ is missing" in r.stderr


def test_checkout_runs_from_src():
    r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, os.path.join(ROOT, "ida_better_reverse.py"), "--help"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True)
    assert r.returncode == 0 and "--repair" in r.stdout
```
